    properties (Access = private)
        AcqFile;
        Acqknowledge_Data;                       %Data loaded from Acqknowledge
        Acqknowledge_DataAll;                    %All data files for Acqknowledge
        AllInputsCell;                           %All inputs for uploading single excel
        AllPlotted_Data;                         %All plotted data (for trial select)
        BrainVision_Data;                        %Data loaded from BrainVision export
        BrainVision_DataAll;                     %All data files for BrainVision
        BrainSight_Data;                         %Data loaded from Brain Sight export
        ChangeCurrentRow                         %for when a row is changed in the conditions table (boolean)
        ComList;                                 %List of comments
        ConditionGroupNames;                     %Condition Group names from excel file
        Conditions_Data;                         %Data attached to Conditions table
        Conditions_DataAll;                      %All data from all conditions
        CurrChTitle;                             %Channel title of current data
        CurrentRow;                              %Current row in Conditions table that is being changed
        CustomUploadFile;                        %Custom upload file selected
        DeletedRows;                             %Conditions Table rows that were most recently deleted
        DisableLI_FindEvents;                    %used to disable loading indicator for find events button
        labChart_Data;                           %Data loaded from labChart export
        LoadedConditions;                        %Loaded Conditions file data
        LoadedConditionsAll;                     %All loaded Condition files data
        numStates;                               %Number of States (for Signal Data)
        numTrialsState;                          %Number of trials for each state
        Old;                                     %Old condition file uploaded (Boolean)
        Plotted_Data;                            %Current data that is plotted and will be analyzed
        Plugins;                                 %Plugins list
        SelectedCondition;                       %Selected condition in Conditions list box
        SelectedSheet;                           %selected excel sheet
        SelectedTableRows;                       %Rows that are selected in the Conditions table
        Signal_Data;                             %Data loaded from Signal export
        SignalFile;                              %Signal import file type (exported .mat, exported .cfs/converted .cfs)
        Spike_Data;                              %Data loaded from Spike export

        %analyze
        OverrideUsed;                            %Boolean, override was used = 1
        OverrideRange;                           %first and second click location for override
        PreStimStart=-110;                       %Start of pre-stim period (ms)
        PreStimEnd=-10;                          %End of pre-stim period (ms)
        PreStimMean;                             %Calculated mean of the rectified pre stim data
        PreStimRMS;                              %Calculated RMS for all analyzed trials during pre-stim period
        PreStimSTDV;                             %Calculated STDV for all analyzed trials during pre-stim period
        Analyze_TrialsUsed;                      %Which trials were used in the most recent Analyze
        Analyze_ConditionsUsed;                  %Which conditions were used in the most recent Analyze
        Analyze_OverrideUsed;                    %Boolean, if override was used in the most recent Analyze
        Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName;         %Condition name for the trials used in most recetn Analyze
        Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel;               %Data channels that were analyzed in the most recent Analyze
        MEP_Amp;                                 %Calculated MEP amplitude for all analyzed trials
        MEP_Area;                                %Calculated MEP area for all analyzed trials
        AllOnOffsetTime;                         %All onset and offset times for all analyzed trials
        SP_PercDecrease;                         %Calculated SP percent decrease for all analyzed trials
        SP_Area;                                 %Calculated SP area for all analyzed trials
        PrevToolBarSelect;                       %Previous item selected in axes toolbar
        CurrToolBarSelect;                       %Current item selected in axes toolbar
        POriginalAllPlottedData;                 %Original data before processing
        POriginalAvgPlottedData;                 %Original average data before processing
        POriginalTime;                           %Original time before processing
        Processed;                               %If data has been processed
        AvgConditionData;                        %Average of each condition data
        Toolbar;                                 %Axes toolbar
        PrevSelectedTab;                         %Previously selected tab

        %Plotting lines
        OnsetLine;                               %Xline plots for all onsets found
        OffsetLine;                              %Xline plots for all offsets found
        PreStimArea;                             %Area plot for pre-stim area
        StartLine;                               %Xline plot for start time
        meanPreStimLine;                         %Yline plot for mean of pre stim
        PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig;                %Figure for the "plot onset/offset" switch
        
        
    end

    properties (Access = public)
        AllSampleRate;                           %Sample rate of data
        autoImportEvents;                        %For when Import Events button is pressed for filling in the Condtions table (boolean)
        Block;                                   %Data epoched based on blocks
        Ch;                                      %Channel

        Convert2PlottedUnits;                    %Conversion for volts to selected unit, since updateSpinners and FindEvents pulls data in volts

        DataType;                                %Which data file type is imported
        DataExtracted;                           %Data has been epoched (boolean)

        DownSampledRate;                         %sample rate if the data is downsampled

        EventThresh;                             %Threshold for events

        PostDur;                                 %Time after event to include
        PreDur;                                  %Time before event to include

        %figures

        ToolboxDocFolderName='TMSToolBoxApp';    %Name of the folder created in Documents/MATLAB
    end

    methods (Access = public)
        function refreshApp(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Resets figure and data variables to default
            %==============================================================

            %Analysis Variables--------------------------------------------
            %Pre stim
            app.PreStimMeanEditField.Value=0; app.PreStimRMS=0;
            app.PreStimStdEditField.Value=0; app.PreStimSTDV=0;

            %Onset/Offset Detection
            app.AllOnOffsetTime=[];


            %Override
            app.OverrideUsed=0;
            app.OverrideRange=[];
            app.UIFigure.Pointer = 'arrow';
            app.OverrideLamp.Color=[0.8 0.8 0.8];

            %Analyze
            app.MEP_Amp=[];
            app.MEP_Area=[];
            app.SP_PercDecrease=[];
            app.SP_Area=[];
            app.Analyze_TrialsUsed=[]; 
            app.Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName=cell(1,1); 
            app.Analyze_ConditionsUsed=[];
            app.Analyze_OverrideUsed=0;
            app.Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel=cell(1,1);
            app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Visible=0;
            app.PrevSelectedTab='';

            %Save Analyze Results
            app.SavecsvButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];
            app.SavecsvEditField.Value="";

            %Plotting
            if ishghandle(app.OnsetLine) == 1
                delete(app.OnsetLine);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.OffsetLine) == 1
                delete(app.OffsetLine);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.PreStimArea) == 1
                delete(app.PreStimArea);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.StartLine) == 1
                delete(app.StartLine);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.OnsetLimitLines) == 1
                delete(app.OnsetLimitLines);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.OffsetLimitLines) == 1
                delete(app.OffsetLimitLines);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.meanPreStimLine) == 1
                delete(app.meanPreStimLine);
            end
            app.PrevToolBarSelect="";

            %Average
            app.AverageCheckBox.Value=0;
            app.AverageCheckBox.Enable=1;
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Enable=1;
            app.SelectAllCondButton.Enable=1;

            %Process
            app.Processed=0;
            app.ResetDataButton.Enable=0;
            app.POriginalAllPlottedData=cell(1,1);
            app.POriginalAvgPlottedData=[];
            app.DownSampledRate=[];
            app.ProcessOrder=[];
            app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll=cell(1,4);

            app.ProcessButton.Enable=0;
            app.AnalyzeButton.Enable=0; 
            app.OverrideButton.Enable=0; 

            app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.Enable=0;
            app.SavematEditField.Enable=0;

            %Reset figure look---------------------------------------------
            app.UIFigure.AutoResizeChildren = 'off';
            %Enable all Epoch Types
            app.BlocksButton.Enable=1;
            app.CommentsButton.Enable=1;
            app.EventsButton.Enable=1;
            app.UnitsDropDown.Value='mV';
            app.FindEventsButton.Visible=1;
            app.DataType=[];

            %Set current evet type to Block
            app.BlocksButton.Value=1;
            app.EpochTypesButtonGroup.Visible=1;
            app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Visible=1;

            %Enable Spinners
            app.ChannelSpinner.Enable=0;
            app.TrialSpinner.Enable=0;
            app.ChannelSpinner.Visible=1; app.ChannelSpinnerLabel.Visible=1;
            app.TrialSpinner.Visible=1; app.TrialSpinnerLabel.Visible=1;

            %Hide epoch type specific panels
            app.SetDataWindowPanel.Visible=0;
            app.EventNameListBox.Visible=0; app.EventNameListBoxLabel.Visible=0; app.EventNameListBox.Items=""; app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData=[];
            app.TrialSelectListBox.Visible=1; app.TrialSelectListBoxLabel.Visible=1; app.TrialSelectListBox.Items="";
            app.SelectAllButton.Visible=1;
            app.EventParametersPanel.Visible=0;
            app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Visible=1;

            %Enable Data Organization tab items
            app.EpochTypesButtonGroup.Enable=1;
            app.TrialSpinnerLabel.Enable=1;
            app.ChannelSpinnerLabel.Enable=1;
            app.FindEventsButton.Enable=1;
            app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Enable=1;
            app.TrialSelectListBox.Enable=1;
            app.TrialSelectListBoxLabel.Enable=1;

            %Clear graph
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            title(app.UIAxes,'Title');
            xlabel(app.UIAxes,'X');
            ylabel(app.UIAxes,'Y');
            app.PlottedUnits='mV';
            app.UIAxes.XLimMode = 'auto';
            app.UIAxes.YLimMode = 'auto';
            app.MinEditField.Value=0;
            app.MaxEditField.Value=0;
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=0;
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=0;
            app.SampleRateLabel.Text='Sample Rate: ';

            %Clear Filename and Event list box
            app.DataFileEditField.Value="";
            app.ComList="";
            app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBox.Items="";

            %Clear Conditions
            app.UITable.Data=[];
            app.SelectedTableRows=[];
            s = uistyle('BackgroundColor','white');
            addStyle(app.UITable,s);
            app.autoImportEvents=0;
            app.ImportEventsNameL=1;
            app.Conditions_Data=cell(1,3);
            app.LoadedConditionsAll=struct();
            app.ConditionsListBox.Visible=0; app.ConditionsListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
            app.ConditionsListBox.Items=""; app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData=[];
            app.ConditionsListBox.Value='';
            app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;
            app.UITable.ColumnEditable=true;
            app.SelectAllButton.Enable=1;
            app.AutoFillEditField.Value=2;
            app.DeletedRows=struct();
            app.RecoverButton.Enable=0;
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Visible=0; app.ConditionTrialsLabel.Visible=0;
            app.SelectAllCondButton.Visible=0;
            app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=0; app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
            app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=0; app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;
            app.numTrialsCondition=zeros(1,3);
            app.Conditions_DataAll=cell(1,2);

            %Enable Conditions Table buttons
            app.AddSelectedTrialsButton.Enable=1;
            app.AddBlankRowButton.Enable=1;
            app.ImportEventsButton.Enable=1;
            app.AutoFillButton.Enable=1;
            app.AutoFillEditField.Enable=1;
            app.KeeporDeleteRowsButton.Enable=1;
            app.SaveButton.Enable=1;
            app.ClearButton.Enable=1;
            app.ApplyButton.Enable=1;
            app.LoadConditionsButton.Enable=1;
            app.UploadExcelFileButton.Enable=1;
            app.Old=0;
            app.SheetNameEditField.Value='';
            app.ConditionGroupNames=cell(1,1);
            app.SheetNameEditField.Enable=1;
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;

            %Reset key variables-------------------------------------------
            %Data
            app.labChart_Data=[];
            app.BrainSight_Data=[];
            app.Signal_Data=[];
            app.Acqknowledge_Data=[];
            app.BrainVision_Data=[];
            app.Spike_Data=[];
            app.Custom_Data=[];
            app.Custom_DataAll=struct();
            app.DataExtracted=0;
            app.Block=struct();
            app.Comments=struct();
            app.Events=[];
            app.Convert2PlottedUnits=1000;               %default plotting unit mV, data saved in volts
            app.EpochType=1;
            app.numTrialsC=[];
            app.CustomUploadFile='';
            app.numBlocks=0;
            app.Plotted_Data=[];
            app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,1);

            %Pre/post duration and Trigger Time
            app.PostDurationsEditField.Value=0.15;  app.PostDur=0.15;
            app.PreDurationsEditField.Value=0.15;   app.PreDur=0.15;
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value=0;      app.PreTriggerTime=0;
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Enable=1;
            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=0;     app.PostTriggerTime=0;

            %Event Parameters
            app.EventChannelEditField.Value=1;      app.EventChannel=1;
            app.ThresholdmVEditField.Value=0;       app.EventThresh=0;
            app.SearchISIsEditField.Value=0;        app.SearchISI=0;
            app.DetectionSetting=1;

            %Trial and channel selected
            app.ChannelSpinner.Value=1;             app.Ch=1;
            app.TrialSpinner.Value=1;               app.Trial=1;

            %File upload values
            app.StateV=1;
            app.SignalFile=2;
            app.AcqFile=1;
            %unload CEDS64ML library if loaded
            if libisloaded('ceds64int')
                unloadlibrary ceds64int;
            end


            %Plugins-------------------------------------------------------
            %look for plugin folder
            %Find plugins for data import "Custom" option =================
            if ~isfolder(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Custom_File_Import'))
                mkdir(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Custom_File_Import'));
            end           
            PluginsAnalysisFileFolder=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Custom_File_Import');
            Files=dir(PluginsAnalysisFileFolder);
            e=1; Filenames=strings(1,1);
            for i=3:length(Files)
                if  Files(i).name(end-1:end) == '.m'
                    Filenames(e)=string(Files(i).name);
                    e=e+1;
                end
            end

            %Remove CustomFileTemplate.m from list 
            Filenames(Filenames == 'CustomFileTemplate.m') =[]; %!!!

            %Look for plugin files
            delete(app.CustomMenu.Children)
            if ~(Filenames == "") %if there are files in the filenames
                %Create menus for each custom file
                for i=1:length(Filenames)
                    MenuName=char(Filenames(i));
                    MenuName=MenuName(1:end-2);  %remove '.m'.
                    app.Plugins.CustomFile.(MenuName)=uimenu(app.CustomMenu);
                    app.Plugins.CustomFile.(MenuName).MenuSelectedFcn = createCallbackFcn(app, @CustomFileMenuSelected, true);
                    app.Plugins.CustomFile.(MenuName).Text=MenuName;
                end
            end

            %Find Data processing plugins =================================
            if ~isfolder(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Processing'))
                mkdir(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Processing'));
            end           
            PluginsAnalysisFileFolder=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Processing');
            Files=dir(PluginsAnalysisFileFolder);
            e=1; Filenames=strings(1,1);
            for i=3:length(Files)
                if  Files(i).name(end-1:end) == '.m'
                    Filenames(e)=string(Files(i).name);
                    e=e+1;
                end
            end
            Filenames(Filenames == 'ProcessingPluginTemplate.m') =[]; %Ignore template

            %Look for plugin files
            delete(app.CustomProcessTree.Children);
            if ~(Filenames == "") %if there are files in the filenames
                %Create menus for each custom file         
                for i=1:length(Filenames)
                    MenuName=char(Filenames(i));
                    MenuName=MenuName(1:end-2);  %remove '.m'.

                    %update Custom Processes Tree
                    uitreenode(app.CustomProcessTree,'Text',MenuName,'NodeData',i);
                end

                %Add plugins to Process Tab
                app.CustomProcessTree.Visible=1;
                app.CustomProcessesLabel.Visible=1;
            else
                app.CustomProcessTree.Visible=0;
                app.CustomProcessesLabel.Visible=0;
            end

            
            %Find Analysis plugins ========================================
            if ~isfolder(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis'))
                mkdir(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis'));
            end           
            PluginsAnalysisFileFolder=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
            Files=dir(PluginsAnalysisFileFolder);
            e=1; Filenames=strings(1,1);
            for i=3:length(Files)
                if  Files(i).name(end-1:end) == '.m'
                    Filenames(e)=string(Files(i).name);
                    e=e+1;
                end
            end
            Filenames(Filenames == 'AnalysisPluginTemplate.m') =[]; %Ignore template

            %Look for plugin files
            if ~(Filenames == "") %if there are files in the filenames
                %Create menus for each custom file
                app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items={'SD Bounds', 'Garvey et al'};
                for i=1:length(Filenames)
                    MenuName=char(Filenames(i));
                    MenuName=MenuName(1:end-2);  %remove '.m'.

                    %update onset/offset detection dropdown 
                    app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items(end+1)={MenuName};
                end
            end

            %add plugins folder to path
            addpath(genpath(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins')));

            %End of Plugins Section----------------------------------------

        end %end resfreshApp

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractLabChart(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from LabChart export files (.mat)
            %Extracts block data, then epochs based on comments or events
            %Comment text options are listed in the EventNameListBox
            %==============================================================

            %Extract Block data
            if app.DataExtracted == 0 %Block data is used for comments and events, only extract once
                [app.numChannels,app.numBlocks,app.Block,app.AllSampleRate]=ExtractLB_BlockData(app.labChart_Data);
                app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                app.DataExtracted=1;
            end

            %Epoch type: Blocks, Comments, Events
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                case 2 %Comments
                    numComments=length(app.labChart_Data.com(:,1));
                    [app.Comments,app.numTrialsC] = ExtractLB_CommentsData(app.labChart_Data,app.Block,app.numChannels,numComments,app.PreDur,app.PostDur,app.AllSampleRate);
                    app.SelectedComtxt = app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                    %update trial spinner limits and trial number based on specified comment text
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);           %redefine numTrials
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                    app.Trial = app.TrialSpinner.Value;             %update trial number, if current trial number selected is above the number of trials in the newly
                    %selected comment text the spinner will set to the max trial number, this will set the trial number to this value

                case 3 %Events
                    [app.numTrials, app.Events] = GetEvents(app);
                    if app.numTrials == 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    end

                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);

            end %end epochType switch

            %Plotting
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1 %Block
                    Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                           %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate
                    if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                        Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
                    end
                    if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                            %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                        FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                        %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                        if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                            FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                        end
                        Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                        Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
                    end
                case 2 %Comments
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);                                                       %Determine current specified comment text
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;                                         %Use text to define the column the data is in
                    Plot_Data=app.Comments.Data{app.Trial,comCol}(app.Ch,:);                              %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);                                                 %Determine number of trials based on comment text
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;                                     %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
                case 3 %Events
                    Plot_Data=app.Events.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;
            end

        end %end ExtractLabChart

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractBrainSight(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from BrainSight export files (.txt)
            %Extracts block data only
            %Only 2 channels of data, assumed to be named EMG Data 1 and EMG Data 2
            %The number of "samples" is the number of blocks
            %XYZ coordinates are added on to the Block variable
            %==============================================================

            HeaderRow=find(app.BrainSight_Data(:,1) == "# Sample Name");                              %find row with headers
            Samples=cell2mat(strfind(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:end,1),'Sample'));               %determine rows with sample data
            numSamples=sum(Samples);                                                                  %number of samples, each sample is a block
            Data1_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "EMG Data 1");                         %find EMG data 1
            Data2_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "EMG Data 2");                         %find EMG data 2
            DataCols=[Data1_Col Data2_Col];                                                           %columns with EMG data

            %Determine time and EMG units
            UnitsLoc=find(contains(app.BrainSight_Data(1:HeaderRow,1),'Units')==1);
            Units=app.BrainSight_Data(UnitsLoc,1);
            findSec=strfind(Units,'seconds');
            findVolts=strfind(Units,'volts');
            Units=char(Units);
            TimeUnit=Units(findSec-5:findSec-1);
            EMGUnit=Units(findVolts-5:findVolts-1);
            %Determine if time unit needs to be converted to seconds
            switch TimeUnit
                case 'milli'  %milliseconds
                    ConvertSec=0.001;
                otherwise  %units are in seconds
                    ConvertSec=1;
            end
            %Determine if EMG unit needs to be converted to volts
            switch EMGUnit
                case 'milli'  %millivolts
                    ConvertVolts=0.001;
                case 'micro' %microvolts
                    ConvertVolts=1e-6;
                otherwise  %units are in volts
                    ConvertVolts=1;
            end

            %XYZ coordinates
            LocX_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "Loc. X");
            LocY_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "Loc. Y");
            LocZ_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "Loc. Z");

            LocX=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,LocX_Col);
            LocY=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,LocY_Col);
            LocZ=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,LocZ_Col);
            XYZCoordinates=double([LocX LocY LocZ]);

            %number of emg channels used
            EMGChannels_Col=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "EMG Channels");
            numEMGChannelsAll=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,EMGChannels_Col);     %number of EMG channels used for each sample
            %check if the same number of channels is used for all samples
            comparenumEMGCh=numEMGChannelsAll == numEMGChannelsAll(1);
            numEMGCh_Same=find(comparenumEMGCh == 0, 1);
            numEMGChannelsAll=double(numEMGChannelsAll);                   %number of EMG channels for each sample

            %Extract EMG Data
            if isempty(numEMGCh_Same) == 1 %all blocks have the same number of channels
                numEMGChannels=numEMGChannelsAll(1);
                EMGData=cell(numEMGChannels,1);
                for i=1:numEMGChannels  %for each EMG channel extract data
                    DataCol=DataCols(i);
                    EMG=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,DataCol);              %extract semicolon delimited data
                    EMG=split(EMG,';');                                                             %parse out data
                    EMG=double(EMG);                                                                %convert from string to numeric matrix
                    for e=1:numSamples
                        EMGData{i,e}=EMG(e,:)*ConvertVolts; %convert to volts
                    end
                end
            else
                %there are some blocks with different number of emg channels
                EMGData=cell(max(numEMGChannelsAll) ,1);
                for i=1:max(numEMGChannelsAll)  %for the max number of EMG channels
                    DataCol=DataCols(i);
                    EMGA=app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,DataCol);             %extract semicolon delimited data
                    [MissingDataLoc,~]=find(EMGA=="");
                    EMGI=[(1:length(num2str(numEMGChannelsAll)))' EMGA];
                    EMGI(MissingDataLoc,:)=[];
                    EMG=split(EMGI(:,2),';');                                                       %parse out data
                    EMG=[double(EMGI(:,1)) double(EMG)];                                            %convert from string to numeric matrix
                    EMG2=zeros(size(EMG));
                    %refill indices with missing data
                    for e=1:length(EMG(:,1))+length(MissingDataLoc)
                        [ILoc,~]=find(EMG(:,1)==e);
                        if isempty(ILoc) %if there is no index (cleared due to no data)
                            EMG2(e,:)=[e nan(1,length(EMG(1,:))-1)]; %add index with Nan data
                        else %fill with existing data
                            EMG2(e,:)=EMG(ILoc,:);
                        end
                    end

                    for e2=1:numSamples
                        EMGData{i,e2}=EMG2(e2,2:end)*ConvertVolts; %convert to volts
                    end
                end
            end


            %Time
            StartTime=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "EMG Start");
            app.PreTriggerTime=abs(double(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1,StartTime))); %Assumed the same for all samples
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value=app.PreTriggerTime;                         %update edit field
            EndTime=find(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow,:) == "EMG End");
            FrameTime=[double(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,StartTime)) double(app.BrainSight_Data(HeaderRow+1:HeaderRow+numSamples,EndTime))];
            DurationAll=FrameTime(:,2)-FrameTime(:,1);
            compareDuration=DurationAll == DurationAll(1);
            Duration_Same=find(compareDuration == 0,1);
            if isempty(Duration_Same) ==1
                %same duration for all samples
                Duration=DurationAll(1);
            else
                Duration=DurationAll;
            end

            %Calculate sample rate
            numFrames=length(EMGData{1,1}(1,:));
            app.AllSampleRate=round((numFrames-1)/(Duration*ConvertSec));
            app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];

            %Define app variables
            app.Block.Data=EMGData; %numChannels x
            app.Block.Coordinates=XYZCoordinates;
            app.Block.Titles=["EMG Data 1";"EMG Data 2"];
            app.numBlocks=numSamples;
            app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
            app.numChannels=max(numEMGChannelsAll);


            %Plotting
            Plot_Data=EMGData{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                                   %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
            ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                      %Channel title of current selected channel
            Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate);  %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
            if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                 %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
            end
            if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                             %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                    FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                    app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                end
                Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
            end
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Enable=0;

        end %end ExtractBrainSight

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractSignal(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from Signal export files (.mat and .cfs)
            %Signal data has states each with multiple blocks
            %Extracts block data for each State
            %States are listed in the EventNameListBox
            %File Type is determined in SignalmatcfsMenuSelected callback
            %==============================================================

            switch app.SignalFile
                case 1 %exported .mat
                    app.AllSampleRate=round(1/app.Signal_Data.interval); %samplerate
                    app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];

                    %channel info
                    app.numChannels=double(app.Signal_Data.chans);
                    ChTitles={app.Signal_Data.chaninfo.title}';
                    DataUnits={app.Signal_Data.chaninfo.units}';

                    StateA=[app.Signal_Data.frameinfo.state]';
                    app.numStates=StateA(end); %assumes largest state value to be at the end
                    app.numBlocks=app.Signal_Data.frames;
                    labelsAll={app.Signal_Data.frameinfo.label}';

                    %Convert Data units to volts
                    switch DataUnits{1} %convert to V
                        case 'V'
                            ConvertV=1;
                        case 'mV'
                            ConvertV=0.001;
                    end

                    %dividing into blocks
                    Block1=cell(app.numChannels,1); StateB=cell(app.numStates,1); e=1;
                    for i=1:app.numBlocks %separate Blocks into their "States"
                        DataValues=app.Signal_Data.values(:,:,i); %numPointsxnumChannels
                        for i2=1:app.numChannels
                            Block1{i2,e}=DataValues(:,i2)'*ConvertV;
                        end
                        label=labelsAll{i};
                        State=StateA(i);

                        StateB{State,1}=Block1;
                        StateB{State,2}=label;

                        %reset for when state changes, assumes states occur in chronological order
                        if (i~=app.numBlocks) && (State ~= StateA(i+1))
                            e=1;
                            Block1=cell(app.numChannels,1);
                        else
                            e=e+1;
                        end


                    end

                    app.PreTriggerTime=abs(round(app.Signal_Data.start));
                    PreTriggerTime_Units=app.Signal_Data.xunits; %check PreTriggerTime unit

                case 2 %convert .cfs or converted .cfs

                    %extract from loaded data
                    app.AllSampleRate=round(1/app.Signal_Data.D.param.xScale(1));
                    app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                    app.PreTriggerTime=abs(round(app.Signal_Data.D.param.xOffset(1)));
                    PreTriggerTime_Units=app.Signal_Data.D.param.xUnits{1};        %check PreTriggerTime units

                    app.Signal_Data.values=app.Signal_Data.D.data;
                    app.numChannels=size(app.Signal_Data.values,2);
                    IsWave=[app.Signal_Data.Chans.IsWaveData];
                    ChTitles1={app.Signal_Data.Chans.Name}';
                    ChTitles=ChTitles1(IsWave);
                    DataUnits1={app.Signal_Data.Chans.yUnits}';
                    DataUnits=DataUnits1(IsWave);

                    app.numBlocks=size(app.Signal_Data.values,3);
                    StateInfoLoc=find(string({app.Signal_Data.DSVars.Desc})=="State");
                    StateA=app.Signal_Data.DSVars(StateInfoLoc).Values';
                    if StateA{1} == 0 %zero index
                        app.numStates=StateA{end}+1;                                   %assumes largest state value to be at the end
                    elseif StateA{1} == 1 %one index
                        app.numStates=StateA{end};
                    end
                    LabelsLoc=find(string({app.Signal_Data.DSVars.Desc})=="StateL");
                    labelsAll=app.Signal_Data.DSVars(LabelsLoc).Values;

                    %Convert Data units to volts
                    switch DataUnits{1} %convert to V
                        case 'V'
                            ConvertV=1;
                        case 'mV'
                            ConvertV=0.001;
                    end

                    %dividing into blocks
                    Block1=cell(app.numChannels,1); StateB=cell(app.numStates,1); e=1;
                    for i=1:app.numBlocks
                        DataValues=app.Signal_Data.values(:,:,i); %numPointsxnumChannels
                        for i2=1:app.numChannels
                            Block1{i2,e}=DataValues(:,i2)'*ConvertV;
                        end
                        label=labelsAll{i};

                        %check for zero vs one indexed 
                        if StateA{1}==0
                            State=StateA{i}+1;
                            if (i~=1) && (State ~= StateA{i-1}+1)  %reset for when state changes, assumes states occur in chronological order
                                e=1;
                                Block1=cell(app.numChannels,1);
                            end
                        else
                            State=StateA{i};
                            if (i~=1) && (State ~= StateA{i-1})    %reset for when state changes, assumes states occur in chronological order
                                e=1;
                                Block1=cell(app.numChannels,1);
                            end
                        end

                        StateB{State,1}=Block1; %organize by state, StateB contains cells with block data for each state, convert to volts
                        StateB{State,2}=label;
                        e=e+1;
                    end

            end %end SignalFile (import type)

            %Convert PreTriggerTime units to seconds
            switch PreTriggerTime_Units %convert to s
                case 's'
                    ConvertS=1;
                case 'ms'
                    ConvertS=1000;
            end
            app.PreTriggerTime=app.PreTriggerTime/ConvertS;                %convert PreTriggerTime to units s
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value=app.PreTriggerTime;            %update edit field
            app.PreTriggermsEditField.Enable=0;

            app.Block.Data=StateB; %block data
            app.Block.Titles=string(ChTitles);

            %Remove any empty rows
            EmptyRows=all(cellfun(@isempty,app.Block.Data),2);
            if any(EmptyRows) %there are empty rows
                app.Block.Data(EmptyRows,:)=[];
                app.numStates=app.numStates-sum(EmptyRows);
            end

            %fill Event Name List box
            app.EventNameListBox.Items=string(app.Block.Data(:,2));
            app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData=1:length(app.EventNameListBox.Items);
            app.numTrialsState=cellfun(@(x) size(x,2) ,app.Block.Data(:,1));
            app.numTrials=app.numTrialsState(app.StateV);

            %check spinner values
            updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);

            %output
            Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.StateV,1}{app.Ch, app.Trial};
            Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
            if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                 %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
            end
            if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                             %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                    FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                    app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                end
                Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
            end
            ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
        end %end ExtractSignal

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle]= ExtractAcqknowledge(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from Acqknowledge export files (.mat and .acq)
            %Extracts block data, then epochs based on events
            %File Type is determined in AcqknowledgematacqMenuSelected callback
            %Multiple file upload is possible
            %Continuous blocks, each file is a block
            %==============================================================

            switch app.AcqFile
                case 1 %Exported .mat
                    %Extract Block data
                    if app.DataExtracted == 0 %Block data is used for events, only extract once
                        for e=1:length(app.Filename)
                            app.Acqknowledge_Data=app.Acqknowledge_DataAll.(['File' num2str(e)]); %load each file's data

                            if e==1 %assumed same number of channels for each file
                                app.numBlocks=0; %just continuous data so only one block, new block for each file
                                app.numChannels=length(app.Acqknowledge_Data.data(1,:));

                                %Sample Rate
                                ISI=app.Acqknowledge_Data.isi;
                                ISI_Units=app.Acqknowledge_Data.isi_units;
                                %check units, covnert to seconds
                                if ISI_Units == 'ms'
                                    ISI=ISI*0.001;
                                end
                                app.AllSampleRate=1/ISI;
                                app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                            end
                            FilenumChannels=length(app.Acqknowledge_Data.data(1,:));

                            Data=app.Acqknowledge_Data.data;

                            Units=cell(FilenumChannels,1);
                            FileTitles=string(app.Acqknowledge_Data.labels); %assumed to be channel titles
                            for i=1:FilenumChannels
                                Units{i,1}=app.Acqknowledge_Data.units;
                                switch Units{i,1}
                                    case 'mV' %convert to volts
                                        ConvertV=0.001;
                                    case 'V'
                                        ConvertV=1;
                                    otherwise
                                        ConvertV=1;
                                end

                                app.Block.Titles(i,e)= FileTitles(i);
                                app.Block.Data{i,e}=Data(:,i)'*ConvertV;                                        %Blocks
                            end

                            app.numBlocks=app.numBlocks+1; %add new block for each file, since each file is continuous each file is one block
                        end %end file data extract loop


                        %if there is more than one file
                        CheckMultiFileChan(app);

                        app.DataExtracted=1;
                    end %End if DataExtracted

                case 2 %.acq/converted .mat
                    if app.DataExtracted == 0 %Block data is used for  events, only extract once
                        %for each file, extract data
                        for e=1:length(app.Filename)
                            app.Acqknowledge_Data=app.Acqknowledge_DataAll.(['File' num2str(e)]); %load each file's data
                            if e==1 %assumed same number of channels for each file
                                app.numBlocks=0; %just continuous data so only one block, new block for each file
                                app.numChannels=app.Acqknowledge_Data.hdr.graph.num_channels;
                                app.AllSampleRate=1/(app.Acqknowledge_Data.hdr.graph.sample_time/1000); %1/convert sample time to seconds
                                app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                            end
                            FilenumChannels=app.Acqknowledge_Data.hdr.graph.num_channels;

                            Data=app.Acqknowledge_Data.data;
                            Units=cell(FilenumChannels,1);
                            for i=1:FilenumChannels
                                Units{i,1}=app.Acqknowledge_Data.hdr.per_chan_data(i).units_text;
                                switch Units{i,1}
                                    case 'mV' %convert to volts
                                        ConvertV=0.001;
                                    case 'V'
                                        ConvertV=1;
                                    otherwise
                                        ConvertV=1;
                                end

                                app.Block.Titles(i,e)= string(app.Acqknowledge_Data.hdr.per_chan_data(i).comment_text); %assumed to be channel titles
                                app.Block.Data{i,e}=Data(:,i)'*ConvertV;                                        %Blocks
                            end
                            app.numBlocks=app.numBlocks+1; %add new block for each file, since each file is continuous each file is one block

                        end %end file data extract loop


                        %if there is more than one file
                        CheckMultiFileChan(app);

                        app.DataExtracted=1;
                    end %End if DataExtracted


            end % end AcqFile switch


            %Epoch Type
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                case 3
                    [app.numTrials, app.Events] = GetEvents(app);
                    if app.numTrials == 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    end

                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
            end

            %Plotting
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1 %Blocks
                    Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                           %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
                    if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                        Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
                    end
                    if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                            %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                        FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                        %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                        if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                            FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                        end
                        Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                        Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
                    end
                case 3 %Events
                    Plot_Data=app.Events.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;

            end
        end %end ExtractAcqknowledge

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractBrainVision(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from BrainVision export files (.eeg)
            %Extracts block data, then epochs based on comments or events
            %Comment text options are listed in the EventNameListBox
            %Multiple file upload is possible
            %Continuous blocks, each file is a block
            %==============================================================

            if app.DataExtracted == 0
                for e=1:length(app.Filename)
                    app.BrainVision_Data=app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['File' num2str(e)]);
                    %Extract Blocks once
                    if e==1
                        app.AllSampleRate=app.BrainVision_Data.FileHeader.Fs;   %assumed same sample reate for all channels and blocks
                        app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                        FnumBlocks=1;
                        app.numBlocks=0;
                    end
                    FileNumChannels=app.BrainVision_Data.FileHeader.nChans; %number of channels

                    %check units
                    Units=app.BrainVision_Data.FileHeader.chanunit(1); %assumed same units for all channels
                    switch Units{1}
                        case 'uV'
                            ConvertV=1e6;
                        case 'mV'
                            ConvertV=1000;
                        case 'V'
                            ConvertV=1;
                    end

                    %Blocks
                    DataCell=cell(FileNumChannels,FnumBlocks);
                    for i=1:FileNumChannels
                        DataCell{i,FnumBlocks}=app.BrainVision_Data.Data(i,:)/ConvertV;%convert units to volts
                    end
                    app.Block.Data(1:length(DataCell(:,1)),e)=DataCell;
                    app.Block.Titles(1:length(DataCell(:,1)),e)=string(app.BrainVision_Data.FileHeader.label);
                    app.numBlocks=app.numBlocks+1; %Continuous data, each file is a block
                end %end for each file

                %if there are multiple files
                CheckMultiFileChan(app)

                app.DataExtracted=1;
            end %end DataExtracted

            %EpochType
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                case 2 %comments

                    %Create list of frames with comments
                    CommentTxtOptions=app.Comments.CommentTxtOptions;
                    CommentsList=cell(1,length(CommentTxtOptions));
                    for L=1:length(app.Filename) %for each file
                        app.BrainVision_Data.Comments=app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['Comments' num2str(L)]);
                        w=1;
                        for e=1:length(CommentTxtOptions) %for each comment text option
                            for i=1:length(app.BrainVision_Data.Comments) %search through all comments
                                CurrComText=app.BrainVision_Data.Comments(i).CommentText;
                                if CurrComText == CommentTxtOptions(e)
                                    CommentsList{L}(w,e)=app.BrainVision_Data.Comments(i).CommentLoc; %each cell is a block
                                    w=w+1;
                                end
                            end
                            w=1;

                        end
                    end
                    PreDurValue=app.PreDur*app.AllSampleRate;
                    PostDurValue=app.PostDur*app.AllSampleRate;

                    %create comments
                    app.numTrialsC=zeros(1,length(CommentTxtOptions)); CData=zeros(app.numChannels,length(-PreDurValue:PostDurValue));
                    for e=1:length(CommentTxtOptions) %for each comment text option
                        i2=1;
                        for L=1:length(app.Filename)
                            Frames=CommentsList{L}(:,e);
                            for i=Frames'
                                StartDiff=0; EndDiff=0;
                                Start=i-PreDurValue; End=i+PostDurValue;
                                if Start <= 0 %if start is less than zero, fill in with NaNs
                                    StartDiff=-Start+1;
                                    Start=1;
                                end
                                DataLength=length(app.Block.Data{L});
                                if End > DataLength %if end is greater than the length of the data, fill with NaNs
                                    EndDiff=End-DataLength;
                                    End=DataLength;
                                end

                                %reformat and cut data
                                for w=1:app.numChannels
                                    if isempty(app.Block.Data{w,L})
                                        app.Block.Data{w,L}= nan(1,DataLength);
                                    end
                                    CData(w,:)=[nan(1,StartDiff) app.Block.Data{w,L}(Start:End) nan(1,EndDiff)]; %fill beginning and end with NaNs if needed
                                end
                                app.Comments.Data{i2,e}=CData; %each column is for a comment text, order of comment texts is the same
                                i2=i2+1;
                            end
                            numEmpty=cellfun(@isempty,app.Comments.Data);          %Find number of empty vectors to determine
                            app.numTrialsC(e)=length(find(numEmpty(:,e) == 0));    %number of comments for each comment text
                        end
                    end

                    %Update spinner limits
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);
                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);

                case 3 %Events
                    [app.numTrials, app.Events] = GetEvents(app);
                    if app.numTrials == 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    end

                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
            end

            %Plotting
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1 %Block
                    Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                           %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate
                    if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                        Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
                    end
                    if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                            %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                        FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                        %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                        if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                            FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                        end
                        Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                        Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
                    end
                case 2 %Comments
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);                                                       %Determine current specified comment text
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;                                         %Use text to define the column the data is in
                    Plot_Data=app.Comments.Data{app.Trial,comCol}(app.Ch,:);                              %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);                                                 %Determine number of trials based on comment text
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;                                     %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
                case 3 %Events
                    Plot_Data=app.Events.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;
            end


        end %end ExtractBrainVision

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractSpike(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from Spike export files (.smr)
            %Extracts block data, then epochs based on  events
            %Blocks are from the Waveform channel type
            %Blocks are separated by the gaps in the Waveform data
            %If the channel units aren't defined they are assumed to be in mV
            %There is a check at the end to fill in any missing data
            %==============================================================

            %Blocks
            if app.DataExtracted == 0
                %Extract Data types of all possible channels
                maxChan = CEDS64MaxChan(app.Spike_Data.hdr); %max number of channels this file can hold
                dataTypeAll=zeros(maxChan,1);
                for i=1:maxChan %look through all possible channels
                    dataTypeAll(i,1)=CEDS64ChanType(app.Spike_Data.hdr,i); %channel type
                end

                %Determine channel type for each channel    %Channel Types
                % noChan=find(dataTypeAll=0);               %No channel
                WaveformChan=find(dataTypeAll == 1);        %Waveform
                % EventFallChan=find(dataTypeAll == 2);     %Event falling edge
                % EventRiseChan=find(dataTypeAll == 3);       %Event rising edge
                % EventBothChan=find(dataTypeAll == 4);     %Event both edges
                % MarkerChan=find(dataTypeAll == 5);        %Marker
                % WaveMarkChan=find(dataTypeAll == 6);      %WaveMark
                % RealMarkChan=find(dataTypeAll == 7);      %RealMark
                % TextMarkChan=find(dataTypeAll == 8);      %TextMark
                % RealWaveChan=find(dataTypeAll == 9);      %RealWave

                %Extract blocks
                CBlock=1; %current block
                for i=1:app.numChannels

                    maxTimeTicks = CEDS64ChanMaxTime(app.Spike_Data.hdr, WaveformChan(i))+1; %last tick to have an item
                    [~, ChData, FirstTick ] = CEDS64ReadWaveF(app.Spike_Data.hdr, WaveformChan(i), maxTimeTicks, 0, maxTimeTicks ); %(handle, channel number, max number of events to return, starting at, up to)
                    %FirstTick = first tick with an item
                    app.Spike_Data.data{i,1}=ChData';
                    app.Spike_Data.Ticks{i,1}=[FirstTick:CEDS64ChanDiv(app.Spike_Data.hdr,WaveformChan(i)):maxTimeTicks];

                    %ReadWaveF stops at gaps in the data
                    %Separating Blocks based on gaps
                    while (length(ChData)*CEDS64ChanDiv(app.Spike_Data.hdr,WaveformChan(i))+FirstTick) < maxTimeTicks %keep reading the data for this channel until the last tick with an item is reached
                        next=length(ChData)*CEDS64ChanDiv(app.Spike_Data.hdr,WaveformChan(i))+FirstTick;              %next starting tick for the search
                        [~, ChData, FirstTick ] = CEDS64ReadWaveF(app.Spike_Data.hdr, WaveformChan(i), maxTimeTicks, next, maxTimeTicks ); %search for next block of data
                        app.Spike_Data.data{i,CBlock+1}=ChData';
                        app.Spike_Data.Ticks{i,CBlock+1}=[FirstTick:CEDS64ChanDiv(app.Spike_Data.hdr,WaveformChan(i)):maxTimeTicks];
                        CBlock=CBlock+1;
                    end
                    CBlock=1;

                end

                %check units
                E=0; AmV=[]; AllChUnits=strings(app.numChannels,1);
                for i=1:app.numChannels
                    [~,ChUnits] = CEDS64ChanUnits(app.Spike_Data.hdr, i);
                    if isempty(ChUnits)
                        ChUnits="mV"; %if the units are not defined, assume mV !!!
                        E=E+1;
                        AmV(E)=i;
                        
                    else
                        ChUnits=string(ChUnits);
                    end
                    ChUnitsChar=char(ChUnits);
                    ChUnitsChar(ChUnitsChar == ' ')='';
                    AllChUnits(i,1)=string(ChUnitsChar);
                end


                %Convert to volts
                %For channels assumed to be in mV convert to volts
                if E ~= 0
                    if E==app.numChannels
                        app.Spike_Data.data=cellfun(@(x) x*0.001,app.Spike_Data.data,'UniformOutput',false); %convert to volts
                        %msgbox('Note: All channels are assumed to be in mV');
                    else
                        for i=AmV %convert channels that are assumed
                            app.Spike_Data.data(i,:)=cellfun(@(x) x*0.001,app.Spike_Data.data(i,:),'UniformOutput',false); %convert to volts
                        end
                        %msgbox({'Note: Some channel units are not defined, they are assumed to be mV'; ['Channels ' num2str(AmV)]});

                    end
                else
                    %for each channel check units, convert to volts
                    for i=1:app.numChannels
                        ChUnit=AllChUnits(i);
                        switch ChUnit
                            case {"volt","V"}

                            case "mV"
                                app.Spike_Data.data(i,:)=cellfun(@(x) x*0.001,app.Spike_Data.data(i,:),'UniformOutput',false);
                            case "uV"
                                app.Spike_Data.data(i,:)=cellfun(@(x) x*1e-6,app.Spike_Data.data(i,:),'UniformOutput',false);
                        end
                    end
                end


                %check for missing data
                %make sure each block for every channel has the same amount of data
                maxArray = max(cellfun(@(x) size(x,2),app.Spike_Data.data)); %max length for each block
                Interp1=0;
                for iData = 1:size(app.Spike_Data.data,1) %for each channel
                    %check each block
                    for i=1:length(app.Spike_Data.data(iData,:)) %checks every block for each channel
                        if size(app.Spike_Data.data{iData,i},2) ~= maxArray(i)
                            factor = 1/(size(app.Spike_Data.data{iData},2)/maxArray);
                            app.Spike_Data.data{iData,i}= interp(app.Spike_Data.data{iData,i},round(factor)); %interpolate for missing data
                            Interp1=Interp1+1;
                        end
                    end
                end
                %display how many data points were interpolated
                % if Interp1 ~= 0
                %     disp(['Spike Interp1: ' num2str(Interp1)]);
                % end

                %create blocks
                app.Block.Data=app.Spike_Data.data;
                app.numBlocks=length(app.Block.Data(1,:));

                %extract channel titles
                app.Block.Titles=strings(8,1);
                for i=1:app.numChannels
                    [~, T]=CEDS64ChanTitle(app.Spike_Data.hdr,i);
                    app.Block.Titles(i)=string(T);
                end

                %Update channel titles if desired
                % ChangeTitles=questdlg('There are untitled channels. Would you like to rename them?','Change Titles?','Yes','No','No');
                % if ChangeTitles == 'Yes'
                %     untitles=find(app.Block.Titles == "untitled");
                %     prompt=strings(length(untitles),1);
                %     e=1;
                %     for i=untitles'
                %         prompt(e)=['Channel ' num2str(i) ':'];
                %         e=e+1;
                %     end
                %     dlgtitle='Title Change';
                %     NewChannelNames=inputdlg(prompt,dlgtitle);
                %     e=1;
                %     for i=untitles'
                %         if isempty(NewChannelNames{e,1})
                %             NewChannelNames{e,1}='Untitled';
                %         end
                %         app.Block.Titles(i)=string(NewChannelNames{e,1});
                %         e=e+1;
                %     end
                % end

                app.DataExtracted=1;
            end

            %Epoch Type
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);

                case 2
                    %Comments !!! assumed there is only one comment text option
                    app.ComList="Events";
                    app.Comments.CommentTxtOptions=app.ComList;
                    app.Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;
                    for i=1:app.numChannels
                        for T=1:length(app.EventTicks)
                            DiffStart=0; DiffEnd=0;
                            EventIndx=find(app.Spike_Data.Ticks{1}==app.EventTicks(T)); %!!! why just the first channel ticks?
                            if ~isempty(EventIndx)
                                Data=app.Spike_Data.data{i};
                                StartIndx=EventIndx-app.PreDur*app.AllSampleRate;
                                EndIndx=EventIndx+app.PostDur*app.AllSampleRate;
                                if StartIndx <= 0
                                    DiffStart=-StartIndx+1;
                                    StartIndx=1;
                                end
                                if EndIndx > length(Data)
                                    DiffEnd=EndIndx-length(Data);
                                    EndIndx=length(Data);
                                end
                                    
                                app.Comments.Data{T,1}(i,:)=[nan(1,DiffStart) Data(StartIndx:EndIndx) nan(1,DiffEnd)];
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if ~isfield(app.Comments,'Data') %no comment were found
                        msgbox('No Comments found');
                        app.CommentsButton.Enable=0;
                        app.BlocksButton.Value = 1;
                        EpochTypesButtonGroupSelectionChanged(app,1);
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    else
                        app.numTrialsC=length(app.Comments.Data);
                        app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC;
                    end
                case 3 %Events
                    [app.numTrials, app.Events] = GetEvents(app);
                    if app.numTrials == 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    end

                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
            end

            %Plotting
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1 %Block
                    Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                           %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate
                    if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                        Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
                    end
                    if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                            %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                        FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                        %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                        if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                            FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                        end
                        Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                        Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
                    end

                case 2 %Comments
                    Time=app.Time;
                    Plot_Data=app.Comments.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);

                case 3 %Events
                    Plot_Data=app.Events.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;
            end

        end %end ExtractSpike

        function [Plot_Data,Time,ChTitle] = ExtractCustom(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Extract Blocks and Comment data using a custom function
            %==============================================================
            if app.DataExtracted == 0
                for i=1:length(app.Filename)
                    [numChannelsF,numBlocksF,BlockF,AllSampleRateF,EpochTypeOptions,Custom_DataF,CommentLocAllF,numTrialsCF,CommentsF.CommentTxtOptions] = eval([app.CustomUploadFile '(app,i);']); %Run associated function

                    if i==1
                        %assume same number of channels for all files
                        %assume same sample rate for all files
                        app.numChannels=numChannelsF;                      %assume same number of channels for each file
                        app.AllSampleRate=AllSampleRateF;
                        app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
                        app.Block.Data=cell(app.numChannels,1);
                    end

                    %Update variables
                    app.numBlocks=app.numBlocks+numBlocksF;                                                                              %add to numBlocks
                    Range=length(app.Block.Data(1,:))+1:length(app.Block.Data(1,:))+length(BlockF.Data(1,:));
                    app.Block.Data(1:numChannelsF,Range)=BlockF.Data;    %Add to block data
                    app.Block.Titles(1:numChannelsF,Range)=repmat(BlockF.Titles,1,length(Range));
                    app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(i) ]).Block=BlockF.Data;                                                        %Add block data for file
                    app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(i) ]).Data=Custom_DataF;                                                        %save orignal data format
                    app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(i) ]).CommentLocAll=CommentLocAllF;                                             %add to CommentLocAll
                    app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(i)]).CommentTxtOptions=CommentsF.CommentTxtOptions;                             %add comment text options
                    app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(i)]).numTrialsC=numTrialsCF;                                                    %add number of comments
                    app.ComList(end+1:end+length(CommentsF.CommentTxtOptions),1)=CommentsF.CommentTxtOptions;                            %update ComList (displayed in Event Name List Box)


                    %Checks for invalid values
                    %numChannels, AllSampleRate, numBlocks
                    if app.numChannels <= 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        msgbox('Invalid number of channels');
                        return
                    elseif app.AllSampleRate <=0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        msgbox('Invalid sample rate');
                        return
                    elseif app.numBlocks <= 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        msgbox('Invalid number of blocks');
                        return
                    end

                end %end File loop

                app.Block.Data(:,1)=[];
                app.Block.Titles(:,1)=[];
                app.ComList=app.ComList(2:end);
                app.Comments.CommentTxtOptions=app.ComList;

                %if multiple files, check channels
                CheckMultiFileChan(app)


                %Enable buttons for desired Epoch Types
                for i=1:length(EpochTypeOptions)
                    switch i
                        case 1 %Set up for Block data
                            app.BlocksButton.Enable=1;
                        case 2 %Set up for Comments
                            app.CommentsButton.Enable=1;
                        case 3 %Set up for Events
                            app.EventsButton.Enable=1;
                    end
                end
                app.EpochType=1; %set to block initially
                app.DataExtracted=1;

            end %end DataExtracted

            %If Block Data is empty
            if isempty(app.Block)
                Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                msgbox('Block Data is empty');
                return
            end

            %Check epoch type
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.numTrials=app.numBlocks;
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                case 2 %Comments
                    PreDurValue=app.PreDur*app.AllSampleRate;
                    PostDurValue=app.PostDur*app.AllSampleRate;
                    Data2=zeros(app.numChannels,length(-PreDurValue:PostDurValue));

                    ComColAll=1; EndBlock=1;
                    %!!! look into effiency of this
                    for e=1:length(app.Filename)                                            %for each file
                        app.Custom_Data=app.Custom_DataAll.(['File_' num2str(e)]); %Set current data file
                        %FilenumChannels=length(app.Custom_Data.Block(:,1));
                        numBlocksF=length(app.Custom_Data.Block(1,:));
                        app.Custom_Data.Block=app.Block.Data(:,EndBlock:EndBlock+numBlocksF-1);
                        EndBlock=EndBlock+numBlocksF;
                        for i=1:length(string(app.Custom_Data.CommentTxtOptions))           %for each comment text
                            for i2=1:app.Custom_Data.numTrialsC(i)                          %for each comment of the current comment text
                                CommentLoc=app.Custom_Data.CommentLocAll{i2,i}(1,2);
                                CommentBlk=app.Custom_Data.CommentLocAll{i2,i}(1,1);
                                %Parse out desired data
                                StartDiff=0; EndDiff=0;
                                Start=CommentLoc-PreDurValue; End=CommentLoc+PostDurValue;
                                if Start <= 0 %if start is less than zero, fill in with NaNs
                                    StartDiff=-Start+1;
                                    Start=1;
                                end
                                %first row with data
