                                FR=find(cellfun(@isempty,app.Custom_Data.Block(:,CommentBlk)) == 0);
                                DataLength= length(app.Custom_Data.Block{FR(1),CommentBlk});                %use first row/channel that has data
                                if End > DataLength %if end is greater than the length of the data, fill with NaNs
                                    EndDiff=End-DataLength;
                                    End=DataLength;
                                end
                                Data=app.Custom_Data.Block(:,CommentBlk);
                                %reformat and cut data
                                for i3=1:app.numChannels
                                    if isempty(Data{i3})
                                        Data{i3}= nan(1,DataLength);
                                    end
                                    Data2(i3,:)=[nan(1,StartDiff) Data{i3}(Start:End) nan(1,EndDiff)]; %fill beginning and end with NaNs if needed
                                end
                                app.Comments.Data{i2,ComColAll}=Data2; %each column is for a comment text, order of comment texts is the same
                            end
                            ComColAll=ComColAll+1;
                        end %end for each comment text option
                        app.numTrialsC=[app.numTrialsC app.Custom_Data.numTrialsC];

                    end %end for each file

                    app.SelectedComtxt = app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                    %update trial spinner limits and trial number based on specified comment text
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);           %redefine numTrials
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                    app.Trial = app.TrialSpinner.Value;             %update trial number, if current trial number selected is above the number of trials in the newly
                    %selected comment text the spinner will set to the max trial number, this will set the trial number to this value

                case 3 %Events
                    [app.numTrials, app.Events] = GetEvents(app);
                    if app.numTrials == 0
                        Plot_Data=[]; ChTitle=""; Time=[];
                        return
                    end


                    %update trial spinner limits
                    updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);

            end %end epochtype switch


            %Plotting
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1 %Block
                    Plot_Data=app.Block.Data{app.Ch,app.Trial};                                           %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=0:1/app.AllSampleRate:length(Plot_Data)/app.AllSampleRate-(1/app.AllSampleRate); %Time defined by sample rate
                    if app.PreTriggerTime ~= 0
                        Time=Time-app.PreTriggerTime/1000;                                                %Adjust for Pre trigger setting
                    end
                    if app.PostTriggerTime ~=0                                                            %Adjust for Post trigger setting
                        FramesAfter=app.PostTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate+app.PreTriggerTime/1000*app.AllSampleRate;
                        %check for if post trigger time is beyond the dataset
                        if FramesAfter > length(Plot_Data)
                            FramesAfter=length(Plot_Data);
                            app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=FramesAfter/app.AllSampleRate*1000;
                        end
                        Time=Time(1:FramesAfter);
                        Plot_Data=Plot_Data(1:FramesAfter);
                    end
                case 2 %Comments
                    Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);                                                       %Determine current specified comment text
                    comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;                                         %Use text to define the column the data is in
                    Plot_Data=app.Comments.Data{app.Trial,comCol}(app.Ch,:);                              %Define data based on current selected channel and trial
                    app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);                                                 %Determine number of trials based on comment text
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);                                                     %Channel title of current selected channel
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;                                     %Time defined by sample rate and pre/post duration settings
                case 3 %Events
                    Plot_Data=app.Events.Data{app.Trial}(app.Ch,:);
                    ChTitle=app.Block.Titles(app.Ch);
                    Time=-app.PreDur:1/app.AllSampleRate:app.PostDur;
            end

        end %end ExtractCustom

        %Custom File Upload
        function CustomFileMenuSelected(app,event)

            %Select file
            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.*'),'multiSelect','on');
            figure(app.UIFigure)            %focus back to app
            if isequal(app.Filename,0)      %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end

            %Check for one file
            if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                TempFilename=app.Filename;
                app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
            end

            %print filename or list of files
            if length(app.Filename) > 1
                app.DataFileEditField.Value="See File Order";
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=1; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
                app.FileOrderListBox.Items=app.Filename;
            else
                app.DataFileEditField.Value=string(app.Filename);
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
            end

            app.DataExtracted=0;
            app.DataType=8;        %Custom file
            app.BlocksButton.Enable=0; app.CommentsButton.Enable=0; app.EventsButton.Enable=0;

            %Identify custom file function
            app.CustomUploadFile=event.Source.Text;

        end

        function loadingScreen(app,Enable1)
            %==============================================================
            %Disable all of app, so nothing can be interacted with during
            %certain processes
            %==============================================================
            switch Enable1
                case 1
                    app.App_Panel.Enable=0;
                    app.FileMenu.Enable=0;
                    pause(0.001);
                case 0
                    pause(0.001);
                    app.App_Panel.Enable=1;
                    app.FileMenu.Enable=1;
            end


        end

        function UIAxesLimitsChangedFcn(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Update axes edit fields when they are changed
            %Callback for UIAxes YLim and XLim listeners
            %==============================================================
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

        end

        function axToolbarSelected(app,event,varargin)
            %==============================================================
            %Checks previous axes toolbar selection, ignores a warning
            %Callback of axes toolbar
            %==============================================================

            PrevSelection=app.PrevToolBarSelect;
            CurrSelection=string(event.Selection.Tag);
            app.CurrToolBarSelect=CurrSelection; %added for check in override
            if PrevSelection == "" %New item has been select, when nothing was selected before
                warning('off','MATLAB:modes:mode:InvalidPropertySet'); %ignore this warning because, the callback is not actually being set !!!
            end
            if CurrSelection == app.PrevToolBarSelect %if the same item is selected again (deselected)
                app.PrevToolBarSelect="";
                app.CurrToolBarSelect="";
                warning('on','MATLAB:modes:mode:InvalidPropertySet');
            else
                app.PrevToolBarSelect=CurrSelection;
            end

        end


        
       
    end


    % Callbacks that handle component events
    methods (Access = private)

        % Code that executes after component creation
        function startupFcn(app)
            %==============================================================
            %Startup function runs when app is initially opened
            %Adds functions folder
            %Resets figure and variables to default
            %==============================================================

            addpath(genpath('Fcns'));       %add Functions folder and subfolders to path

            %Check directory
            if isfile(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'))                  %directory has been set before, use this directory
                DirSettings=load(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'));
                app.Directory.SaveFolder=DirSettings.Dir.SaveFolder;
                app.Directory.DataFolder=DirSettings.Dir.DataFolder;
            else                                                %directory has not been set before, prompt for directory
                %Select save folder
                uiwait(msgbox("Select Save Folder, this is where outputs will be saved"));
                app.Directory.SaveFolder=uigetdir(fullfile(userpath,'Save Folder'));
                %Select data folder
                uiwait(msgbox("Select Data Folder, this is where the raw data is stored"));
                app.Directory.DataFolder=uigetdir(fullfile(userpath,'Data Folder'));
                figure(app.UIFigure)
                if string(class(app.Directory.DataFolder)) ~= "char" || string(class(app.Directory.SaveFolder)) ~= "char"  %if either folder was not selected
                    if (string(class(app.Directory.DataFolder)) ~= "char" && app.Directory.DataFolder == 0) && (string(class(app.Directory.SaveFolder)) == "char") %cancel was hit for Data folder, make the same as Save folder
                        %app.Directory.SaveFolder=app.Directory.DataFolder;
                        app.Directory.DataFolder=app.Directory.SaveFolder;
                        Dir=app.Directory;
                        save(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'),'Dir');

                    elseif (string(class(app.Directory.SaveFolder)) ~= "char" && app.Directory.SaveFolder == 0) && (string(class(app.Directory.DataFolder)) == "char") %cancel was hit for Save folder, make the same as Save folder
                        app.Directory.SaveFolder=app.Directory.DataFolder;
                        Dir=app.Directory;
                        save(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'),'Dir');

                    else  %cancel was hit for both, default both to folder app is saved in
                        app.Directory.SaveFolder=userpath;
                        app.Directory.DataFolder=userpath;
                    end
                else %both were selected
                    Dir=app.Directory;
                    if ~isfolder(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName))
                        mkdir(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName));
                    end
                    save(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'),'Dir');
                end
            end

            %Axes settings
            app.Toolbar=axtoolbar(app.UIAxes,{'export','datacursor','pan','zoomin','zoomout'},'SelectionChangedFcn',@(scrs,event) axToolbarSelected(app,event));
            saveasfig=axtoolbarbtn(app.Toolbar,"push");
            saveasfig.ButtonPushedFcn=@saveasmfig;
            saveasfig.Icon = "export";
            saveasfig.Tooltip = "Save to .fig file";

            addlistener(app.UIAxes, 'XLim', 'PostSet', @(src, event) app.UIAxesLimitsChangedFcn);
            addlistener(app.UIAxes, 'YLim', 'PostSet', @(src, event) app.UIAxesLimitsChangedFcn);

            %Reset app
            app.refreshApp; 
            app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData = {'_MEP','_SP','_EMGA'}; %Analysis options
            movegui(app.UIFigure,'center');   %moves app to center of screen

            %Disable Analyze EMG Analysis 12/3/25 !!!
            S=uistyle("BackgroundColor","#b82c43"); %#F77A8F red, #858482 dark gray
            addStyle(app.AnalysisOptionDropDown,S,"item",3);

            function saveasmfig(~,event)
                SaveFolder=app.Directory.SaveFolder;
                figure(); hold on;
                lgd=[];
                for i=1:length(event.Axes.Children)
                    switch string(class(event.Axes.Children(i)))
                        case "matlab.graphics.chart.primitive.Line"
                            XData=event.Axes.Children(i).XData;
                            YData=event.Axes.Children(i).YData;
                            plot(XData,YData);

                        case "matlab.graphics.chart.decoration.ConstantLine"
                            if event.Axes.Children(i).InterceptAxis == 'y'
                                yline(event.Axes.Children(i).Value,'color',event.Axes.Children(i).Color,'linestyle',event.Axes.Children(i).LineStyle,'linewidth',event.Axes.Children(i).LineWidth);
                                lgd=[lgd string(event.Axes.Children(i).DisplayName)];
                            else %should be 'y' or 'x'
                                xline(event.Axes.Children(i).Value,'color',event.Axes.Children(i).Color,'linestyle',event.Axes.Children(i).LineStyle,'linewidth',event.Axes.Children(i).LineWidth);
                                lgd=[lgd string(event.Axes.Children(i).DisplayName)];
                            end

                        case "matlab.graphics.primitive.Patch"
                            patch(event.Axes.Children(i).XData,event.Axes.Children(i).YData,'b','FaceColor','#969590','EdgeColor','black','FaceAlpha',0.2);
                            lgd=[lgd string(event.Axes.Children(i).DisplayName)];
                    end
                end
                legend(lgd);
                xlabel(event.Axes.XLabel.String)
                ylabel(event.Axes.YLabel.String)
                title(event.Axes.Title.String)

                %save figure
                SaveFigName=inputdlg('Enter filename:','Save figure',[1 35]);
                if isempty(SaveFigName)
                    return;
                end

                %Check if .fig was included in the filename
                if sum(SaveFigName{1,1}(end-3:end) == '.fig') == 4
                    SaveFigName{1,1}(end-3:end)='';
                end

                %Save figure
                savefig([SaveFolder '\' SaveFigName{1,1}]); 
            end


        end

        % Button pushed function: ChangeDataFolderButton
        function ChangeDataFolderButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Change the Data folder of the directory
            %New directory path is saved in DirectorySettings.mat
            %==============================================================

            PrevDir=app.Directory.DataFolder; %save previous directory
            uiwait(msgbox("Select Data Folder, this is where the raw data is stored"));
            app.Directory.DataFolder=uigetdir(app.Directory.DataFolder,'Data Folder');
            figure(app.UIFigure)
            if app.Directory.DataFolder == 0 %cancel was hit, don't change directory
                app.Directory.DataFolder=PrevDir;
            else %update directory
                Dir=app.Directory;
                save(fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'DirectorySettings.mat'),'Dir');
            end
        end

            end

        % Menu selected function: SignalmatcfsMenu
        function SignalmatcfsMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Loads Signal export files (.mat and .cfs)
            %Uses a dialog to determine which file type will be imported
            %If the user chooses to convert a .cfs file, the file will be
            %converted and the resulting .mat file will be loaded
            %==============================================================

            %Checks which file type was loaded
            %.cfs files are converted to .mat files with the same name,
            %they are saved to the same folder

            %Determine file type: exported .mat or .cfs file/converted .cfs file
            FileType = questdlg('File Type?','Import','Import .mat export','Convert .cfs or import converted .cfs','Convert .cfs or import converted .cfs');
            switch FileType
                case 'Import .mat export' % used if excported to .mat in Signal
                    %Import files
                    [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.mat'),"multiSelect","off");
                    if isequal(app.Filename,0) %if cancel is chosen return to app
                        msgbox('No File Chosen');
                        return
                    end
                    figure(app.UIFigure)

                    loadingScreen(app,1);
                    Signal_Data1 = load(fullfile(app.Path, app.Filename));
                    loadingScreen(app,0);
                    DataName = fieldnames(Signal_Data1); 
                    app.Signal_Data=Signal_Data1.(DataName{1,1});

                    app.SignalFile = 1;

                case 'Convert .cfs or import converted .cfs' % used if file was originally saved as .cfs and/or converted within toolbox
                    %Import files
                    [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile({'*.cfs;*.mat','CFS Files (.cfs), MAT Files (.mat)'}, "Choose a file to load",app.Directory.DataFolder,"multiselect", "off");
                    if numel(app.Filename) == 1 && all(app.Filename == 0)
                        msgbox('No File Chosen');
                        return
                    end
                    figure(app.UIFigure)

                    loadingScreen(app,1);
                    %determine data file type
                    Extension=app.Filename(end-3:end);
                    switch Extension
                        case '.cfs' %convert to .mat
                            cfs2mat.Convert(app.Filename, app.Path); %convert .cfs file to .mat file, saves to folder
                            Signal_Data1=load([app.Path app.Filename(1:end-4) '.mat']); %converted file should have the same filename
                            app.Signal_Data=Signal_Data1.CfsFile;
                        case '.mat'%load converted .cfs file
                            Signal_Data1=load([app.Path app.Filename(1:end-4) '.mat']);
                            app.Signal_Data=Signal_Data1.CfsFile;
                    end
                    loadingScreen(app,0);

                    app.SignalFile = 2;
            end

            app.DataFileEditField.Value=app.Filename;
            app.DataType=3; %Signal Data

            %block type data only
            app.CommentsButton.Enable=0;
            app.EventsButton.Enable=0;
            app.EventNameListBox.Visible=1; app.EventNameListBoxLabel.Visible=1;




        end

        % Menu selected function: AcqknowledgematacqMenu
        function AcqknowledgematacqMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Loads Acqknowledge export files (.mat and .acq)
            %Uses a dialog to determine which file type will be imported
            %If the user chooses to convert a .acq file, a .mat file of
            %the same name will be searched for, if that file doesn't
            %exist, the .acq file will be converted and the resulting .mat
            %file will be loaded
            %Mulitple file upload is possible
            %The filenames are listed in the FileOrderListBox
            %==============================================================

            %Determine file type: exported .mat or .cfs file/converted .cfs file
            FileType = questdlg('File Type?','Import','Import .mat export','Convert .acq or import converted .acq','Convert .acq or import converted .acq');
            switch FileType
                case 'Import .mat export' % used if excported to .mat in Signal
                    %Import files
                    [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.mat'),"multiSelect","on");
                    if isequal(app.Filename,0) %if cancel is chosen return to app
                        msgbox('No File Chosen');
                        return
                    end
                    figure(app.UIFigure)

                    %check if there is only one file
                    if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                        TempFilename=app.Filename;
                        app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                        app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
                    end

                    loadingScreen(app,1);
                    %load each file
                    for i=1:length(app.Filename)
                        CurrFilename=app.Filename{i};
                        app.Acqknowledge_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i)]) = load(fullfile(app.Path, CurrFilename));
                    end
                    loadingScreen(app,0);

                    app.AcqFile=1; %exported .mat

                case 'Convert .acq or import converted .acq' % used if file was originally saved as .cfs and/or converted within toolbox
                    %Import files
                    [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile({'*.acq;*.mat','ACQ Files (.acq), MAT Files (.mat)'}, "Choose a file to load",app.Directory.DataFolder,"multiselect", "on");
                    if numel(app.Filename) == 1 && all(app.Filename == 0)
                        msgbox('No File Chosen');
                        return
                    end
                    figure(app.UIFigure)

                    %check if there is only one file
                    if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                        TempFilename=app.Filename;
                        app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                        app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
                    end


                    loadingScreen(app,1);
                    %load each file
                    for i=1:length(app.Filename) %can import mixture of .mat and .acq
                        CurrFilename=app.Filename{i};
                        %determine data file type
                        Extension=CurrFilename(end-3:end);
                        switch Extension
                            case '.acq' %convert to .mat
                                acq2matF(app.Path,CurrFilename); %Check for a .mat version of the file, if there is one load that file, if there isn't create one
                                Acqknowledge_DataA = load(fullfile(app.Path, [CurrFilename(1:end-4) '.mat'])); %load .mat file
                                app.Acqknowledge_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i)])=Acqknowledge_DataA.acq;
                            case '.mat' %load converted .cfs file
                                Acqknowledge_DataA = load(fullfile(app.Path, CurrFilename));
                                app.Acqknowledge_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i)])=Acqknowledge_DataA.acq;
                        end
                    end
                    loadingScreen(app,0);

                    app.AcqFile = 2;
            end

            %print filename or list of files
            if length(app.Filename) > 1
                app.DataFileEditField.Value="See File Order";
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=1; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
                app.FileOrderListBox.Items=app.Filename;
            else
                app.DataFileEditField.Value=string(app.Filename);
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
            end
            app.DataExtracted=0;                                            %reset for block data
            app.DataType=4; %Acqknowledge Data

            %block and epoch type data only
            app.CommentsButton.Enable=0;
        end

        % Menu selected function: BrainVisioneegMenu
        function BrainVisioneegMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Loads BrainVision export files (.eeg)
            %Data folder must contain the .eeg, .vhdr, and .vmrk files
            %Updates comment text options list
            %Mulitple file upload is possible
            %The filenames are listed in the FileOrderListBox
            %==============================================================

            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.eeg'),"multiSelect","on");
            if isequal(app.Filename,0) %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end
            figure(app.UIFigure)

            %check if there is only one file
            if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                TempFilename=app.Filename;
                app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
            end

            loadingScreen(app,1);
            %Load data
            for i=1:length(app.Filename)
                File=fullfile(app.Path,app.Filename{1,i});
                %Read file events
                FileEvents=ft_read_event(File);
                app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i) ]).FileEvents=FileEvents;

                %read header file
                FileHeader=ft_read_header([File(1:end-3),'vhdr']);
                app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i) ]).FileHeader=FileHeader;

                %read data
                FileData=ft_read_data([File(1:end-3),'vhdr']);
                app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['File' num2str(i) ]).Data=FileData; %chxsample
            end
            %loadingScreen(app,0);


            %% Find comment text
            for e=1:length(app.Filename) %for each file
                app.BrainVision_Data=app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['File' num2str(e)]);
                for i=1:length(app.BrainVision_Data.FileEvents) %For all comments
                    Sample=app.BrainVision_Data.FileEvents(i).sample;
                    CurrComText=string(app.BrainVision_Data.FileEvents(i).value);
                    if isempty(CurrComText) %empty trial
                        CurrComText="";
                    end

                    app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['Comments' num2str(e)])(i).CommentText=CurrComText;
                    app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['Comments' num2str(e)])(i).CommentLoc=Sample;

                    %Create comment text options list
                    if i==1
                        CommentTxtOptions=app.BrainVision_DataAll.(['Comments' num2str(e)])(1).CommentText; %add the first one
                    end
                    %check if current comment text is in the list already
                    L=CommentTxtOptions==CurrComText;
                    if sum(L)==0 %if not, add it to the list
                        CommentTxtOptions(end+1,1)=CurrComText;
                    end
                end
            end
            loadingScreen(app,0);

            %look for empty comments
            CommentTxtOptions(CommentTxtOptions=="")=[]; %remove empty comments

            app.Comments.CommentTxtOptions=CommentTxtOptions;
            app.ComList=app.Comments.CommentTxtOptions;


            %figure(app.UIFigure)

            %print filename or list of files
            if length(app.Filename) > 1
                app.DataFileEditField.Value="See File Order";
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=1; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
                app.FileOrderListBox.Items=app.Filename;
            else
                app.DataFileEditField.Value=string(app.Filename);
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
            end

            app.DataExtracted=0;                                           %reset for block data
            app.DataType=5; %BrainVision Data



        end

        % Menu selected function: SpikesmrMenu
        function SpikesmrMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Loads Spike export files (.smr)
            %Loads the CEDS64ML library
            %==============================================================

            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.smr'),"multiSelect","off");
            if isequal(app.Filename,0) %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end
            figure(app.UIFigure)

            %load CEDS64ML library, library is unloaded if the app is reset or closed
            cedpath=fullfile(pwd,'Fcns','Data_File_Import','Spike','CEDS64ML');
            CEDS64LoadLib( cedpath );

            loadingScreen(app,1);
            app.Spike_Data.hdr=CEDS64Open(fullfile(app.Path, app.Filename));    %Integer file handle for this file
            if (app.Spike_Data.hdr <= 0); unloadlibrary ceds64int; return; end  %If an error code is returned unload the library, add error warning? !!!
            
            %Check for comments
            %Extract Data types of all possible channels
            maxChan = CEDS64MaxChan(app.Spike_Data.hdr); %max number of channels this file can hold
            dataTypeAll=zeros(maxChan,1);
            for i=1:maxChan %look through all possible channels
                dataTypeAll(i,1)=CEDS64ChanType(app.Spike_Data.hdr,i); %channel type
            end
            EventRiseChan=find(dataTypeAll == 3);       %Event rising edge, for comments
            WaveformChan=find(dataTypeAll == 1);        %Waveform, for numChannels
            app.AllSampleRate=1/(CEDS64ChanDiv(app.Spike_Data.hdr, WaveformChan(1))*CEDS64TimeBase(app.Spike_Data.hdr)); %assumed same sampling rate for all channels and blocks
            app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];
            app.numChannels=length(WaveformChan);

            %Determine Comment locations if they exist
            [~, app.EventTicks]=CEDS64ReadEvents(app.Spike_Data.hdr, EventRiseChan, 10000, 1);
            if isempty(app.EventTicks) %there are no comments
                app.CommentsButton.Enable=0;
            else
                app.EventNameListBox.Items="Events"; app.ComList="Events";
                app.EventNameListBox.Value=app.EventNameListBox.Items{1};
                
            end

            loadingScreen(app,0);

            app.DataFileEditField.Value=app.Filename;
            app.DataExtracted=0;
            app.DataType=6;

            %block and epoch type data only
            %app.CommentsButton.Enable=0; %1/22/26 !!! adding comments


        end

        % Menu selected function: OpenConditionFileMenu
        function OpenConditionFileMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Open and load a Conditions File
            %This function is called when an old condition file is uploaded
            %as well. There is a check for if it is an old condition file
            %and the data is reformatted.
            %==============================================================

            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(strcat(app.Directory.DataFolder,'/*.mat'),"multiSelect","on");
            if isequal(app.Filename,0) %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end

            figure(app.UIFigure)
            loadingScreen(app,1);

            %check if there is only one file
            if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                TempFilename=app.Filename;
                app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
            end

            %load files
            if app.Old == 1
                warning('off','MATLAB:load:classNotFound'); %turn off warning for trying to load an app property
            end
            AllFileTitles=[];AllSampleRates=zeros(length(app.Filename),1); Filenames=strings(1);
            for i=1:length(app.Filename)
                app.LoadedConditions=load(fullfile(app.Path, app.Filename{1,i}));
                if app.Old == 1                                                         %if an old condition file is uploaded, reformat the data
                    app.LoadedConditions=convertOldConditionFile(app.LoadedConditions);
                end
                app.UITable.Data=[app.UITable.Data; app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix];
                app.Conditions_DataAll=[app.Conditions_DataAll; app.LoadedConditions.AllData];
                app.numTrialsCondition=[app.numTrialsCondition; app.LoadedConditions.numTrialsperCondition];
                app.LoadedConditionsAll.(['ConditionFile' num2str(i)])=app.LoadedConditions;
                AllFileTitles=[AllFileTitles; app.LoadedConditions.ChTitles];
                AllSampleRates(i)=app.LoadedConditions.SampleRate;
                Filenames=[Filenames string(app.LoadedConditions.FileName)];

                %app.Conditions_Data=[app.Conditions_Data; app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsData];

            end
            if app.Old == 1
                warning('on','MATLAB:load:classNotFound'); %turn warning back on
            end
            app.Old=0; %reset Old variable

            %Redefine Loaded Conditions, assume channel titles and sample rate are the same for all condition files
            %!!! don't assume same channels, sample rate: data is saved with its time points ?? ??
            app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix=app.UITable.Data;
            app.LoadedConditions.ChTitles=AllFileTitles;                                 %!!! 5/28 need to update channel list to match titles
            app.LoadedConditions.SampleRate=AllSampleRates;
            app.AllSampleRate=app.LoadedConditions.SampleRate(1); %!!! assume same sample rate
            app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate(1)') 'Hz']; %!!! displays first sample rate, assume sample rates are the same
            app.LoadedConditions.FileName=Filenames(2:end);
            app.Conditions_DataAll=app.Conditions_DataAll(2:end,:);
            app.numTrialsCondition=app.numTrialsCondition(2:end,:);

            %combine Conditions_DataAll
            if length(app.Filename) > 1
                for i=1:length(app.Conditions_DataAll(:,1)) %for each file
                    MinMaxTimes(i,1)=min(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,2});
                    MinMaxTimes(i,2)=max(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,2});
                end
                newTime=min(MinMaxTimes(:,1)):1/app.AllSampleRate:max(MinMaxTimes(:,2));
                DataAll=nan(sum(app.numTrialsCondition(:,1)),length(newTime));
                e=1;
                for i=1:length(app.Conditions_DataAll(:,1)) %for each file
                    if MinMaxTimes(i,1) > newTime(1) %if the data starts later
                        %determine padding length
                        PadLength=(MinMaxTimes(i,1)-newTime(1))*app.AllSampleRate;
                        app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}=[nan(length(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}(:,1)),single(PadLength)) app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}];
                    end
                    if MinMaxTimes(i,2) < newTime(end) %if the data ends earlier
                        %determine padding length
                        PadLength=(newTime(end)-MinMaxTimes(i,2))*app.AllSampleRate;
                        app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}=[app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1} nan(length(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}(:,1)),single(PadLength)) ];
                    end
                    DataAll(e:e+length(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}(:,1))-1,:)=app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1};
                    e=e+length(app.Conditions_DataAll{i,1}(:,1));
                end
                app.Conditions_DataAll=cell(1,2);
                app.Conditions_DataAll{1}=DataAll;
                app.Conditions_DataAll{2}=newTime;

                %combine numTrials
                CStart=1;
                for i=1:length(app.numTrialsCondition(:,1))
                    CEnd=CStart+app.numTrialsCondition(i,1)-1;
                    app.numTrialsCondition(i,2)=CStart;
                    app.numTrialsCondition(i,3)=CEnd;
                    CStart=CEnd+1;

                end
            end

            %print filename or list of files
            if length(app.Filename) > 1
                app.DataFileEditField.Value="See File Order";
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=1; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
                app.FileOrderListBox.Items=app.Filename;
            else
                app.DataFileEditField.Value=string(app.Filename);
                app.FileOrderListBox.Visible=0; app.FileOrderListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
            end


            %Hide/Show windows
            %Switch to Process tab automatically
            app.TabGroup.SelectedTab=app.ProcessTab;
            app.ProcessButton.Enable=1; app.AnalyzeButton.Enable=1; app.OverrideButton.Enable=1; %Enable processing and analysis

            %Disable windows
            app.EpochTypesButtonGroup.Enable=0;
            app.TrialSpinner.Enable=0; app.TrialSpinnerLabel.Enable=0;
            app.ChannelSpinner.Enable=0; app.ChannelSpinnerLabel.Enable=0;
            app.FindEventsButton.Enable=0;
            app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Enable=0;
            app.UITable.ColumnEditable=false;
            app.TrialSelectListBox.Enable=0;
            app.TrialSelectListBoxLabel.Enable=0;
            app.SelectAllButton.Enable=0;

            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Visible=1; app.ConditionTrialsLabel.Visible=1;
            app.SelectAllCondButton.Visible=1;
            app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=1;
            app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=1;
            app.ConditionsListBox.Visible=1; app.ConditionsListBoxLabel.Visible=1;

            %Disable Conditions Table buttons
            app.AddSelectedTrialsButton.Enable=0;
            app.AddBlankRowButton.Enable=0;
            app.ImportEventsButton.Enable=0;
            app.AutoFillButton.Enable=0;
            app.AutoFillEditField.Enable=0;
            app.KeeporDeleteRowsButton.Enable=0;
            app.SaveButton.Enable=0;
            app.ClearButton.Enable=0;
            app.ApplyButton.Enable=0;
            app.LoadConditionsButton.Enable=0;
            app.UploadExcelFileButton.Enable=0;
            app.SheetNameEditField.Enable=0;

            %Auto select first conditions
            %Fill Conditions List box
            app.ConditionsListBox.Items=app.UITable.Data(:,2);          %List Group Names
            app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData=app.ConditionsListBox.Items;

            %check for duplicate names
            ItemsData=string(app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData);
            for i=1:length(ItemsData) %for each item
                Copies=ItemsData(i) == ItemsData; %check for copies
                if sum(Copies) ~= 1 %if there are copies,
                    CopyLoc=find(Copies==1); %find the locations of all copies
                    for e=2:length(CopyLoc)  %for each copy after the first
                        ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)) = strcat(ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)), num2str(e)); %rename the copy
                    end
                end

            end
            app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData=ItemsData;

            loadingScreen(app,0);

            app.ConditionsListBox.Value=app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData(1); %Initial selection is the first condition
            ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1); %plot condition


        end

        % Menu selected function: OpenVersion1ConditionFilesMenu
        function OpenVersion1ConditionFilesMenuSelected(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Open and load an old Conditions File. Before new app.
            %==============================================================

            app.Old=1;
            OpenConditionFileMenuSelected(app, 1);

        end

        % Button pushed function: FindEventsButton
        function FindEventsButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Extracts data from files
            %Plots epoched data
            %==============================================================

            %Check if a file is loaded
            if isempty(app.DataType)
                msgbox('Load File First');
                return
            end

            if app.DisableLI_FindEvents == 0
                loadingScreen(app,1);
            end

            %Extract data based on which type of file is loaded
            switch app.DataType
                case 1  %LabChart
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractLabChart(app);     %Block, Comments, Events

                case 2 %BrainSight
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractBrainSight(app);   %Block only

                case 3 %Signal
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractSignal(app);       %Block only

                case 4 %Acqknowledge
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractAcqknowledge(app); %Block and Events

                case 5 %BrainVision
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractBrainVision(app);  %Block, Comments, Events

                case 6 %Spike
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractSpike(app);        %Block and Events

                case 8 %Custom file
                    [app.Plotted_Data,app.Time,app.CurrChTitle] = ExtractCustom(app);

            end

            if app.DisableLI_FindEvents == 0
                loadingScreen(app,0);
            end


            %update AllPlotted_Data, there will only be one column of data
            app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,1);
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,1}=app.Plotted_Data;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,3}=app.EpochType;
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}='Block';
                case 2
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                case 3
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventChannel;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,5}=app.EventThresh;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,6}=app.SearchISI;
            end


            %convert to selected units
            app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

            %Plot desired data
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data,'Color','k');
            app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
            app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
            app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

            %If there is only one trial or channel, disable the spinner
            if app.numChannels == 1
                app.ChannelSpinner.Enable=0;
            else
                app.ChannelSpinner.Enable=1;
            end
            if app.numTrials == 1
                app.TrialSpinner.Enable=0;
            else
                app.TrialSpinner.Enable=1;
            end

            %Update multiple trial select list
            updateMultiTrialList(app,app.numTrials);


        end

        % Value changed function: ChannelSpinner
        function ChannelSpinnerValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates channel spinner limits
            %Plots new channel data
            %==============================================================

            app.ChannelSpinner.Limits = [1 app.numChannels];
            app.Ch = app.ChannelSpinner.Value;

            %Update data to be plotted
            [app.Plotted_Data, app.CurrChTitle, app.Time]=updateSpinners(app,app.Trial);

            %update AllPlotted_Data, there will only be one column of data
            app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,1);
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,1}=app.Plotted_Data;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,3}=app.EpochType;
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}='Block';
                case 2
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                case 3
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventChannel;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,5}=app.EventThresh;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,6}=app.SearchISI;
            end

            %convert to selected units
            app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

            %Plot desired data
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data,'Color','k');
            app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
            app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
            app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);



        end

        % Value changed function: TrialSpinner
        function TrialSpinnerValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================

            updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
            app.Trial = app.TrialSpinner.Value;

            %Update data to be plotted

            %update AllPlotted_Data, there will only be one column of data
            app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,1);
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,1}=app.Plotted_Data;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
            app.AllPlotted_Data{1,3}=app.EpochType;
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}='Block';
                case 2
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                case 3
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4}=app.EventChannel;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,5}=app.EventThresh;
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,6}=app.SearchISI;
            end

            %convert to selected units
            app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

            %Plot desired data
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data,'Color','k');
            app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
            app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
            app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);


        end

        % Value changed function: UnitsDropDown
        function UnitsDropDownValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates plotted units
            %The data is converted from the previous unit to volts, then
            %converted to the desired unit
            %app.Convert2PlottedUnits is applied in FindEvents,
            %ChannelSpinnerValueChanged, and TrialSpinnerValueChanged
            %The data pulled from these callbacks are in volts
            %==============================================================

            PlottedUnitsPrev=app.PlottedUnits;     %save previous unit setting before updating
            app.PlottedUnits = app.UnitsDropDown.Value;

            %convert data to volts
            switch PlottedUnitsPrev
                case 'V'
                    %units already in volts
                case 'mV'
                    app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data/1000;

                case 'uV'
                    app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data/1e6;
            end

            %convert data to selected units
            switch app.PlottedUnits
                case 'V'
                    %units remain in volts
                    app.Convert2PlottedUnits=1;
                case 'mV'
                    app.Convert2PlottedUnits=1000;
                    app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;
                case 'uV'
                    app.Convert2PlottedUnits=1e6;
                    app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

            end

            %Plot
            %Plot desired data
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            if length(app.Plotted_Data(:,1)) == 1
                plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data,'Color','k'); %if single line plot, color black
            else
                hold(app.UIAxes,'On');
                plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data);
            end
            app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
            app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
            app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

            hold(app.UIAxes,'Off');


        end

        % Selection changed function: EpochTypesButtonGroup
        function EpochTypesButtonGroupSelectionChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Determines the epoch type (Blocks, Comments, or Events)
            %Default Epoch Type is Blocks
            %Udpates which windows are available based on the Epoch Type
            %Blocks: Pre/Post Trigger Time
            %Comments: EventsListBox, Set Data Window
            %Events: Event Parameters, Set Data Window
            %Also updates when the Multiple Trial Select Box is available
            %==============================================================

            %If comments are selected, show list of comment text
            if app.CommentsButton.Value == 1
                app.EpochType=2;
                %Update List of comments
                app.EventNameListBox.Items=string(app.ComList);
                app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData=app.EventNameListBox.Items;

                %check for duplicate names
                ItemsData=string(app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData);
                for i=1:length(ItemsData) %for each item
                    Copies=ItemsData(i) == ItemsData; %check for copies
                    if sum(Copies) ~= 1 %if there are copies,
                        CopyLoc=find(Copies==1); %find the locations of all copies
                        for e=2:length(CopyLoc)  %for each copy after the first
                            ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)) = strcat(ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)), num2str(e)); %rename the copy
                        end
                    end

                end
                app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData=ItemsData;
                app.SelectedComtxt = app.EventNameListBox.Value;

                %Update Window options
                app.SetDataWindowPanel.Visible=1;
                app.EventNameListBox.Visible=1; app.EventNameListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
                app.EventParametersPanel.Visible=0;
                app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Visible=0;
                app.ImportEventsButton.Enable=1;

                FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);

            elseif app.EventsButton.Value ==1
                app.EpochType=3;
                %Update Window options
                app.SetDataWindowPanel.Visible=1;
                app.EventNameListBox.Visible=0; app.EventNameListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
                app.EventParametersPanel.Visible=1;
                app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Visible=0;
                app.ImportEventsButton.Enable=0;

                %if Search ISI is zero don't auto run find events, if it is not zero it is assumed that at some point the user used the Events epoch type and there is valid data in the other parameters, if not an error will occur (part of GetEvents function)
                if app.SearchISI ~=0
                    FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
                end


            elseif app.BlocksButton.Value == 1
                app.EpochType=1;
                %Update Window options
                app.SetDataWindowPanel.Visible=0;
                app.EventNameListBox.Visible=0; app.EventNameListBoxLabel.Visible=0;
                app.EventParametersPanel.Visible=0;
                app.PrePostTriggerTimemsPanel.Visible=1;
                app.ImportEventsButton.Enable=1;

                FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);

            end


        end

        % Value changed function: EventNameListBox
        function EventNameListBoxValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates selected comment text option or selected state (for Signal data type)
            %Also updates the Trial Select List box
            %==============================================================

            switch app.DataType
                case {1,5,8,6} %LabChart, BrainVision, Custom, Spike
                    app.SelectedComtxt = app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                    %update trial spinner limits and trial number based on specified comment text
                    if ~isempty(app.Comments) && app.CommentsButton.Value == 1
                        Com=string(app.SelectedComtxt);
                        comCol=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData == Com;
                        app.numTrials=app.numTrialsC(comCol);           %redefine numTrials
                        updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                        app.Trial = app.TrialSpinner.Value;             %update trial number, if current trial number selected is above the number of trials in the newly
                        %selected comment text the spinner will set to the max trial number, this will set the trial number to this value
                    end
                case 3 %Signal
                    app.StateV=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                    %update trial spinner limits and trial number based on specified state
                    if ~isempty(app.Block)
                        app.numTrials=app.numTrialsState(app.StateV);
                        updateTrialSpinnerLimits(app);
                        app.Trial = app.TrialSpinner.Value;             %update trial number, if current trial number selected is above the number of trials in the newly
                        %selected comment text the spinner will set to the max trial number, this will set the trial number to this value
                    end
            end

            FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);

            %Update Trial Select List Box
            app.TrialSelectListBox.Items=string(1:app.numTrials);
        end

        % Button pushed function: AutoScaleButton
        function AutoScaleButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Sets X and Y axis limits to auto
            %==============================================================

            app.UIAxes.XLimMode = 'auto';
            app.UIAxes.YLimMode = 'auto';
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);
        end

        % Value changed function: TrialSelectListBox
        function TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates which trials are selected and plotted
            %==============================================================

            TrialSelectedList= app.TrialSelectListBox.Value;

            %Reset average check box if needed
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 1
                app.AverageCheckBox.Value=0;
            end

            app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,1);
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            hold(app.UIAxes,'on');
            LengthTime=0;
            for i=1:length(TrialSelectedList)
                TrialSelected=str2double(TrialSelectedList{i});

                [app.Plotted_Data, app.CurrChTitle, app.Time]=updateSpinners(app,TrialSelected);

                %Save all data plotted
                %AllPlotted_Data updated
                app.AllPlotted_Data{i,1}=app.Plotted_Data; 
                if app.EpochType ~= 1 && i==1
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time; %time should be the same for all plotted
                elseif app.EpochType == 1 && app.PreTriggerTime == 0 && app.PostTriggerTime == 0 %if plotting Blocks without adjusting the data it is possible for the data to be different lengths
                    if length(app.Time) > LengthTime
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
                        LengthTime=length(app.Time); 
                    end
                else
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
                end

                app.AllPlotted_Data{i,3}=app.EpochType;
                switch app.EpochType
                    case 1
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{i,4}='Block';
                    case 2
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{i,4}=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                    case 3
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{i,4}=app.EventChannel;
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{i,5}=app.EventThresh;
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{i,6}=app.SearchISI;
                end

                %convert to selected units
                app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

                %Plot desired data
                plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data);
                app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
                app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
                app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
                %update Scaling edit fields
                app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
                app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
                app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
                app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);
            end

            %Make lengths of the data the same
            DataLengths=cellfun(@length,app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1));
            if ~all(DataLengths == LengthTime)
                ShortLoc=find(DataLengths ~= LengthTime);
                for i=ShortLoc'
                    app.AllPlotted_Data{i,1}(end+1:end+(LengthTime-DataLengths(i)))=nan;
                end
            end


            hold(app.UIAxes,'off');


        end

        % Button pushed function: SelectAllButton
        function SelectAllButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Select all trials in the Trial Select list box and plot them
            %==============================================================

            app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=app.TrialSelectListBox.Items;
            TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app, 1)


        end

        % Button pushed function: SelectAllCondButton
        function SelectAllCondButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Select all trials in the Condition Trials list box and plot them
            %==============================================================
            %tic
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value=app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items;
            %a=toc %~16s current 9/22/25 using first 15 files of stress test data !!!
            %tic
            ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app, 1)
            %b=toc %~7s current 9/22/25 using first 15 files of stress test data !!!

        end

        % Button pushed function: AddSelectedTrialsButton
        function AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Add selected trials to a group in conditions table (UITable)
            %Saves the data as well (Conditions_Data)
            %==============================================================


            %Uncheck Average button - averaging will be done to the condition data if needed
            app.AverageCheckBox.Value=0;
            AverageCheckBoxValueChanged(app,1);

            %Determine trials to be added
            TrialsToAdd= app.TrialSelectListBox.Value;

            %Convert to comma delimited character array
            TrialsToAdd=convert2commadel(TrialsToAdd,',');


            %Create group information
            %groupInfo=cell(1,7); %xyz and grid not implemented
            groupInfo=cell(1,3);
            groupInfo{1}=TrialsToAdd; %Trials


            %Prompt for group name
            if app.ChangeCurrentRow == 0

                if app.autoImportEvents == 0 %Don't update group name for Import Events button

                    GroupName=inputdlg('Enter group name:','Group Name',[1 35],{''});
                    if isempty(GroupName) %if cancel is hit, don't add trials
                        pause(0.2); %disappears quickly so it "flashes", added pause to reduce flash affect
                        figure(app.UIFigure)
                        return;

                    else
                        %Check for spaces
                        GroupName=GroupName{1};
                        if isempty(GroupName) %if Ok is hit without filling a name, leave the cell empty

                        else
                            GroupName=strrep(GroupName,' ','_');
                            if GroupName(1) == '_'
                                GroupName(1)=[];
                            end
                            if GroupName(end) == '_'
                                GroupName(end) = [];
                            end
                            groupInfo{2}=GroupName;
                        end

                    end
                end

                groupInfo{3}=char(num2str(app.Ch));

                app.UITable.Data=[app.UITable.Data; groupInfo]; %update conditions table

                %Attach data
                numRows=length(app.UITable.Data(:,1)); %last row is the  newest row, so data is associated with that row
                CurrRow=numRows;

            elseif app.ChangeCurrentRow == 1 %A specific row in the table is being updated

                %Use info in the current row being changed
                groupInfo{2}=app.UITable.Data{app.CurrentRow,2};
                groupInfo{3}=app.UITable.Data{app.CurrentRow,3};
                app.UITable.Data(app.CurrentRow,:)=groupInfo;
                CurrRow=app.CurrentRow;
            end


            %Attach Data
            %Check if Data is all the same length
            % % LengthCheck=cellfun(@length, app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1));
            % % if ~all(LengthCheck == LengthCheck(1)) %if all the data isn't the same length
            % %     MaxLength=max(LengthCheck);
            % %     ShortLoc=find(LengthCheck ~= MaxLength);
            % % 
            % % end

            app.Conditions_Data{CurrRow,1}=app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1); %data
            app.Conditions_Data{CurrRow,1}{1,2}=app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}; %time
            app.Conditions_Data{CurrRow,2}=app.AllPlotted_Data{1,3};   %epoch type
            if length(app.AllPlotted_Data(1,:)) > 4
                app.Conditions_Data{CurrRow,3}=app.AllPlotted_Data(1,4:end); %more event details
            elseif length(app.AllPlotted_Data(1,:)) > 3
                app.Conditions_Data{CurrRow,3}=app.AllPlotted_Data{1,4};     %comment or event details
            end




        end

        % Button pushed function: ImportEventsButton
        function ImportEventsButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Automatically creates rows for each of the items in the Event
            %Name List Box,
            %fills with all the trials for each Event Name
            %uses the current channel spinner value
            %==============================================================
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=0;
            pause(0.1);
            
            app.autoImportEvents=1; %for AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed, Don't update group name for Import Events button

            %Current Epoch Type
            switch app.EpochType
                case 1
                    %Check if data has been created
                    if isempty(app.Block)
                        msgbox('Create Block data first');
                        return
                    end

                    if app.DataType == 3 %Signal - as of 7/15/25 this is the only data type this would be needed for
                        NumGroups=app.numStates;
                        numTrialsList=zeros(1,NumGroups);
                        for e=1:NumGroups
                            numTrialsList(e)=size(app.Block.Data{e,1},2);
                        end
                    else
                        msgbox('Not applicable');
                        return;
                    end

                case 2
                    %Check if data has been created
                    if isempty(app.Comments)
                        msgbox('Create Comments data first');
                        return
                    end
                    NumGroups=length(app.numTrialsC);
                    numTrialsList=app.numTrialsC;

            end

            %Select and add trials for each Event Name
            app.ImportEventsNameL=1; app.StateV=0;
            for i=1:NumGroups      %for each "group" (item in Event Name list box)
                if app.DataType==3 %Signal states
                    app.StateV=app.StateV+1;
                end
                app.TrialSelectListBox.Items=string(1:numTrialsList(i));   %update items in ListBox

                app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=app.TrialSelectListBox.Items; %select them all
                TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                     %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                       %Add Trials
                app.ImportEventsNameL=app.ImportEventsNameL+1;             %update which Event Name
            end
            if app.DataType == 3 %Signal
                app.StateV=1;
                app.EventNameListBox.Value=1;
            end

            app.autoImportEvents=0; %reset this variable
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;

        end

        % Button pushed function: AutoFillButton
        function AutoFillButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %In the Conditions Table, add in the selected trials columns
            %trials in numerical order
            %The amount added is chosen by the user, AutoFillEditField
            %==============================================================
            Rows=sort(app.SelectedTableRows);
            for e=Rows
                TableData=app.UITable.Data{e,1};
                CurrentTrials=string(TableData);
                CurrentTrials=str2double(split(CurrentTrials,','))'; %convert character array to double vector
                AmountToAdd=app.AutoFillEditField.Value;             %Value in auto fill edit field
                FinalValue=CurrentTrials(end) + AmountToAdd;         %determine final value
                AddOn=string(CurrentTrials(end)+1:1:FinalValue);     %create string with values from the end of the current values to the final value
                AddOnC=cell(1,length(AddOn));                        %convert to cell
                for i=1:length(AddOn)
                    AddOnC{i}=char(AddOn(i));
                end

                %Convert to comma delimited
                TrialsToAdd=convert2commadel(AddOnC,',');

                NewTrialList=strcat(TableData,',',TrialsToAdd);
                app.UITable.Data{e,1}=NewTrialList;

                %Check channel number
                app.ChannelSpinner.Value = str2double(app.UITable.Data{e,3});   %update channel number of data
                ChannelSpinnerValueChanged(app,1);

                %Update Data
                app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=split(NewTrialList,',')';          %select them all
                TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                          %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                app.ChangeCurrentRow=1;
                app.CurrentRow=e;
                AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                            %Add Trials
                app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;
            end


        end

        % Cell edit callback: UITable
        function UITableCellEdit(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update cell information using correct format
            %For Epoch column add commas between numbers
            %For Group Name column add underscores in whitespaces
            %==============================================================

            indices = event.Indices;       %Indices of table that are being edited
            editData = event.EditData;     %Updated data in indices
            PrevData = event.PreviousData; %Old data before updating

            TableColumn=indices(2);

            switch TableColumn
                case 1  %if Epochs column
                    if isempty(editData) %if the cell is edited to remove all trials,
                        app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={PrevData}; %Don't update cell
                        return;
                    end
                    editData2=strrep(editData,' ',',');
                    if editData2(1) == ','
                        editData2(1)=[];
                    end
                    if editData2(end) == ','
                        editData2(end) = [];
                    end
                    NewTrialList=split(editData2,',')';

                    Low=find(cellfun(@str2double, NewTrialList) <= 0);
                    High=find(cellfun(@str2double, NewTrialList) > app.numTrials);
                    OutofBounds=[Low High];

                    if ~isempty(OutofBounds) %if there are any invalid trial numbers, only add valid ones
                        msgbox('There are invalid trial numbers listed. Only adding valid numbers.')
                        NewTrialList(OutofBounds)=[];
                        editData2=convert2commadel(NewTrialList,',');
                        app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={editData2}; %update cell
                        return;
                    end

                    %Update Data
                    app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=NewTrialList;               %select the new trials
                    TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                   %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                    app.ChangeCurrentRow=1;
                    app.CurrentRow=indices(1);
                    AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                     %Add Trials
                    app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;

                    app.UITable.Data(indices(1), 3)={char(num2str(app.Ch))};

                case  2 %if group name
                    if isempty(editData)
                        app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={PrevData};  %Don't update cell
                        return;
                    end
                    %fill spaces with underscores
                    editData2=strrep(editData,' ','_');
                    if editData2(1) == '_'
                        editData2(1)=[];
                    end
                    if editData2(end) == '_'
                        editData2(end) = [];
                    end
                case 3 %update channel
                    %Check trials
                    Trials=app.UITable.Data(indices(1),1);
                    if isempty(Trials{1}) || isempty(editData) || str2double(editData) > app.numChannels || str2double(editData) <= 0 %if there are no trials or if the new channel is blank,
                        app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={PrevData}; %Don't update cell
                        return;
                    end


                    %Update plotted data
                    app.ChannelSpinner.Value=str2double(editData);
                    ChannelSpinnerValueChanged(app, 1);                     %Change channel

                    %check for trials outside of range
                    TableTrials=split(app.UITable.Data(indices(1),1),',')';
                    TrialSelectItems=string(app.TrialSelectListBox.Items); e=1;
                    for i=1:length(TableTrials)
                        TrialNum=string(TableTrials{i});
                        Present=TrialSelectItems == TrialNum;
                        if sum(Present) == 1
                            %The trial list contains this number
                            NewTrialList{e}=char(TrialNum);
                            e=e+1;
                        end
                    end

                    %Not all the trials were in the Trial select  !!!
                    if length(NewTrialList) ~= length(string(TableTrials)) %If there were invalid trials they are removed, there will be a difference in the length of the Trial list
                        %Prompt for whether to continue with new data, or to cancel and switch Event Name
                        K=questdlg('There are invalid trial numbers. Please check if the correct Event under Event Names is selected. If so, select continue and only valid trial numbers will be added/updated. If not, select cancel to change the Event Name.','Check Event Name','Continue','Cancel','Cancel');
                        switch K
                            case 'Continue'
                                app.UITable.Data(indices(1),1)={convert2commadel(NewTrialList,',')}; %update table
                            case 'Cancel'
                                app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={PrevData}; %Don't update cell
                                return;

                        end
                    end


                    app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=split(app.UITable.Data(indices(1),1),',')';    %select trials
                    TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                                      %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                    app.ChangeCurrentRow=1;
                    app.CurrentRow=indices(1);
                    AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                                        %Add Trials
                    app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;

                    editData2=editData;


                otherwise
                    editData2=editData;
            end

            %Update table
            app.UITable.Data(indices(1),indices(2))={editData2};



        end

        % Button pushed function: AddBlankRowButton
        function AddBlankRowButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Add a blank row of data to conditions table
            %==============================================================
            groupInfo = cell(1,3);  %xyz and grid not implemented
            app.UITable.Data = [app.UITable.Data;groupInfo];
        end

        % Cell selection callback: UITable
        function UITableCellSelection(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Highlight row selected, unhighlight row deselected
            %==============================================================

            indices = event.Indices;
            if isempty(indices)
                return
            end
            TableRow=indices(1); %row of selected cell

            if isempty(find(app.SelectedTableRows == TableRow, 1))
                s = uistyle('BackgroundColor','#a09fa1'); %Light #d8d7d9 #c6c5c7 #a09fa1 Dark
                addStyle(app.UITable,s,'row',TableRow);  %Highlight row
                app.SelectedTableRows(end+1)=TableRow;   %Add row to list of selected rows

            else %if value is selected already, deselect
                s = uistyle('BackgroundColor','white');
                addStyle(app.UITable,s,'row',TableRow); %Unhighlight row
                Row=find(app.SelectedTableRows == TableRow, 1); %locate row in vector
                app.SelectedTableRows(Row)=[]; %remove row from list
            end

        end

        % Button pushed function: ClearButton
        function ClearButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Clear Conditions table and Conditions_Data
            %==============================================================
            s = uistyle('BackgroundColor','white');
            addStyle(app.UITable,s,'table','');
            app.SelectedTableRows=[]; %Clear selected rows
            app.UITable.Data = [];
            app.Conditions_Data=cell(1,3); %xyz and grid not implemented
            app.SheetNameEditField.Value='';
        end

        % Button pushed function: KeeporDeleteRowsButton
        function KeeporDeleteRowsButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Keep or delete selected rows in the Conditions Table
            %Prompts user if they want to keep or delete the selected rows
            %==============================================================
            K=questdlg('Keep or delete selected row(s)?','Keep or Delete','Keep','Delete','Keep');

            %Save current table and data for Recover option
