            app.DeletedRows.Table=app.UITable.Data;
            app.DeletedRows.Data=app.Conditions_Data;
            app.RecoverButton.Enable=1;

            %Determine which rows to delete
            switch K
                case 'Keep'
                    AllRows=1:length(app.UITable.Data(:,1));
                    %determine which rows to delete
                    DeleteRows=AllRows;
                    for i=app.SelectedTableRows
                        DeleteRows=DeleteRows(DeleteRows ~= i);
                    end
                case 'Delete'
                    %determine which rows to delete
                    DeleteRows=app.SelectedTableRows;
            end

            %Delete rows from table and data
            e=0;
            for i=DeleteRows
                app.UITable.Data(i-e,:)=[];
                if ~isempty(app.Conditions_Data) %if there is data, delete rows from data
                    app.Conditions_Data(i-e,:)=[];
                end
                e=e+1;
            end
            s = uistyle('BackgroundColor','white');
            addStyle(app.UITable,s);
            app.SelectedTableRows=[]; %Clear selected rows
        end

        % Button pushed function: RecoverButton
        function RecoverButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Undoes the most recent keep or delete action
            %app.DeletedRows variable is updated when rows are deleted in
            %KeeporDeleteRowsButtonPushed function
            %It is a copy of the table and data before deleting the rows
            %==============================================================

            %Replace table and data with old table and data
            app.UITable.Data=app.DeletedRows.Table;
            app.Conditions_Data=app.DeletedRows.Data;
            app.DeletedRows=struct();   %reset DeleteedRows variable
            app.RecoverButton.Enable=0; %disable recover button

        end

        % Button pushed function: SaveButton
        function SaveButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Save condition file
            %==============================================================
            %check for blanks in rows
            if any(any(cellfun(@isempty,app.UITable.Data)))
                msgbox('There are empty cells in Condition Table. Please fill these in.');
                return;
            end

            %Apply matrix
            ApplyButtonPushed(app, 1);

            %Define data for saving
            ConditionsMatrix=app.UITable.Data;
            ChTitles=app.Block.Titles;
            SampleRate=app.AllSampleRate;
            FileName=app.Filename;
            numTrialsperCondition=app.numTrialsCondition;
            AllData=app.Conditions_DataAll;
            EventInfo=app.Conditions_Data(:,2:end);

            %Prompt for file name, no need to add .mat at end of filename
            SavedCondFName=inputdlg('Enter filename:','Save Conditions File',[1 35]);
            if isempty(SavedCondFName)
                return;
            end

            %Check if .mat was included in the filename
            if sum(SavedCondFName{1,1}(end-3:end) == '.mat') == 4
                SavedCondFName{1,1}(end-3:end)='';
            end

            %check if "Conditions" folder exists if not, create it
            ConditionsFolderDir=fullfile(app.Directory.SaveFolder, 'Conditions');
            if exist(ConditionsFolderDir,'dir') ~= 7 %7 folder exists
                mkdir(ConditionsFolderDir); 

            end

            %Disable panel to indicate loading
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=0;
            pause(0.1)
            save(fullfile(ConditionsFolderDir, [SavedCondFName{1, 1} '.mat']),'ConditionsMatrix','AllData','numTrialsperCondition','ChTitles','SampleRate','EventInfo','FileName');
            pause(0.1)
            %Enable panel to indicate saving is done
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;

        end

        % Button pushed function: ApplyButton
        function ApplyButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Apply current conditions table to current loaded data
            %==============================================================

            %check for blanks in rows
            if (isempty(app.UITable.Data)) || any(any(cellfun(@isempty,app.UITable.Data)))
                msgbox('There are empty cells in Condition Table. Please fill these in.');
                return;
            end

            %Uncheck average box
            app.AverageCheckBox.Value=0;
            if app.EventNameListBox.Visible == 1 %reset to first Event name items, if Import Events is used the last selections are the last manually selected Event Name and all the trials of the last Event Name item
                app.EventNameListBox.Value=app.EventNameListBox.ItemsData(1); %select first Event Name
                EventNameListBoxValueChanged(app,1);
            end
            AverageCheckBoxValueChanged(app,1);

            %Make all data same length
            numConditions=length(app.Conditions_Data(:,1));
            TimeRanges=zeros(numConditions,2);
            for i=1:numConditions
                TimeRanges(i,1)=app.Conditions_Data{i}{1,2}(1);
                TimeRanges(i,2)=app.Conditions_Data{i}{1,2}(end);
            end

            %Determine max time length, and which condition has this max length
            minTimeRange=min(TimeRanges(:,1));
            maxTimeRange=max(TimeRanges(:,2));

            %create time base on the smallest and largest time
            newTime=minTimeRange:1/app.AllSampleRate:maxTimeRange;

            newConditions_Data=cell(numConditions,length(app.Conditions_Data(1,:))+1);
            newConditions_Data(:,3:end)=app.Conditions_Data(:,2:end);
            newConditions_Data{1,2}=newTime;
            for i=1:numConditions
                Data=app.Conditions_Data{i}(:,1);
                DataTime=app.Conditions_Data{i}{1,2};
                %check if time already matches new Time
                if (length(DataTime) ~= length(newTime)) || all(DataTime ~= newTime) %if time doesn't match newTime
                    %find start index
                    [~,StartIndx]=min(abs(newTime - DataTime(1)));
                    [~,EndIndx]=min(abs(newTime - DataTime(end)));
                    DataMat=cell2mat(Data);
                    newData=[nan(length(DataMat(:,1)),StartIndx-1) DataMat nan(length(DataMat(:,1)),length(newTime)-EndIndx)];
                    newConditions_Data{i,1}=newData;

                else
                    newConditions_Data{i,1}=cell2mat(Data);


                end

            end

            app.Conditions_DataAll{1,1}=cell2mat(newConditions_Data(:,1));
            app.Conditions_DataAll{1,2}=newTime;

            %Create list of all Conditions Trials
            [numTrialsperCondition, ~]=cellfun(@size, app.Conditions_Data(:,1), 'UniformOutput', false);
            app.numTrialsCondition=zeros(length(numTrialsperCondition),3);
            app.numTrialsCondition(:,1)=cell2mat(numTrialsperCondition);
            CStart=1;
            for i=1:length(app.numTrialsCondition(:,1)) %for each trial in each condition
                CEnd=CStart+app.numTrialsCondition(i,1)-1;
                app.numTrialsCondition(i,2)=CStart;
                app.numTrialsCondition(i,3)=CEnd;
                CStart=CEnd+1;
            end

            %Create LoadedConditions
            app.LoadedConditions=struct();
            app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix=app.UITable.Data;
            app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsData=app.Conditions_Data;
            app.LoadedConditions.ChTitles=app.Block.Titles;
            app.LoadedConditions.SampleRate=app.LoadedConditions;
            app.LoadedConditions.FileName=app.Filename;

            %Open Conditions List Box
            app.TrialSelectListBox.Visible=1; app.TrialSelectListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Visible=1; app.ConditionTrialsLabel.Visible=1;
            app.SelectAllCondButton.Visible=1;
            app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=1;
            app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Visible=1;
            app.ConditionsListBox.Visible=1; app.ConditionsListBoxLabel.Visible=1;
            app.ProcessButton.Enable=1; app.AnalyzeButton.Enable=1; app.OverrideButton.Enable=1; %Enable processing and analysis

            %Auto select first condition
            %Fill Conditions List box
            app.ConditionsListBox.Items=app.UITable.Data(:,2);          %List Group Names
            app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData=app.ConditionsListBox.Items;

            %check for duplicate names
            ItemsData=string(app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData);
            for i=1:length(ItemsData) %for each item
                Copies=ItemsData(i) == ItemsData; %check for copies
                if sum(Copies) ~= 1 %if there are copies,
                    CopyLoc=find(Copies==1); %find the locations of all copies
                    for e=2:length(CopyLoc)  %for each copy after the first
                        ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)) = strcat(ItemsData(CopyLoc(e)), num2str(e)); %rename the copy
                    end
                end

            end
            app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData=ItemsData;


            app.ConditionsListBox.Value=app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData(1); %Initial selection is the first condition
            ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1); %plot condition


        end

        % Button pushed function: LoadConditionsButton
        function LoadConditionsButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %As of 12/3/25 Disabled
            %Load in a saved Conditions file
            %Fill in the Conditions table with saved matrix
            %Apply new matrix to current loaded data, if there is no loaded
            %data, apply matrix to table only
            %==============================================================
            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile(app.Directory.SaveFolder,'multiSelect','on');
            figure(app.UIFigure)            %focus back to app
            if isequal(app.Filename,0)      %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=0;
            pause(0.1)

            %check if there is only one file
            if iscell(app.Filename) == 0
                TempFilename=app.Filename;
                app.Filename=cell(1,1);
                app.Filename{1,1}=TempFilename;
            end

            %load files
            for i=1:length(app.Filename)
                Conditions= load(fullfile(app.Path, app.Filename{1,i}));
                app.UITable.Data=[app.UITable.Data; Conditions.ConditionsMatrix];                  %Only load in matrix data

            end

            %Apply new matrix to current data
            if ~isempty(app.Block)  %if there is data loaded
                numConditions=length(app.UITable.Data(:,1));
                emptyRow=[];
                for i=1:numConditions %for each condition/row
                    TrialsToAdd=app.UITable.Data{i,1};

                    %Update Data
                    if str2double(app.UITable.Data{i,3}) > app.numChannels
                        app.ChannelSpinner.Value=app.numChannels;
                        app.UITable.Data{i,3}=num2str(app.numChannels);
                    else
                        app.ChannelSpinner.Value = str2double(app.UITable.Data{i,3});
                    end
                    ChannelSpinnerValueChanged(app,1);

                    %check for trial numbers outstide of range
                    MaxTrial=str2double(app.TrialSelectListBox.Items{end});
                    TrialsToAddDouble=str2double(split(TrialsToAdd,',')');
                    TrialsToAddDouble(TrialsToAddDouble > MaxTrial)=[];
                    if ~isempty(TrialsToAddDouble)
                        TrialsToAddNew=cellstr(string(TrialsToAddDouble));
                        TrialsToAdd=convert2commadel(TrialsToAddNew,',');

                        app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=split(TrialsToAdd,',')';      %select trials
                        TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                     %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                        app.ChangeCurrentRow=1;
                        app.CurrentRow=i;
                        AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                       %Add Trials
                        app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;
                    else
                        %if all trial numbers are out of range
                        app.UITable.Data{i,1}='';       %no trials
                        app.UITable.Data{i,3}='';       %no channel
                        app.Conditions_Data(i,:)=cell(1,3); %empty data
                        emptyRow=[emptyRow i];
                    end

                end %end update each condition row

                %check for empty rows
                if ~isempty(emptyRow)
                    e=0;
                    for i=emptyRow
                        %Remove empty rows
                        app.UITable.Data(i-e,:)=[];
                        app.Conditions_Data(i-e,:)=[];
                        e=e+1;
                    end
                end
            end

            pause(0.1)
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;


        end

        % Button pushed function: UploadExcelFileButton
        function UploadExcelFileButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Upload excel File that contains Conditions matrix
            %Column data is separated by * and all data is tab delimited
            %==============================================================

            %Import excel file
            [app.Filename, app.Path] = uigetfile('*.xlsx', 'Select Excel file',app.Directory.DataFolder);
            figure(app.UIFigure)            %focus back to app
            if isequal(app.Filename,0)      %if cancel is chosen return to app
                msgbox('No File Chosen');
                return
            end
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=0;
            pause(0.01);

            CurrentExcelFile=fullfile(app.Path,app.Filename);

            T=readcell(CurrentExcelFile,'Sheet','DefineCondition');

            %Base Name
            if length(T(:,1)) < 2 %the whole second row is empty
                BaseNameChar='';
            else %if there is at least one thing in the second row
                BaseNameList=T(2,:);
                Classes=string(cellfun(@class,BaseNameList,'UniformOutput',false)); %classes of cells
                BaseNameList=BaseNameList(Classes ~= "missing"); %remove empty spaces
                BaseNameChar=char(convert2commadel(BaseNameList,'_'))';
                BaseNameChar=reshape(BaseNameChar,1,[]); %reformat
                BaseNameChar=BaseNameChar(BaseNameChar ~= ' '); %remove spaces

            end

            SheetNamesAll=sheetnames(CurrentExcelFile);
            SheetNames=char(SheetNamesAll(2:end,:));

            %Select which sheet to apply
            if length(SheetNamesAll) > 2 %more one cond sheet, assume DefineConditons still present
                %Create figure with drop down for sheet selection
                %figure
                SheetNameDropDownFig=uifigure();
                SheetNameDropDownFig.Position(3:4)=[350 400];
                movegui(SheetNameDropDownFig,'center');
                %label
                lbl=uilabel(SheetNameDropDownFig,'Text','Select Sheet Name','FontWeight', 'bold');
                lbl.FontSize=16;
                lbl.Position=[100 375 150 20];
                %dropdown
                SheetNameDropDown=uidropdown(SheetNameDropDownFig);
                SheetNameDropDown.Position=[75 345 200 20];
                SheetNameDropDown.Items={'Select'};

                %Fill with sheet names
                Items=SheetNameDropDown.Items;
                for i=1:size(SheetNames,1)
                    Items{i+1}=SheetNames(i,:);
                end
                SheetNameDropDown.Items = Items;

                %button
                SheetNameButton=uibutton(SheetNameDropDownFig,'Text','Confirm','ButtonPushedFcn',@(btn,event) Confirm2ButtonPushed(SheetNameDropDownFig,app,SheetNameDropDown));
                SheetNameButton.Position=[125 315 100 20];

                %wait for selection to be made
                uiwait(SheetNameDropDownFig);
                app.SheetNameEditField.Value=app.SelectedSheet;
                WhichSheet=find(string(SheetNames) == string(app.SelectedSheet));
            else
                app.SheetNameEditField.Value=SheetNames(1,:);
                WhichSheet=1;
            end


            CurrSheet=SheetNames(WhichSheet,:);
            CurrSheet(CurrSheet == ' ')=[];

            CurrSheet_Data=readcell(CurrentExcelFile,'Sheet',CurrSheet);
            numRows=length(CurrSheet_Data(:,1));
            e=1;
            for i2=1:numRows
                %Group Name
                Row=string(CurrSheet_Data(i2,:));
                AstLoc=find(Row == "*");
                %Second asterisk denotes start of group name, third asterisk denotes end of group name
                GroupNameCell=CurrSheet_Data(i2,AstLoc(2)+1:AstLoc(3)-1);
                GroupName=convert2commadel(GroupNameCell,'_');

                %Check for double data type
                for i3=1:length(GroupName)
                    Value=GroupName{i3};
                    if ~ischar(Value)
                        Value2=num2str(Value);
                        GroupName{i3}=Value2;
                    end
                end

                GroupName=reshape(char(GroupName)',1,[]);
                GroupName(GroupName ==' ')=[];
                if isempty(BaseNameChar)
                    CondTable{e,2}=GroupName;
                else
                    CondTable{e,2}=[BaseNameChar '_' GroupName];
                end
                app.ConditionGroupNames{e,1}=GroupName;

                %Trials to add
                TrialstoAdd=CurrSheet_Data(i2,AstLoc(1)+1:AstLoc(2)-1);
                for i4=1:length(TrialstoAdd)
                    if ismissing(TrialstoAdd{1,i4})
                        TrialstoAdd{1,i4}='';
                    end
                    TrialstoAdd{1,i4}=num2str(TrialstoAdd{1,i4}); %convert numbers to characters
                end
                %remove empty cells
                Empty=cellfun(@isempty, TrialstoAdd);
                TrialstoAdd=TrialstoAdd(~Empty);

                TrialstoAdd=convert2commadel(TrialstoAdd,',');
                CondTable{e,1}=TrialstoAdd;

                %Channel number
                ChannelNum=CurrSheet_Data(i2,AstLoc(3)+1);
                CondTable{e,3}=num2str(ChannelNum{1,1});

                e=e+1;
            end %end for numRows


            %Define data organization for conditions loaded
            %figure
            EpochTypeFig=uifigure();
            EpochTypeFig.Position(3:4)=[1337 400];
            movegui(EpochTypeFig,'center');

            %Header
            Header1=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Condition', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header1.Position=[20 365 300 50];
            Header2=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Epoch Type', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header2.Position=[332 365 100 50];
            Header3=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Pre (ms)', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header3.Position=[440 365 100 50];
            Header4=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Post (ms)', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header4.Position=[502 365 100 50];
            Header5=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Comment Text', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header5.Position=[570 365 100 50];
            Header6=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Detection Settings ', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header6.Position=[698 365 200 50];
            Header7=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Event Channel', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header7.Position=[840 365 100 50];
            Header8=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Event Threshold (mV)', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header8.Position=[948 365 150 50];
            Header9=uilabel(EpochTypeFig, 'Text', 'Event ISI (s)', 'FontWeight', 'bold');
            Header9.Position=[1089 365 100 50];

            %Epoch type panel
            Pan=uipanel(EpochTypeFig);
            Pan.Position=[10 40 1180 340];
            nRows=length(app.ConditionGroupNames);
            GridL=uigridlayout(Pan, [nRows, 9],'Scrollable','on');
            GridL.ColumnWidth = {300, 100, 52, 52, 100, 150, 100, 130, 75};
            GridL.RowHeight = repmat({'fit'}, 1, nRows);

            EpochTypeInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            PreDurInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            PostDurInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            CommentTextInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            DetectSetInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            EventChInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            EventThreshInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);
            EventISIInputs = gobjects(nRows,1);

            %first column: conditions
            for i=1:nRows
                %Group name
                CondList=uilabel(GridL);
                CondList.Text=app.ConditionGroupNames{i,1};
                CondList.Layout.Row = i; CondList.Layout.Column = 1;

                %Epoch Type dropdown
                EpochTypeDropDown=uidropdown(GridL);
                EpochTypeDropDown.Items={'Blocks','Comments','Events'};
                EpochTypeDropDown.Value=app.EpochTypesButtonGroup.SelectedObject.Text;          %Auto set to what is in the "Data Organization" tab
                EpochTypeDropDown.Layout.Row = i; EpochTypeDropDown.Layout.Column = 2;
                EpochTypeInputs(i)=EpochTypeDropDown;

                %Check with epoch type is used for pre and post values, for default values
                if EpochTypeDropDown.Value == "Blocks"
                    PreValue=app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value;
                    PostValue=app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value;
                else %Comments or events
                    PreValue=app.PreDurationsEditField.Value;
                    PostValue=app.PostDurationsEditField.Value;
                end

                %PreDur
                PreDurEditField=uieditfield(GridL,'numeric','Value',PreValue,'Limits',[0 Inf]);  %Auto set to what is in the "Data Organization" tab
                PreDurEditField.Layout.Row = i; PreDurEditField.Layout.Column = 3;
                PreDurInputs(i)=PreDurEditField;

                %PostDur
                PostDurEditField=uieditfield(GridL,'numeric','Value',PostValue,'Limits',[0 Inf]); %Auto set to what is in the "Data Organization" tab
                PostDurEditField.Layout.Row = i; PostDurEditField.Layout.Column = 4;
                PostDurInputs(i)=PostDurEditField;

                %Comment Text
                CommentTextDropDown=uidropdown(GridL);
                if app.ComList == ""
                    CommentTextDropDown.Items={''};
                else
                    ComListCell=cell(1,size(app.ComList,1));
                    for ComLF=1:size(app.ComList,1)
                        ComListCell{ComLF}=char(app.ComList(ComLF,:));
                    end
                    CommentTextDropDown.Items=ComListCell;
                end
                if EpochTypeDropDown.Value == "Comments"
                    CommentTextDropDown.Value=app.EventNameListBox.Value;
                end
                CommentTextDropDown.Layout.Row = i; CommentTextDropDown.Layout.Column = 5;
                CommentTextDropDown.Visible=0;
                CommentTextInputs(i)=CommentTextDropDown;


                %Event Detection Settings
                DetectSetDropDown=uidropdown(GridL);
                DetectSetDropDown.Items={'Threshold Crossing'}; %!!! add callback to change following parameters based on future detection setting methods
                DetectSetDropDown.Layout.Row = i; DetectSetDropDown.Layout.Column = 6;
                DetectSetDropDown.Visible=0;
                DetectSetInputs(i)=DetectSetDropDown;


                %Event Channel
                EventChEditField=uieditfield(GridL,'numeric','Value',app.EventChannelEditField.Value,'Limits',[1 app.numChannels]);
                EventChEditField.Layout.Row = i; EventChEditField.Layout.Column = 7;
                EventChEditField.Visible=0;
                EventChInputs(i)=EventChEditField;


                %Event Threshold
                EventThreshEditField=uieditfield(GridL,'numeric','Value',app.ThresholdmVEditField.Value,'Limits',[-Inf Inf]);
                EventThreshEditField.Layout.Row = i; EventThreshEditField.Layout.Column = 8;
                EventThreshEditField.Visible=0;

                EventThreshInputs(i)=EventThreshEditField;

                %Event ISI
                EventISIEditField=uieditfield(GridL,'numeric','Value',app.SearchISIsEditField.Value,'Limits',[0 Inf]);
                EventISIEditField.Layout.Row = i; EventISIEditField.Layout.Column = 9;
                EventISIEditField.Visible=0;
                EventISIInputs(i)=EventISIEditField;



            end

            %Consolidate graphic objects and headers
            AllInputs=[EpochTypeInputs,CommentTextInputs,DetectSetInputs,EventChInputs,EventThreshInputs,EventISIInputs,PreDurInputs,PostDurInputs];

            %add callback function for Epoch Type Dropdown
            for i=1:length(AllInputs(:,1))
                AllInputs(i,1).ValueChangedFcn=@(src,ev) EpochTypeChanged(i,AllInputs);
                EpochTypeChanged(i,AllInputs)
            end

            app.AllInputsCell=[];
            %add confirm button
            EpochTypeButton=uibutton(EpochTypeFig,'Text','Confirm','ButtonPushedFcn',@(btn,event) Confirm3ButtonButtonPushed(AllInputs,app.ConditionGroupNames));
            EpochTypeButton.Position=[332 10 100 20];

            uiwait(EpochTypeFig);

            figure(app.UIFigure);
            if isempty(app.AllInputsCell)
                msgbox('Canceled Action')
                app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;
                return;
            end

            app.UITable.Data=[app.UITable.Data; CondTable];

            %Apply to Data
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1; %Disable loading indicator for Find Events

            %number of current conditions in table
            if length(app.Conditions_Data(:,1)) == 1 &&  isempty(app.Conditions_Data{1}) %Cleared table
                CurrentRowinTable=1;
            else
                CurrentRowinTable=length(app.Conditions_Data(:,1))+1;
            end
            for i=1:length(app.ConditionGroupNames)

                CurrRow=app.AllInputsCell(i,:);
                CurrEpochType=CurrRow{2};

                switch CurrEpochType
                    case 'Block'
                        %Update Epoch Types display
                        app.BlocksButton.Value=1; app.EpochType=1;
                        EpochTypesButtonGroupSelectionChanged(app,1);
                        %set pre/post trigger time
                        app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value=CurrRow{3};
                        app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value=CurrRow{4};
                        PreTriggermsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);
                        PostTriggermsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);

                    case 'Comments'
                        %Update Epoch Types display
                        app.CommentsButton.Value=1; app.EpochType=2;
                        EpochTypesButtonGroupSelectionChanged(app,1);
                        FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);

                        %Set Comment Text
                        app.EventNameListBox.Value=string(CurrRow{5}); %select chosen comment text
                        EventNameListBoxValueChanged(app,1);

                        %Set pre/post duration
                        app.PreDurationsEditField.Value=CurrRow{3};
                        app.PostDurationsEditField.Value=CurrRow{4};
                        PreDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);
                        PostDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);

                    case 'Events'
                        %Update Epoch Types display
                        app.EventsButton.Value=1; app.EpochType=3;
                        EpochTypesButtonGroupSelectionChanged(app,1);

                        %Set Event Detection Settings and parameters
                        app.DetectionSettingsDropDown.Value=CurrRow{6};
                        app.EventChannelEditField.Value=CurrRow{7};
                        app.ThresholdmVEditField.Value=CurrRow{8};
                        app.SearchISIsEditField.Value=CurrRow{9};
                        DetectionSettingsDropDownValueChanged(app,1);
                        EventChannelEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);
                        ThresholdmVEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);
                        SearchISIsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);

                        %Set pre/post duration
                        app.PreDurationsEditField.Value=CurrRow{3};
                        app.PostDurationsEditField.Value=CurrRow{4};
                        PreDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);
                        PostDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app,1);

                end

                %Apply setttings
                app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1;
                FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
                app.ChannelSpinner.Value = str2double(app.UITable.Data{CurrentRowinTable,3});
                ChannelSpinnerValueChanged(app, 1);

                %Update Data and add select trials
                NewTrialList=app.UITable.Data{CurrentRowinTable,1};
                NewTrials=split(NewTrialList,',');
                if any(~ismember(string(NewTrials),string(app.TrialSelectListBox.Items))) %if there are any values that aren't in the list
                    msgbox('Error: There are trials in excel that are not in data');
                    app.UITable.Data = [];
                    app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;
                    return;
                end

                app.TrialSelectListBox.Value=split(NewTrialList,',');      %select the new trials
                TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);                     %plot and update AllPlotted_Data
                app.ChangeCurrentRow=1;
                app.CurrentRow=CurrentRowinTable;
                AddSelectedTrialsButtonPushed(app,1)                       %Add Trials
                app.ChangeCurrentRow=0;

                CurrentRowinTable=CurrentRowinTable+1;

            end

            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;
            pause(0.01);
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1;


            %callback functions============================================
            function Confirm2ButtonPushed(SheetNameDropDownFig,app,SheetNameDropDown)
                app.SelectedSheet=SheetNameDropDown.Value;
                close(SheetNameDropDownFig);
            end

            function Confirm3ButtonButtonPushed(AllInputs,ConditionGroupNames)
                nRows=length(AllInputs(:,1));

                %Format parameters to cell
                app.AllInputsCell=cell(nRows,9);
                for w=1:nRows
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,1}=ConditionGroupNames{w};   %Condition
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,2}=AllInputs(w,1).Value;     %Epoch Type
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,3}=AllInputs(w,7).Value;  %Pred Dur
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,4}=AllInputs(w,8).Value; %Post Dur
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,5}=AllInputs(w,2).Value;     %Comment text
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,6}=AllInputs(w,3).Value;     %Detection settings
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,7}=AllInputs(w,4).Value;     %Event Channel
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,8}=AllInputs(w,5).Value;     %Event Threshold
                    app.AllInputsCell{w,9}=AllInputs(w,6).Value;     %Event ISI

                end

                close(EpochTypeFig);

            end

            function EpochTypeChanged(e,AllInputs)
                %For reference: AllInputs=[EpochTypeInputs,CommentTextInputs,DetectSetInputs,EventChInputs,EventThreshInputs,EventISIInputs,PreDurInputs,PostDurInputs];
                CurrEpochType=AllInputs(e,1).Value;
                switch CurrEpochType
                    case 'Comments'
                        AllInputs(e,2).Visible=1;      %make Commment text dropdown visible

                        %Hide event headers and parameters
                        AllInputs(e,3).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,4).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,5).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,6).Visible=0;

                    case 'Events'
                        %Display event headers and parameters
                        AllInputs(e,3).Visible=1;
                        AllInputs(e,4).Visible=1;
                        AllInputs(e,5).Visible=1;
                        AllInputs(e,6).Visible=1;

                        %Hide Comment text header and parameter
                        AllInputs(e,2).Visible=0;
                    case 'Block'
                        %Hide Comment text header and parameter
                        AllInputs(e,2).Visible=0;

                        %Hide event headers and parameters
                        AllInputs(e,3).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,4).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,5).Visible=0;
                        AllInputs(e,6).Visible=0;
                end
            end

        end

        % Value changed function: ConditionsListBox
        function ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates graph by plotting all trials in newly selected
            %condition(s) in Conditions List Box
            %==============================================================
            %tic
            app.SelectedCondition = app.ConditionsListBox.Value;

            %For each condition, plot all trials
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items={''};
            app.Analyze_ConditionsUsed=[];
            ValueIndex=matches(string(app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData),app.SelectedCondition);
            ConditionIndex=find(ValueIndex);
            app.Analyze_ConditionsUsed=ConditionIndex;
            %update trial select list box for Conditions (ConditionTrialsListBox)
            TrialRange=nan(length(ConditionIndex),max(app.numTrialsCondition(ConditionIndex,1)));
            for i2=1:length(ConditionIndex)
                Range=app.numTrialsCondition(ConditionIndex(i2),2):app.numTrialsCondition(ConditionIndex(i2),3);
                TrialRange(i2,1:length(Range))=Range;
            end
            TrialRange=reshape(TrialRange',1,[]);
            TrialRange(isnan(TrialRange))=[];
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items=mat2cell(num2str(TrialRange'),ones(1,length(TrialRange)));
            %c=toc

            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 0
                %select all trials of selected conditions
                SelectAllCondButtonPushed(app,1);
            else
                %plot averaged data
                PlotData=app.AvgConditionData(ConditionIndex,:);

                %AllPlotted_Data
                app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(length(app.AvgConditionData(:,1)),2);
                app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
                app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1)=mat2cell(app.AvgConditionData,ones(1,length(app.AvgConditionData(:,1))),length(app.AvgConditionData(1,:)));

                %convert to selected units
                app.Plotted_Data=PlotData*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

                %Plot desired data
                cla(app.UIAxes);
                hold(app.UIAxes,'On');
                plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data); %,'Color','k');
                app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
                app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
                %update Scaling edit fields
                app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
                app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
                app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
                app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

                hold(app.UIAxes,'Off');

            end

            %CondTime=toc %time to plot conditions

        end

        % Value changed function: ConditionTrialsListBox
        function ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates which trials are selected and plotted
            %==============================================================

            %Determine which trial is selected
            TrialSelectedList= app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value;
            if length(app.SelectedCondition) == 1
                if isempty(app.LoadedConditions.ChTitles) %for if there are no titles
                    app.UIAxes.Title.String=" ";
                else
                    ValueIndex=string(app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData) == string(app.SelectedCondition);
                    app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.LoadedConditions.ChTitles(str2double(app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix{ValueIndex,3}));
                end
            else
                app.UIAxes.Title.String="Multiple Conditions";
            end

            %Disable Analyze Arrows if needed
            if length(TrialSelectedList) == 1 %if only one trial is selected
                if string(TrialSelectedList) == string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items{end})
                    app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                elseif string(TrialSelectedList) == string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items{1})
                    app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                else 
                    app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                end 
            end


            %Determine which data to plot depending on processing
            if app.Processed==1
                app.Time=app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{2};
                Data=app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{1,1};
            else
                app.Time=app.Conditions_DataAll{2};
                Data=app.Conditions_DataAll{1,1};
            end

            %AllPlotted_Data updated
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value ==0
                app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(length(Data(:,1)),2);
                Trials=cell2mat(cellfun(@str2double,TrialSelectedList,'UniformOutput',false));
                PlotData=Data';
                PlotData=PlotData(:,Trials);
                app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
                app.AllPlotted_Data(Trials,1)=mat2cell(Data(Trials,:),ones(1,length(Data(Trials,1))), length(Data(1,:)));
            else

                app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(length(app.AvgConditionData(:,1)),2);
                app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;
                app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1)=mat2cell(app.AvgConditionData,ones(1,length(app.AvgConditionData(:,1))),length(app.AvgConditionData(1,:)));
                PlotData=app.AvgConditionData;
            end

            %convert to selected units
            app.Plotted_Data=PlotData*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;

            %Plot desired data
            cla(app.UIAxes);
            hold(app.UIAxes,'On');
            plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data); %,'Color','k');
            app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
            app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
            %update Scaling edit fields
            app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
            app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
            app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

            hold(app.UIAxes,'Off');


        end

        % Value changed function: AnalysisOptionDropDown
        function AnalysisOptionDropDownValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update Analysis Panel for the selected analysis option
            %==============================================================
            SelectedAnalysisOption = app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.Value;

            %Change panel title
            switch SelectedAnalysisOption
                case app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{1} %MEP
                    app.MotorEvokedPotentialPanel.Title = 'Motor Evoked Potential';
                case app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{2} %SP
                    app.MotorEvokedPotentialPanel.Title = 'Silent Period';
                case app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{3} %EMG Analysis
                    %Not implemented 12/3/25 !!!
                    app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.Value=app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{1};
                    AnalysisOptionDropDownValueChanged(app,1);
            end

        end

        % Value changed function: OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown
        function OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDownValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Updates Analysis Panel based on which onset/offset detection
            %method is selected
            %==============================================================
            SelectedMethod = app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value;

            %Update which items are visible
            switch SelectedMethod

                case app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items{2} %Garvey et al
                    app.OnsetSDEditField.Visible=0;  app.SDEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;
                    app.OffsetSDEditField.Visible=0; app.SDEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=0;
                    app.MCDConstantEditField.Visible=1; app.MCDConstantEditFieldLabel.Visible=1;

                    app.StartTimemsEditField.Visible=1; app.StartTimemsEditFieldLabel.Visible=1; 
                    app.OnsetLabel.Visible=1; app.OffsetLabel.Visible=1;
                    app.OnsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=1; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel.Visible=1; 
                    app.OffsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=1; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=1;
                    app.FiftyPercentOnsetCheckBox.Visible=1; app.FiftyPercentOffsetCheckBox.Visible=1;


                case app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items{1} %SD Limits
                    app.OnsetSDEditField.Visible=1;  app.SDEditFieldLabel.Visible=1;
                    app.OffsetSDEditField.Visible=1; app.SDEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=1;
                    app.MCDConstantEditField.Visible=0; app.MCDConstantEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;

                    app.StartTimemsEditField.Visible=1; app.StartTimemsEditFieldLabel.Visible=1; 
                    app.OnsetLabel.Visible=1; app.OffsetLabel.Visible=1;
                    app.OnsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=1; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel.Visible=1; 
                    app.OffsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=1; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=1;
                    app.FiftyPercentOnsetCheckBox.Visible=1; app.FiftyPercentOffsetCheckBox.Visible=1;

                otherwise
                    app.OnsetSDEditField.Visible=0;  app.SDEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;
                    app.OffsetSDEditField.Visible=0; app.SDEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=0;
                    app.MCDConstantEditField.Visible=0; app.MCDConstantEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;
                    app.MCDConstantEditField.Visible=0; app.MCDConstantEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;
                    
                    app.StartTimemsEditField.Visible=0; app.StartTimemsEditFieldLabel.Visible=0; 
                    app.OnsetLabel.Visible=0; app.OffsetLabel.Visible=0;
                    app.OnsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=0; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel.Visible=0;
                    app.OffsetMinDurmsEditField.Visible=0; app.MinDurmsEditFieldLabel_2.Visible=0;
                    app.FiftyPercentOnsetCheckBox.Visible=0; app.FiftyPercentOffsetCheckBox.Visible=0;

            end

            %If the custom pop-up figure exists, delete it
            if (string(class(app.CustomAnalysisOpts)) ~= "double") && (string(class(app.CustomAnalysisOpts)) == "matlab.ui.Figure" && ishghandle(app.CustomAnalysisOpts))
                delete(app.CustomAnalysisOpts);
            end


        end

        % Button pushed function: AnalyzeButton
        function AnalyzeButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Determine onset and offset via Onset/Offset Detection method
            %Calculates the pre-stim RMS and STD and the results of the
            %analysis option chosen
            %==============================================================

            if (string(class(event)) == "matlab.ui.eventdata.ButtonPushedData") && (event ~= 2) %don't use loading screen when using analyze arrows
            loadingScreen(app,1);
            end
            %tic

            if app.OverrideUsed == 0
                %remove existing lines
                if ishghandle(app.OnsetLine) == 1
                    delete(app.OnsetLine);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.OffsetLine) == 1
                    delete(app.OffsetLine);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.PreStimArea) == 1
                    delete(app.PreStimArea);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.StartLine) == 1
                    delete(app.StartLine);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.OnsetLimitLines) == 1
                    delete(app.OnsetLimitLines);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.OffsetLimitLines) == 1
                    delete(app.OffsetLimitLines);
                end
                if ishghandle(app.meanPreStimLine) == 1
                    delete(app.meanPreStimLine);
                end
            end


            %check if the averages are being used
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 0
                %Condense Trials
                TrialsLoc=cellfun(@isempty, app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1));
                app.Analyze_TrialsUsed=find(~TrialsLoc);
                SelectedTrialsData=app.AllPlotted_Data(~TrialsLoc,1); %in volts

                app.Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName=cell(1,1);
                app.Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel=cell(1,1);

            else
                %average is taken of all trials in each condition, list which conditions were used
                SelectedConditionsData=find(matches(string(app.ConditionsListBox.ItemsData),app.SelectedCondition));
                app.Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName=app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix(SelectedConditionsData,2);
                app.Analyze_TrialsUsed=app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix(SelectedConditionsData,1);
                app.Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel=app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix(SelectedConditionsData,3);
                SelectedTrialsData(:,1)=mat2cell(app.AvgConditionData(SelectedConditionsData,:),ones(length(SelectedConditionsData),1),length(app.AvgConditionData(1,:)));

            end

            %Calculations for Pre Stim Period
            %check that prestim period is within the data range
            MinMaxTime=zeros(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)),2);
            %find time range where there is data in all trials (for trials with different time lengths)
            for i=1:length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1))
                DataStartEnd=find(~isnan(SelectedTrialsData{i,1}));
                MinMaxTime(i,1)=app.Time(DataStartEnd(1));
                MinMaxTime(i,2)=app.Time(DataStartEnd(end));
            end
            MinTimeLimit=max(MinMaxTime(:,1)); %start data time (s)
            MaxTimeLimit=min(MinMaxTime(:,2)); %end data time (s)
            if app.PreStimStart/1000 < MinTimeLimit && app.PreStimEnd/1000 < MinTimeLimit %if both the prestim start and end are below the min start time
                app.PreStimStart=MinTimeLimit*1000;
                app.PreStimEnd=(MinTimeLimit+0.01)*1000; %10ms 
                app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value=app.PreStimStart;
                app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value=app.PreStimEnd;

            elseif app.PreStimStart/1000 < MinTimeLimit %if the start time is below the min start time
                app.PreStimStart=MinTimeLimit*1000;
                app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value=app.PreStimStart;

                %if the start is above the min time limit the end should be too, so no check for that

            elseif app.PreStimEnd/1000 > MaxTimeLimit %if the End time is above the max time limit
                app.PreStimEnd=app.PreStimStart+10;
                app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value=app.PreStimEnd;
            end
            %check if the prestim values are the same
            if app.PreStimStart == app.PreStimEnd
                app.PreStimEnd=app.PreStimStart+10;
                app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value=app.PreStimEnd;
            end


            %calculate Pre Stim RMS and STDV
            PreStimRangeTime=app.Time(single(app.Time) >= single(app.PreStimStart/1000) & single(app.Time) <= single(app.PreStimEnd/1000));
            PreStimData=zeros(length(PreStimRangeTime),length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)));
            for i=1:length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)) %for each trial
                TrialData=SelectedTrialsData{i,1}; %data
                PreStimData(:,i)=TrialData(single(app.Time) >= single(app.PreStimStart/1000) & single(app.Time) <= single(app.PreStimEnd/1000))';
            end

            %RMS and STDV
            app.PreStimRMS=rms(PreStimData,1); %take RMS of each trial
            app.PreStimSTDV=std(PreStimData); %find the standard deviation for each trial
            app.PreStimMean=mean(abs(PreStimData));


            %Display results
            app.PreStimMeanEditField.Value=mean(app.PreStimMean(~isnan(app.PreStimMean)))*1000;  %convert to mV
            app.PreStimStdEditField.Value=mean(app.PreStimSTDV(~isnan(app.PreStimRMS)))*1000; %convert to mV

            %plot area of preStim
            PreStimStartSec=app.PreStimStart/1000;
            PreStimEndSec=app.PreStimEnd/1000;
            x=[PreStimStartSec PreStimStartSec PreStimEndSec PreStimEndSec];
            y=[app.UIAxes.YLim(1) app.UIAxes.YLim(2) app.UIAxes.YLim(2) app.UIAxes.YLim(1)];
            hold(app.UIAxes,'on');
            app.PreStimArea=patch(app.UIAxes,x,y,'b','FaceColor','#969590','EdgeColor','black','FaceAlpha',0.2); %plot prestim area
            
            %check units for plotting
            switch app.UnitsDropDown.Value
                case 'mV'
                    Units=1;
                    UnitsV=1000;
                case 'V'
                    Units=0.001;
                    UnitsV=1;
                case 'uV'
                    Units=1000;
                    UnitsV=1e6;
            end
            %app.Annot(1)=text(app.UIAxes,x(1)+0.001,y(2)-0.1*Units,'Pre Stim Area');

            %% Onset/Offset
            %check which analysis option and set initial variables
            switch app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.Value
                case app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{1} %MEP
                    app.MEP_Amp=zeros(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)),1);
                    app.MEP_Area=zeros(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)),1);
                    AnalyzeMethod="MEP";


                case app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.ItemsData{2} %SP
                    app.SP_PercDecrease=zeros(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)),1);
                    app.SP_Area=zeros(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)),1);
                    AnalyzeMethod="SP";
            end

            if ~isempty(app.ProcessOrder) && any(string(app.ProcessOrder(:,1)) == "Down Sample")
                AnalyzeSampleRate=app.DownSampledRate;
            else
                AnalyzeSampleRate=app.AllSampleRate;
            end

            %if override isn't use
            if app.OverrideUsed == 0
                %Check onset/offset method
                switch app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value
                    case app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items{2} %Garvey
                        [app.AllOnOffsetTime, OnsetLimit, OffsetLimit,meanPreStimData]=GarveyMethod(app,AnalyzeSampleRate,PreStimData,SelectedTrialsData,AnalyzeMethod,'MCD',app.FiftyPercentOnsetCheckBox.Value,app.FiftyPercentOffsetCheckBox.Value);

                    case app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Items{1} %SD Limits
                        [app.AllOnOffsetTime, OnsetLimit, OffsetLimit,meanPreStimData]=GarveyMethod(app,AnalyzeSampleRate,PreStimData,SelectedTrialsData,AnalyzeMethod,'SD',app.FiftyPercentOnsetCheckBox.Value,app.FiftyPercentOffsetCheckBox.Value);
                    
                    otherwise
                        MethodName=app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value; PluginsFolderName=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
                        %if the Custom Opts figure doesn't exist, create it
                        if (string(class(app.CustomAnalysisOpts)) ~= "double") && (string(class(app.CustomAnalysisOpts)) == "matlab.ui.Figure" && ishghandle(app.CustomAnalysisOpts))
                            existFig=1;
                        else
                            app.CustomAnalysisOpts=uifigure('Name','Custom Analysis');
                            existFig=0;
                        end
                        [app.AllOnOffsetTime, OnsetLimit, OffsetLimit,meanPreStimData,app.CustomAnalysisOpts]= ... 
                            eval([MethodName '(app,existFig,PluginsFolderName,AnalyzeSampleRate,PreStimData,SelectedTrialsData);']);

                        %check for empty return values
                        numSelectedTrials=length(SelectedTrialsData);
                        if isempty(app.AllOnOffsetTime)
                            app.AllOnOffsetTime = nan(numSelectedTrials,2);
                        end
                        if isempty(OnsetLimit)
                            OnsetLimit = nan(1,numSelectedTrials);
                        end
                        if isempty(OffsetLimit)
                            OffsetLimit = nan(1,numSelectedTrials);
                        end


                end
            else %override is used
                %Set legend
                legend(app.UIAxes,[app.PreStimArea app.OnsetLine app.OffsetLine],{'Pre Stim Area','Onset','Offset'},'AutoUpdate','off');

                %Onset and offset times
                OnsetTime=app.AllOnOffsetTime(1,1);
                OffsetTime=app.AllOnOffsetTime(1,2);

                %check times, change so on/offset times are the closest time value
                DiffTimeOnset=abs(app.Time-OnsetTime); %get the differences between the selected Onset time and time
                minDiffTimeOnset=find(DiffTimeOnset == min(DiffTimeOnset)); %find the smallest difference
                OnsetTime=app.Time(minDiffTimeOnset); %use the location of the smallest difference to determine time 

                DiffTimeOffset=abs(app.Time-OffsetTime);
                minDiffTimeOffset=find(DiffTimeOffset == min(DiffTimeOffset));
                OffsetTime=app.Time(minDiffTimeOffset);

                app.AllOnOffsetTime(1,1)=OnsetTime;
                app.AllOnOffsetTime(1,2)=OffsetTime;

                Tol=eps("double");
                Start=find(abs(app.Time - OnsetTime) < Tol); %frames
                End=find(abs(app.Time - OffsetTime) < Tol);

                if End < Start %if the offset line is placed before the onset line
                    End=Start+round(0.01*AnalyzeSampleRate); %whatever the start was placed at plus ~0.01 seconds, this is just so the code runs after this, it is assumed that the user do the override again
                    app.AllOnOffsetTime(1,2)=End/AnalyzeSampleRate;
                    msgbox('Warning: Offset was placed after Onset');
                end

            end

            %For each trial calculate the analysis results
            MissingAnalyze=0;
            for i=1:length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)) %for each trial
                if app.OverrideUsed == 0 %Override is not used
                    OnsetTime=app.AllOnOffsetTime(i,1);
                    OffsetTime=app.AllOnOffsetTime(i,2);

                    Tol=eps("double");
                    Start=find(abs(app.Time - OnsetTime) < Tol);
                    End=find(abs(app.Time - OffsetTime) < Tol);

                end

                %For MEP data use non-rectified data if needed
                if ~isempty(app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{3}) && AnalyzeMethod=='MEP' %if the third cell isn't empty then the non-rectified data was saved and if MEP is done, use the non-rectified data
                    if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 1
                        TrialDataNR=app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{3}(1,:);
                    else
                        TrialDataNR=app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{3}(app.Analyze_TrialsUsed(i),:);
                    end
                else
                    TrialDataNR=SelectedTrialsData{i,1};
                end

                %if there is an onset/offset
                if ~isnan(OnsetTime) && ~isnan(OffsetTime)
                    %Data range
                    AnalyzeData=TrialDataNR(Start:End); %calculate for non-rectified data

                    %Calculate Results
                    switch AnalyzeMethod
                        case "MEP"
                            %Calculate amplitude and area
                            if ~isempty(app.ProcessOrder) && any(string(app.ProcessOrder(:,1)) == "Rectify") && isempty(app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{3})
                                app.MEP_Amp=nan;
                            else
                                Peak=max(AnalyzeData); 
                                Trough=min(AnalyzeData); 
                                app.MEP_Amp(i,:)=Peak-Trough;
                            end
                            app.MEP_Area(i,:)=trapz(abs(AnalyzeData)); %trapezoidal integration method, for MEP area should be of absolute value of data
                        case "SP"
                            app.SP_PercDecrease(i,:)=100-(mean(AnalyzeData)/mean(PreStimData(:,i))*100); % percent of silent period mean value decreased compared with pre stimulation period

                            %Normalized area of SP
                            SPArea=trapz(AnalyzeData); %trapezoidal integration method
                            %mean preStimulation times length of silent period duration equals the area
                            areaMeanSP = abs(mean(PreStimData(:,i))*length(AnalyzeData));
                            app.SP_Area(i,:) = (1-SPArea/areaMeanSP)*100;      %normalized area of slient period unit
                    end


                else %if not fill with nan
                    switch AnalyzeMethod
                        case "MEP"
                            app.MEP_Amp(i,:)=nan;
                            app.MEP_Area(i,:)=nan;
                        case "SP"
                            app.SP_PercDecrease(i,:)=nan;
                            app.SP_Area(i,:)=nan;
                    end

                    MissingAnalyze=MissingAnalyze+1;
                end

                %If there is only one trial print results
                if length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)) == 1
                    app.OnsetmsValueLabel.Text=num2str(app.Time(Start)*1000); %convert to ms
                    app.DurationmsValueLabel.Text=num2str((app.Time(End)-app.Time(Start))*1000); %convert to ms

                    switch AnalyzeMethod
                        case "MEP"
                            app.DecreaseValueLabel.Text=num2str(round(app.MEP_Amp(1)*1000,2)); %convert to mV for display
                            app.AmplitudemVLabel.Text='Amplitude (mV)';
                            app.AreaValueLabel.Text=num2str(round(app.MEP_Area(1)*1000,2));

                        case "SP"
                            app.DecreaseValueLabel.Text=num2str(round(app.SP_PercDecrease(1),2));
                            app.AmplitudemVLabel.Text='% Decrease';
                            app.AreaValueLabel.Text=num2str(round(app.SP_Area(1),2));
                    end

                else
                    app.DecreaseValueLabel.Text='N/A';
                    app.AmplitudemVLabel.Text='N/A';
                    app.AreaValueLabel.Text='N/A';

                    app.OnsetmsValueLabel.Text='N/A'; %convert to ms
                    app.DurationmsValueLabel.Text='N/A'; %convert to ms


                end

                %ConditionList and ChannelList for saving Analyze results
                if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 0
                    TrialUsed=app.Analyze_TrialsUsed(i);
                    for i2=app.Analyze_ConditionsUsed 
                        inRange=TrialUsed >= app.numTrialsCondition(i2,2) && TrialUsed <= app.numTrialsCondition(i2,3); %if the trial is in the range of the condition trial numbers,
                        if inRange==1                                                                                   %add condition name to list
                            app.Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName{i,1}=app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix(i2,2);
                            app.Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel(i,1)=app.LoadedConditions.ConditionsMatrix(i2,3);
                            break;                                                                                      %there should only be one condition name per trial, so break search if the name is found
                        end
                    end

                end
            end %end for each trial

            %Plot Onset and Offset Lines
            if app.OverrideUsed == 0
                %plotting
                OnsetLimitsV=OnsetLimit*UnitsV; %convert to Unit
                OffsetLimitsV=OffsetLimit*UnitsV;
                if  all(all(isnan(app.AllOnOffsetTime)))  %no onset/offset found for all trials 
                    %Plot start line and onset/offset thresholds
                    app.StartLine=xline(app.UIAxes,app.StartTimemsEditField.Value*0.001,'k','LineStyle','-','LineWidth',2);
                    %Adjust negation of thresholds based on if the data is rectified or not %!!!
                    MedianOnsetThresh=median(OnsetLimitsV(~isnan(OnsetLimitsV)));
                    MedianOffsetThresh=median(OffsetLimitsV(~isnan(OffsetLimitsV)));
                    plotThresholdLinesMainGraph(app,AnalyzeMethod,MedianOnsetThresh,MedianOffsetThresh);
                    meanPreStimDataV=meanPreStimData*UnitsV; %convert to units
                    app.meanPreStimLine=yline(app.UIAxes,median(meanPreStimDataV(~isnan(meanPreStimDataV))),'k--');

                    %Pop-out figures for if no onset/offset found
                    if string(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitch.Value) == "On" 
                        if string(class(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig)) ~= "matlab.ui.Figure"
                            app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig=figure();
                        else
                            if ~isgraphics(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig,'Figure')
                                app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig=figure();
                            else
                                clf(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig);
                            end
                        end
                        ax5=axes(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig);
                        %rectify data for pop-out plot
                        for i=1:length(app.UIAxes.Children)
                            a=string(class(app.UIAxes.Children(i)));
                            b1=strsplit(a,'.');
                            ChildrenClass=b1(end);

                            if ChildrenClass == "Line"
                                CurrentXData(i,:)=app.UIAxes.Children(i).XData;
                                CurrentYData(i,:)=abs(app.UIAxes.Children(i).YData);

                            end
                        end
                        plot(ax5,CurrentXData',CurrentYData'); hold on;
                        %boxplot for thresholds
                        g1=repmat({'OnsetLimits'},length(OnsetLimitsV),1);
                        g2=repmat({'OffsetLimits'},length(OffsetLimitsV),1);
                        g=[g1;g2];

                        Bx2=boxplot(ax5,[OnsetLimitsV';OffsetLimitsV'],g,"Orientation","vertical","Positions",[app.StartTimemsEditField.Value*0.001 app.StartTimemsEditField.Value*0.001+0.1],"Widths",0.02); %!!! adjust location of box plots base on data?
                        set(Bx2,'LineWidth',2)
                        xticks(ax5,'auto');
                        xticklabels(ax5,'auto');
                        yline(ax5,median(OnsetLimitsV(~isnan(OnsetLimitsV))),'r:','LineWidth',2);
                        yline(ax5,median(OffsetLimitsV(~isnan(OffsetLimitsV))),'b--','LineWidth',2);

                        %boxplot for mean pre-stim data
                        g1=repmat({'meanPrestimData'},length(meanPreStimDataV),1);
                        g=g1;

                        Bx3=boxplot(ax5,meanPreStimDataV,g,"Orientation","vertical","Positions",median([app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value])*0.001,"Widths",((app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value-app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value)/2)*0.001); %!!! adjust location of box plots base on data?
                        set(Bx3,'LineWidth',2)
                        xticks(ax5,'auto');
                        xticklabels(ax5,'auto');
                        xlim('auto');
                        yline(ax5,median(meanPreStimDataV(~isnan(meanPreStimDataV))),'k--','LineWidth',2);
                        ylabel(ax5,app.UIAxes.YLabel.String);
                        title(ax5,'Onset and Offset Threshold Boxplots')
                    end
                    %Set legend
                    warning('off','MATLAB:legend:IgnoringExtraEntries')
                    legend(app.UIAxes,[app.PreStimArea app.StartLine app.OnsetLimitLines app.OffsetLimitLines app.meanPreStimLine],{'Pre Stim Area','Start','Onset Limit','Offset Limit','Pre Stim Mean'},'AutoUpdate','off');
                    warning('on','MATLAB:legend:IgnoringExtraEntries')

                    hold(app.UIAxes,'off');
                    
                    %update missing from analyze label
                    app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Visible=1;
                    app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Text=['Missing from analyze: ' num2str(MissingAnalyze) '/' num2str(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)))];

                    loadingScreen(app,0);
                    return;
                end %end if there are no onset/offsets found for all trials

                %All Onsets and Offsets
                AllOffset=app.AllOnOffsetTime(~isnan(app.AllOnOffsetTime(:,2)),2);
                AllOnset=app.AllOnOffsetTime(~isnan(app.AllOnOffsetTime(:,1)),1);

                %Plot start line and onset/offset thresholds
                app.StartLine=xline(app.UIAxes,app.StartTimemsEditField.Value*0.001,'k','LineStyle','-','LineWidth',2); %,'DisplayName', 'Start Time');
                app.OnsetLine=xline(app.UIAxes,median(AllOnset),'r','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %,'DisplayName', 'Onset');
                app.OffsetLine=xline(app.UIAxes,median(AllOffset),'b','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %,'DisplayName', 'Offset');
                %Adjust negation of thresholds based on if the data is rectified or not %!!!
                MedianOnsetThresh=median(OnsetLimitsV(~isnan(OnsetLimitsV)));
                MedianOffsetThresh=median(OffsetLimitsV(~isnan(OffsetLimitsV)));
                plotThresholdLinesMainGraph(app,AnalyzeMethod,MedianOnsetThresh,MedianOffsetThresh);
                meanPreStimDataV=meanPreStimData*UnitsV; %convert to units
                app.meanPreStimLine=yline(app.UIAxes,median(meanPreStimDataV(~isnan(meanPreStimDataV))),'k--');

                %Set legend
                warning('off','MATLAB:legend:IgnoringExtraEntries')
                legend(app.UIAxes,[app.PreStimArea app.StartLine app.OnsetLine app.OffsetLine app.OnsetLimitLines app.OffsetLimitLines app.meanPreStimLine],{'Pre Stim Area','Start','Onset','Offset','Onset Limit','Offset Limit','Pre Stim Mean'},'AutoUpdate','off');
                warning('on','MATLAB:legend:IgnoringExtraEntries')

                %Popup figure - Box plot of onsets and offsets 
                if string(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitch.Value) == "On" %&& (string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value) == "Garvey et al" ||string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value) == "SD Bounds")

                    %Box Plot Data =========================================
                    g1_OnsetOffset=repmat({'Onset'},length(AllOnset),1);
                    g2_OnsetOffset=repmat({'Offset'},length(AllOffset),1);
                    g_OnsetOffset=[g1_OnsetOffset;g2_OnsetOffset];

                    %Box plot for thresholds-----------------------------------------------------------------------
                    g1_Limits=repmat({'OnsetLimits'},length(OnsetLimitsV),1);
                    g2_Limits=repmat({'OffsetLimits'},length(OffsetLimitsV),1);
                    g_Limits=[g1_Limits;g2_Limits];

                    %Boxplot for mean pre-stim data----------------------------------------------------------------------
                    meanPreStimDataV=meanPreStimData*UnitsV; %convert to units
                    g1_PreStim=repmat({'meanPrestimData'},length(meanPreStimDataV),1);
                    g_PreStim=g1_PreStim;

                    %Plotting=============================================
                    if string(class(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig)) ~= "matlab.ui.Figure" 
                        app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig=figure();
                    else
                        if ~isgraphics(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig,'Figure')
                            app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig=figure();
                        else
                            clf(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig);
                        end
                    end
                    ax=subplot(1,2,1,'Parent',app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig); %=======================================Pop up subplot 1
                    for i=1:length(app.UIAxes.Children)
                        a=string(class(app.UIAxes.Children(i)));
                        b1=strsplit(a,'.');
                        ChildrenClass=b1(end);

                        if ChildrenClass == "Line"
                            CurrentXData(i,:)=app.UIAxes.Children(i).XData;
                            CurrentYData(i,:)=abs(app.UIAxes.Children(i).YData);

                        end
                    end
                    plot(ax,CurrentXData',CurrentYData'); hold on;

                    %Onset and Offset
                    BP=boxplot(ax,[AllOnset;AllOffset],g_OnsetOffset,"Orientation","horizontal","Positions",[-1*Units 1*Units],"Widths",0.5*Units); 
                    set(BP,'LineWidth',2)
                    xline(ax,median(AllOnset),'r','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %'DisplayName', 'Median Onset Time');
                    xline(ax,median(AllOffset),'b','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %'DisplayName', 'Median Offset Time');
                    yticks('auto')
                    yticklabels('auto')
                    xlabel(ax,'Time (s)');
                    ylabel(ax,app.UIAxes.YLabel.String);
                    xlim(ax,[min([AllOnset;AllOffset])-0.005 max([AllOnset;AllOffset])+0.005]); %zoom into onset/offset ranges +/-5ms
                    title(ax,'Times Boxplots');
                    %Plot start line and onset/offset thresholds
                    xline(ax,app.StartTimemsEditField.Value*0.001,'k','LineStyle','-','LineWidth',2); %,'DisplayName', 'Start Time');
                    %if a single trial is analyzed, add threshold lines
                    if length(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value) == 1 
                        yline(ax,OnsetLimitsV,'r:','LineWidth',2);
                        yline(ax,OffsetLimitsV,'b--','LineWidth',2);
                    end

                    ax2=subplot(1,2,2,'Parent',app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig); %======================================Pop up subplot 2
                    plot(ax2,CurrentXData',CurrentYData'); hold on;
                    %Onset and Offset Limits
                    Bx2=boxplot(ax2,[OnsetLimitsV';OffsetLimitsV'],g_Limits,"Orientation","vertical","Positions",[median(AllOnset) median(AllOffset)],"Widths",0.02); 
                    set(Bx2,'LineWidth',2)
                    xticks(ax2,'auto');
                    xticklabels(ax2,'auto');
                    if ~any(isnan(OnsetLimitsV)) || ~any(isnan(OffsetLimitsV)) %if there are any nans in the OnsetLimits or OffsetLimits auto scale this plot
                        ylim(ax2,[min([OnsetLimitsV';OffsetLimitsV'])-0.05*Units max([OnsetLimitsV';OffsetLimitsV'])+0.05*Units]); %+/-0.05 of unit
                    end
                    yline(ax2,median(OnsetLimitsV),'r:','LineWidth',2);
                    yline(ax2,median(OffsetLimitsV),'b--','LineWidth',2);
                    %Mean Pre Stim
                    Bx3=boxplot(ax2,meanPreStimDataV,g_PreStim,"Orientation","vertical","Positions",median([app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value])*0.001,"Widths",((app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value-app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value))*0.001); 
                    set(Bx3,'LineWidth',2)
                    xticks(ax2,'auto');
                    xticklabels(ax2,'auto');
                    yline(ax2,median(meanPreStimDataV),'k--','LineWidth',2);
                    xlim(ax2,[app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value*0.001-0.005 max([AllOnset;AllOffset])+0.005]); %zoom into onset/offset ranges +/-5ms
                    xlabel(ax2,'Time (s)');
                    ylabel(ax2,app.UIAxes.YLabel.String);
                    title(ax2,'Threshold Boxplots');
                    sgtitle('Onset and Offset')
                    hold(ax,'off');
                    hold(ax2,'off');
                end %end pop-up figure plotting

            end %end if override is not used

            %Update "missing from analyze" label
            if MissingAnalyze > 0
                app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Visible=1;
                app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Text=['Missing from analyze: ' num2str(MissingAnalyze) '/' num2str(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)))];
            else
                app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Visible=0;
                app.MissingfromanalyzeLabel.Text=['Missing from analyze: ' num2str(MissingAnalyze) '/' num2str(length(SelectedTrialsData(:,1)))];
            end

            hold(app.UIAxes,'off');
            if (string(class(event)) == "matlab.ui.eventdata.ButtonPushedData") && (event ~= 2)
            loadingScreen(app,0);
            end
            %AnalyzeTime=toc %time to analyze



        end

        % Button pushed function: OverrideButton
        function OverrideButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Allow user to choose limits for data analysis via point and
            %click
            %If override button is pushed again, disables override mode
            %==============================================================

            % for i=1:length(app.Toolbar.Children) %12/17/25 this visually deselects all the items in the toolbar, but does not run the callback needed to actually deselect them
            %     app.Toolbar.Children(i).Value=0; %deselect any toolbar items selected
            %     %matlab.graphics.controls.internal.interactionsModeCallback(app.Toolbar.Children(i).Tag,
            %     if i==1
            %     app.PrevToolBarSelect="";
            %     app.CurrToolBarSelect="";
            %     %axToolbarSelected(app,event);
            %     end
            % end

            zoom(app.UIAxes, 'off')

            %Check if override has been clicked
            %Update booleans and reset variables
            if app.OverrideUsed == 0
                app.UIFigure.Pointer = 'crosshair';
                app.OverrideUsed=1;
                app.Analyze_OverrideUsed=1;
                app.OverrideRange=[];
                app.OverrideLamp.Color=[1 0 0];
            elseif app.OverrideUsed == 1 %Override button is pressed again, cancel override
                app.UIFigure.Pointer = 'arrow';
                app.OverrideUsed=0;
                app.Analyze_OverrideUsed=0;
                app.OverrideRange=[];
                app.OverrideLamp.Color=[0.8 0.8 0.8];
            end

            loadingScreen(app,1);
            %Delete any existing xlines and areas
            if any(ishghandle(app.OnsetLine) == 1)
                delete(app.OnsetLine);
            end
            if any(ishghandle(app.OffsetLine) == 1)
                delete(app.OffsetLine);
            end
            if any(ishghandle(app.PreStimArea) == 1)
                delete(app.PreStimArea);
            end
            if any(ishghandle(app.StartLine) == 1)
                delete(app.StartLine);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.OnsetLimitLines) == 1
                delete(app.OnsetLimitLines);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.OffsetLimitLines) == 1
                delete(app.OffsetLimitLines);
            end
            if ishghandle(app.meanPreStimLine) == 1
                delete(app.meanPreStimLine);
            end
            loadingScreen(app,0);


        end

        % Button pushed function: RightArrowAnalyzeButton
        function RightArrowAnalyzeButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use to scroll down individual trials and analyze them
            %==============================================================
            
            %Determine currently selected trial
            CurrTrial=string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value{1});
            AllTrials=string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items);
            CurrTrialLoc=find(CurrTrial == AllTrials);

            %Next trial
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value=app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items{CurrTrialLoc+1};
            ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app,2);
            AnalyzeButtonPushed(app,1);

        end

        % Button pushed function: LeftArrowAnalyzeButton
        function LeftArrowAnalyzeButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use to scroll up individual trials and analyze them
            %==============================================================
            
            %Determine currently selected trial
            CurrTrial=string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value{1});
            AllTrials=string(app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items);
            CurrTrialLoc=find(CurrTrial == AllTrials);

            %Next trial
            app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value=app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Items{CurrTrialLoc-1};
            ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app,2);
            AnalyzeButtonPushed(app,1);
        end

        % Window button up function: UIFigure
        function UIFigureWindowButtonUp(app, event)
