            %==============================================================
            %Allow user to choose limits for data analysis via point and
            %click
            %Updates onset/offset times selected and plots selected times
            %via xlines
            %==============================================================

            %If override is active and a click occurs on the graph
            if app.OverrideUsed==1
                currPt = app.UIAxes.CurrentPoint; %determine click location
                app.OverrideRange=[app.OverrideRange currPt(1,1)]; %update override range

                hold(app.UIAxes,'on');
                %if this is the first click, plot onset line
                if length(app.OverrideRange) == 1 %onset selected
                    app.OnsetLine=xline(app.UIAxes,app.OverrideRange(1),'r','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %,'DisplayName', 'Override Onset');

                    %if this is the second click, plot offset line
                    %reset mouse pointer, start analyze, deactivate override
                elseif length(app.OverrideRange) == 2 %offset selected
                    app.AllOnOffsetTime=[app.OverrideRange(1) app.OverrideRange(2)];
                    app.OffsetLine=xline(app.UIAxes,app.OverrideRange(2),'b','LineStyle','--',"LineWidth",2); %,'DisplayName', 'Override Onset');
                    pause(0.01);
                    app.UIFigure.Pointer = 'arrow';

                    AnalyzeButtonPushed(app,1);

                    app.OverrideUsed=0;
                    app.OverrideLamp.Color=[0.8 0.8 0.8];

                end

            end
        end

        % Button pushed function: SavecsvButton
        function SavecsvButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Save analyze results to csv file
            %==============================================================

            %Change button color
            app.SavecsvButton.BackgroundColor=[0.8 0.8 0.8];
            pause(1);
            %tic

            %check if filename edit field is empty, if so prompt for filename
            if isempty(app.SavecsvEditField.Value)
                app.SavecsvButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];
                msgbox('Please Enter Filename');
                return;
            end

            %File name
            csvFileName=app.SavecsvEditField.Value;

            %check for .csv extension
            if length(csvFileName) >= 4 && all(csvFileName(end-3:end) == '.csv')
                csvFileName(end-3:end)='';
            end

            %Check for AddOn in file name
            AO_FileEndLoc=find(csvFileName == '_');
            if ~isempty(AO_FileEndLoc)
                Ending=csvFileName(AO_FileEndLoc(end):end);
            end
            %Selected analysis option
            AO_Opt=app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.Value;
            %if there is no underscore in the name OR the lengths of the endings don't match OR the Values of the endings don't match
            if (isempty(AO_FileEndLoc)) || (length(AO_Opt) ~= length(Ending)) || (any(AO_Opt ~= Ending))
                csvFileName=[csvFileName AO_Opt];
                app.SavecsvEditField.Value=csvFileName;
            end

            %check if "Results" folder exists if not, create it
            ResultsFolderDir=fullfile(app.Directory.SaveFolder, 'Results');
            if exist(ResultsFolderDir,'dir') ~= 7 %7 folder exists
                mkdir(ResultsFolderDir); 

            end

            ResultFile=fullfile(ResultsFolderDir, [csvFileName '.csv']);

            %create csv file
            NewFile=0;
            %MEP Analysis
            if exist(ResultFile,'file') ~= 2 && string(AO_Opt) == "_MEP" %if file doesn't exist, create it
                Headers{1}='Filename';
                Headers{2}='Trial Number';
                Headers{3}='Condition Channel #';
                Headers{4}='Condition';
                Headers{5}='Process Order';
                Headers{6}='Analyze Option';
                Headers{7}='Override';
                Headers{8}='Onset/Offset Detection Method';
                Headers{9}='Start Time (ms)';
                Headers{10}='unit';
                Headers{11}='Sample Rate';
                Headers{12}='Pre-stim Start Time (ms)';
                Headers{13}='Pre-stim Duration (ms)';
                Headers{14}='Rectified Pre-stim Mean';
                Headers{15}='Pre-stim RMS';
                Headers{16}='Pre-stim STDV'; 
                Headers{17}='Analyze Average Boolean';
                Headers{18}='MEP Onset (ms)';
                Headers{19}='MEP Duration (ms)';
                Headers{20}='MEP Amplitude';
                Headers{21}='MEP Area';
                Headers{22}='Date';

                %check onset/offset detection method
                if app.Analyze_OverrideUsed ~= 1
                    OnOffsetDetectMethod=string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value);
                    switch OnOffsetDetectMethod

                        case "SD Bounds"
                            Headers{23}='OnsetSD';
                            Headers{24}='OffsetSD';
                            Headers{25}='OnsetMinDur (ms)';
                            Headers{26}='OffsetMinDur (ms)';

                        case "Garvey et al"
                            Headers{23}='MCD Constant';
                            Headers{24}='OnsetMinDur (ms)';
                            Headers{25}='OffsetMinDur (ms)';

                        otherwise %custom method
                            PluginsFolderName=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
                            Settings=load(strcat(string(PluginsFolderName),"\",OnOffsetDetectMethod,"_Settings.mat"),"UserVar"); %load in custom settings
                            for i=1:length(Settings.UserVar(:,1))    %use the editfieldnames for the headers
                                Headers{22+i}=Settings.UserVar{i,1};
                            end
                    end
                end

                ResultTable=table(Headers);

                writetable(ResultTable,ResultFile,'WriteMode','Append','WriteVariableNames',0);
                NewFile=1;

            end

            %SP Analysis
            if exist(ResultFile,'file') ~= 2 && string(AO_Opt) == "_SP" %if file doesn't exist, create it
                Headers{1}='Filename';
                Headers{2}='Trial Number';
                Headers{3}='Condition Channel #';
                Headers{4}='Condition';
                Headers{5}='Process Order';
                Headers{6}='Analyze Option';
                Headers{7}='Override';
                Headers{8}='Onset/Offset Detection Method';
                Headers{9}='Start Time (ms)';
                Headers{10}='unit';
                Headers{11}='Sample Rate';
                Headers{12}='Pre-stim Start Time (ms)';
                Headers{13}='Pre-stim Duration (ms)';
                Headers{14}='Rectified Pre-stim Mean';
                Headers{15}='Pre-stim RMS';
                Headers{16}='Pre-stim STDV';
                Headers{17}='Analyze Average Boolean';
                Headers{18}='SP Onset (ms)';
                Headers{19}='SP Duration (ms)';
                Headers{20}='SP Percent Decrease';
                Headers{21}='SP Area';
                Headers{22}='Date';

                %check onset/offset detection method
                if app.Analyze_OverrideUsed ~= 1
                    OnOffsetDetectMethod=string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value);
                    switch OnOffsetDetectMethod

                        case "SD Bounds"
                            Headers{23}='OnsetSD';
                            Headers{24}='OffsetSD';
                            Headers{25}='OnsetMinDur (ms)';
                            Headers{26}='OffsetMinDur (ms)';

                        case "Garvey et al"
                            Headers{23}='MCD Constant';
                            Headers{24}='OnsetMinDur (ms)';
                            Headers{25}='OffsetMinDur (ms)';

                        otherwise %custom method
                            PluginsFolderName=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
                            Settings=load(strcat(string(PluginsFolderName),"\",OnOffsetDetectMethod,"_Settings.mat"),"UserVar"); %load in custom settings
                            for i=1:length(Settings.UserVar(:,1))    %use the editfieldnames for the headers
                                Headers{22+i}=Settings.UserVar{i,1};
                            end


                    end
                end

                ResultTable=table(Headers);

                writetable(ResultTable,ResultFile,'WriteMode','Append','WriteVariableNames',0);
                NewFile=1;
            end

            if exist(ResultFile,'file') == 2 && NewFile == 0 %the file does exist and it wasn't just created, check the header line    
                if app.Analyze_OverrideUsed ~= 1
                    ResultFile_FID=fopen(ResultFile,'r');
                    FileData=fread(ResultFile_FID); %double dec
                    fclose(ResultFile_FID);
                    
                    CarriageReturn=find(FileData == 13); %13 dec == carriage return
                    CarriageReturn=CarriageReturn(1);
                    HeaderLine=char(FileData(1:CarriageReturn-1))';
                    Headers=string(strsplit(HeaderLine,','));
                    %column 23 and after are details related to the onset/offset detection method
                    OnOffsetDetectMethod=string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value);
                    switch OnOffsetDetectMethod

                        case "SD Bounds"
                            HeaderLineText=["OnsetSD","OffsetSD","OnsetMinDur (ms)","OffsetMinDur (ms)"];

                        case "Garvey et al"
                            HeaderLineText=["MCD Constant","OnsetMinDur (ms)","OffsetMinDur (ms)"];
 
                        otherwise %custom method
                            PluginsFolderName=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
                            Settings=load(strcat(string(PluginsFolderName),"\",OnOffsetDetectMethod,"_Settings.mat"),"UserVar"); %load in custom settings
                            HeaderLineText=strings(1,length(Settings.UserVar(:,1)));
                            for i=1:length(Settings.UserVar(:,1))    %use the editfieldnames for the headers
                                HeaderLineText(i)=string(Settings.UserVar{i,1});
                            end
                            

                    end
                    %check if the headers for the custom process exist in the csv file, looks for all the headers together
                    StartHeaderLineSearch=find(Headers == HeaderLineText(1)); 
                    TitlesExist=[];
                    for i=1:length(StartHeaderLineSearch)
                        E=StartHeaderLineSearch(i)+length(HeaderLineText)-1;
                        if E <= length(Headers)
                        TitlesExist(i)=all(Headers(StartHeaderLineSearch(i):E) == HeaderLineText);
                        end
                    end
                    if isempty(TitlesExist) || all(TitlesExist == 0) %if the headers together don't exist
                        HeaderLineTextC=char(join(HeaderLineText,','))'; %make the header text
                        NewFileData=[FileData(1:CarriageReturn-1);double(','); double(HeaderLineTextC); FileData(CarriageReturn:end)]; %insert new headers into file
                        ResultFile_FID=fopen(ResultFile,'w+');
                        fwrite(ResultFile_FID,NewFileData);
                        fclose(ResultFile_FID);
                    end

                    
                end
            end


            %Save Analysis Results
            numColumns=28;
            switch AO_Opt
                case '_MEP'
                    DataOut=cell(length(app.Analyze_TrialsUsed),numColumns);
                    DataOut(:,20)=num2cell((app.MEP_Amp.*1000)');    %convert to mV
                    DataOut(:,21)=num2cell((app.MEP_Area.*1000)');   %convert to mV
                case '_SP'
                    DataOut=cell(length(app.Analyze_TrialsUsed),numColumns);
                    DataOut(:,20)=num2cell(app.SP_PercDecrease');        %this is a percentage
                    DataOut(:,21)=num2cell((app.SP_Area)');              %normalized
            end

            %General Info
            %create filename list
            FilenameList=string(app.Filename);
            Analyze_Filenames=cell(1,length(FilenameList)*2-1);
            Analyze_Filenames(1:2:end)=cellstr(FilenameList);
            Analyze_Filenames(2:2:end)={','};
            Analyze_Filenames=char(Analyze_Filenames);
            Analyze_Filenames=reshape(Analyze_Filenames',1,[]);
            Analyze_Filenames(Analyze_Filenames==' ')=[];
            DataOut(:,1)={Analyze_Filenames};
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 0
                DataOut(:,2)=num2cell(app.Analyze_TrialsUsed);
            else
                DataOut(:,2)=cellstr(app.Analyze_TrialsUsed);
            end
            DataOut(:,3)=app.Analyze_TrialsUsedChannel;    %10/8/25 error !!!
            DataOut(:,4)=app.Analyze_TrialsUsedConditionName;
            %format process order into comma delimited list
            if ~isempty(app.ProcessOrder)
                ProcessOrderRS=reshape(app.ProcessOrder',1,[]);
                ProcessOrderRS(cellfun(@isempty,ProcessOrderRS))=[]; %remove empty cells
                ProcessOrderString=cellfun(@string, ProcessOrderRS, 'UniformOutput', false);

                ProcessOrderUsed='';
                for i=1:length(ProcessOrderString)
                    CellLength=length(ProcessOrderString{i});
                    if CellLength > 1
                        for i2=1:CellLength
                            ProcessOrderUsed=[ProcessOrderUsed char(ProcessOrderString{i}(i2)) ','];
                        end
                    else
                        ProcessOrderUsed=[ProcessOrderUsed char(ProcessOrderString{i}) ','];
                    end

                end
                ProcessOrderUsed(end)='';

                if any(string(app.ProcessOrder(:,1)) == "Down Sample")
                    AnalyzeSampleRate=app.DownSampledRate;
                else
                    AnalyzeSampleRate=app.AllSampleRate;
                end
            else
                ProcessOrderUsed=[];
                AnalyzeSampleRate=app.AllSampleRate;
            end
            DataOut(:,5)={ProcessOrderUsed};
            DataOut(:,6)={app.AnalysisOptionDropDown.Value(2:end)};
            DataOut(:,9)={app.StartTimemsEditField.Value};
            DataOut(:,10)={'mV'}; %all calculations are saved in millivolts

            DataOut(:,11)={AnalyzeSampleRate}; %sample rate assumed to be the same for all condition files !!!
            DataOut(:,22)={datetime('now')};
            DataOut(:,17)={app.AverageCheckBox.Value};
            %PreStim Info
            DataOut(:,12)={app.PreStimStart}; %unit ms
            DataOut(:,13)={app.PreStimEnd-app.PreStimStart}; %unit ms
            DataOut(:,14)=num2cell(app.PreStimMean');
            DataOut(:,15)=num2cell(app.PreStimRMS');           
            DataOut(:,16)=num2cell(app.PreStimSTDV');

            DataOut(:,17)={0};

            %Onset/Offset Info
            DataOut(:,7)={app.Analyze_OverrideUsed};
            DataOut(:,8)={app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value}; %onset/offset detection method

            %check onset/offset detection method
            if app.Analyze_OverrideUsed ~= 1
                if NewFile == 0
                    %check which column the data should go to 
                    ResultFile_FID=fopen(ResultFile,'r');
                    FileData=fread(ResultFile_FID); %double dec
                    fclose(ResultFile_FID);
                    CarriageReturn=find(FileData == 13); %13 dec == carriage return
                    CarriageReturn=CarriageReturn(1);
                    HeaderLine=char(FileData(1:CarriageReturn-1))';
                    Headers=string(strsplit(HeaderLine,','));
                end
                OnOffsetDetectMethod=string(app.OnsetOffsetDetectionDropDown.Value);
                switch OnOffsetDetectMethod

                    case "SD Bounds"
                        HeaderLineText=["OnsetSD","OffsetSD","OnsetMinDur (ms)","OffsetMinDur (ms)"];
                        %find(Headers == HeaderLineText(1))
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(1))={app.OnsetSDEditField.Value};
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(2))={app.OffsetSDEditField.Value};
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(3))={app.OnsetMinDurmsEditField.Value};
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(4))={app.OffsetMinDurmsEditField.Value};

                    case "Garvey et al"
                        HeaderLineText=["MCD Constant","OnsetMinDur (ms)","OffsetMinDur (ms)"];
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(1))={app.MCDConstantEditField.Value};
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(2))={app.OnsetMinDurmsEditField.Value};
                        DataOut(:,Headers == HeaderLineText(3))={app.OffsetMinDurmsEditField.Value};

                    otherwise %custom method
                        PluginsFolderName=fullfile(userpath, app.ToolboxDocFolderName, 'Plugins','Analysis');
                        Settings=load(strcat(string(PluginsFolderName),"\",OnOffsetDetectMethod,"_Settings.mat"),"UserVar"); %load in custom settings
                        for i=1:length(Settings.UserVar(:,1))    %use the editfieldnames for the headers
                            %HeaderLineText(i)=string(Settings.UserVar{i,1});
                            DataOut(:,Headers == string(Settings.UserVar{i,1}))={Settings.UserVar{i,2}};
                        end

                end
            end
            app.Analyze_OverrideUsed=0; %reset after saving

            DataOut(:,18)=num2cell(app.AllOnOffsetTime(:,1)*1000); %unit ms
            DataOut(:,19)=num2cell((app.AllOnOffsetTime(:,2)-app.AllOnOffsetTime(:,1))*1000); %unit ms


            %Write data to file
            ResultTable=table(DataOut);
            if fopen(ResultFile,'a') == -1
                msgbox('Cannot open file. Please try again.');
                app.SavecsvButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];
                fclose('all');
                return;
            else
                fclose('all');
            end
            writetable(ResultTable,ResultFile,'WriteMode','Append','WriteVariableNames',0);

            %SaveCSVTime=toc %time to save CSV file
            app.SavecsvButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];



        end

        % Callback function: ProcessTree
        function ProcessTreeCheckedNodesChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update which edit field options are available base on which
            %processes are selected
            %Also update Process Order list box
            %==============================================================

            %check which nodes are selected and which nodes were previously selected
            CheckedNodes=event.CheckedNodes;
            PrevCheckedNodes=event.PreviousCheckedNodes;

            %Selected processes
            SelectedProcesses=zeros(1,9);
            SelectedProcNames=cell(1,length(CheckedNodes));
            for i=1:length(CheckedNodes)
                NodeData=CheckedNodes(i).NodeData;
                SelectedProcesses(NodeData)=NodeData;
                SelectedProcNames{i}=CheckedNodes(i).Text;
            end

            %Previously selected processes
            PrevSelectedProcesses=zeros(1,9);
            for i=1:length(PrevCheckedNodes)
                PrevNodeData=PrevCheckedNodes(i).NodeData;
                PrevSelectedProcesses(PrevNodeData)=PrevNodeData;
            end

            %Check for which processes was added/removed
            if sum(SelectedProcesses~=0) > sum(PrevSelectedProcesses~=0)
                %one was added
                if ~all(PrevSelectedProcesses==0)
                    Diff=PrevSelectedProcesses~=SelectedProcesses;
                    New=SelectedProcesses(Diff);
                else
                    New=SelectedProcesses(SelectedProcesses ~=0);
                end

                %Update visible edit fields
                switch New
                    %Rectify 8
                    %Custom 9
                    case 1 %Down sample
                        app.FactorEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.FactorLabel.Visible=1;

                    case 3 %Band pass
                        app.BandPassStartEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassDashLabel.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassEndEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassHzLabel.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassFilterType.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassFilterOrder.Visible=1;
                        app.BandPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=1; 

                    case 4 %Low pass
                        app.LowPassEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.LowPassHzLabel.Visible=1;
                        app.LowPassFilterType.Visible=1;
                        app.LowPassFilterOrder.Visible=1;
                        app.LowPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=1;

                    case 5 %High pass
                        app.HighPassEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.HighPassHzLabel.Visible=1;
                        app.HighPassFilterType.Visible=1;
                        app.HighPassFilterOrder.Visible=1;
                        app.HighPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=1;

                    case 2 %Notch
                        app.NotchUSButton.Visible=1;
                        app.NotchEuropeanButton.Visible=1;
                        app.OtherNotchButton.Visible=1;
                        app.OtherNotchEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.OtherNotchHzLabel.Visible=1;

                    case 7 %Delay
                        app.DelayEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.DelaymsLabel.Visible=1;

                    case 6 %Derivative
                        app.DerivativeEditField.Visible=1;
                        app.DerivativeOrderLabel.Visible=1;
                    case 8 %Rectify
                        app.FullWaveButton.Visible=1;
                        app.HalfWaveButton.Visible=1;


                end

            elseif sum(SelectedProcesses~=0) < sum(PrevSelectedProcesses~=0)
                %one was removed
                if ~all(SelectedProcesses==0)
                    Diff=PrevSelectedProcesses~=SelectedProcesses;
                    New=PrevSelectedProcesses(Diff);
                else
                    New=PrevSelectedProcesses(PrevSelectedProcesses ~=0);
                end

                %Update visible edit fields
                switch New
                    %Rectify 8
                    %Custom 9
                    case 1 %Down sample
                        app.FactorEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.FactorLabel.Visible=0;

                    case 3 %Band pass
                        app.BandPassStartEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassDashLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassEndEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassHzLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassFilterType.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassFilterOrder.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.BandPassFilterRp.Visible = 0;
                        app.BandPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = 0;
                        app.BandPassFilterRs.Visible = 0;
                        app.BandPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = 0;

                    case 4 %Low pass
                        app.LowPassEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.LowPassHzLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.LowPassFilterType.Visible=0;
                        app.LowPassFilterOrder.Visible=0;
                        app.LowPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.LowPassFilterRp.Visible = 0;
                        app.LowPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = 0;
                        app.LowPassFilterRs.Visible = 0;
                        app.LowPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = 0;

                    case 5 %High pass
                        app.HighPassEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.HighPassHzLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.HighPassFilterType.Visible=0;
                        app.HighPassFilterOrder.Visible=0;
                        app.HighPassFilterOrderLabel.Visible=0;
                        app.HighPassFilterRp.Visible = 0;
                        app.HighPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = 0;
                        app.HighPassFilterRs.Visible = 0;
                        app.HighPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = 0;

                    case 2 %Notch
                        app.NotchUSButton.Visible=0;
                        app.NotchEuropeanButton.Visible=0;
                        app.OtherNotchButton.Visible=0;
                        app.OtherNotchEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.OtherNotchHzLabel.Visible=0;

                    case 7 %Delay
                        app.DelayEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.DelaymsLabel.Visible=0;

                    case 6 %Derivative
                        app.DerivativeEditField.Visible=0;
                        app.DerivativeOrderLabel.Visible=0;
                    case 8 %Rectify
                        app.FullWaveButton.Visible=0;
                        app.HalfWaveButton.Visible=0;

                end

            end %end check for which processes was added/removed


            %Update Process Order List
            app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items=SelectedProcNames;
            app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData=SelectedProcesses(SelectedProcesses ~=0);

            %Add custom processes
            CustomSelected=app.CustomProcessTree.CheckedNodes;
            for i=1:length(CustomSelected)
                app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(end+1)={CustomSelected(i).Text};
                app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(end+1)=8+CustomSelected(i).NodeData; %8 is the last item data value for the built-in processes
            end

            
            
        end

        % Callback function: CustomProcessTree
        function CustomProcessTreeCheckedNodesChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Add custom processes to Process Order List Box
            %==============================================================
 
            %Run Process Tree Nodes changed
            eventS.CheckedNodes=app.ProcessTree.CheckedNodes;
            eventS.PreviousCheckedNodes=app.ProcessTree.CheckedNodes;
            ProcessTreeCheckedNodesChanged(app,eventS );
            
        end

        % Value changed function: NotchUSButton
        function NotchUSButtonValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use US powerline noise 60Hz in Notch filter, 
            %checked in ProcessingData.m
            %Deselect other Notch filter option buttons
            %==============================================================
            
            %Deactive other options
            if app.NotchUSButton.Value
                app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value = false;
                app.OtherNotchButton.Value = false;
            end
            %if this button is deselected, check if another button is selected, if not reselect this button
            if ~app.NotchUSButton.Value && (app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value == 0 && app.OtherNotchButton.Value == 0)
                app.NotchUSButton.Value =1;
            end

            
        end

        % Value changed function: NotchEuropeanButton
        function NotchEuropeanButtonValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use European powerline noise 50Hz in Notch filter, 
            %checked in ProcessingData.m
            %Deselect other Notch filter option buttons
            %==============================================================
            if app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value
                app.NotchUSButton.Value = false;
                app.OtherNotchButton.Value = false;
            end
            %if this button is deselected, check if another button is selected, if not reselect this button
            if ~app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value && (app.NotchUSButton.Value == 0 && app.OtherNotchButton.Value == 0)
                app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value =1;
            end
            
        end

        % Value changed function: OtherNotchButton
        function OtherNotchButtonValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use user input for Notch filter,
            %checked in ProcessingData.m
            %Deselect other Notch filter option buttons
            %==============================================================
            if app.OtherNotchButton.Value
                app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value = false;
                app.NotchUSButton.Value = false;
            end
            %if this button is deselected, check if another button is selected, if not reselect this button
            if ~app.OtherNotchButton.Value && (app.NotchUSButton.Value == 0 && app.NotchEuropeanButton.Value == 0)
                app.OtherNotchButton.Value =1;
            end
            
            
        end

        % Value changed function: BandPassFilterType
        function BandPassFilterTypeValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Depending on the selected filter type, display related options
            %==============================================================
            if strcmp(app.BandPassFilterType.Value, "Butterworth")
                app.BandPassFilterRp.Visible = false;
                app.BandPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = false;
                app.BandPassFilterRs.Visible = false;
                app.BandPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = false;
            elseif strcmp(app.BandPassFilterType.Value, "Chebyshev I")
                app.BandPassFilterRp.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRs.Visible = false;
                app.BandPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = false;                
            elseif strcmp(app.BandPassFilterType.Value, "Chebyshev II")
                app.BandPassFilterRp.Visible = false;
                app.BandPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = false;                
                app.BandPassFilterRs.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = true;
            elseif strcmp(app.BandPassFilterType.Value, "Elliptic")
                app.BandPassFilterRp.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRpLabel.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRs.Visible = true;
                app.BandPassFilterRsLabel.Visible = true;
            end               
            
        end

        % Value changed function: FullWaveButton
        function FullWaveButtonValueChanged(app, event)

            %Use full wave rectification,
            %checked in ProcessingData.m
            %Deselect other Rectify option buttons
            %==============================================================
            if app.FullWaveButton.Value
                app.HalfWaveButton.Value = false;
            end

            if ~app.FullWaveButton.Value && app.HalfWaveButton.Value == 0
                app.FullWaveButton.Value =1;
            end
        end

        % Value changed function: HalfWaveButton
        function HalfWaveButtonValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Use half wave rectification,
            %checked in ProcessingData.m
            %Deselect other Rectify option buttons
            %==============================================================
            if app.HalfWaveButton.Value
                app.FullWaveButton.Value = false;
            end
            %if this button is deselected, check if another button is selected, if not reselect this button
            if ~app.HalfWaveButton.Value && app.FullWaveButton.Value == 0
                app.HalfWaveButton.Value =1;
            end
        end

        % Button pushed function: MoveUpButton
        function MoveUpButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Move selected process in list Process Order list box up in
            %the order
            %==============================================================

            %check that a process is selected
            if ~isempty(app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value)

                %Determine location in list box
                SelectedLocation=find(app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value == app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData);
                SelectedValue=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value; %selected value

                if SelectedLocation ~= 1
                    SelectedItem=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation); %selected item name

                    %switch current selection with selection above
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation)=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation-1);
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation-1)=SelectedItem;
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation)=app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation-1);
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation-1)=SelectedValue;
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value=app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation-1);

                end
            end
        end

        % Button pushed function: MoveDownButton
        function MoveDownButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Move selected process in list Process Order list box down in
            %the order
            %==============================================================

            %check that a process is selected
            if ~isempty(app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value)
                %Determine location in list box
                SelectedLocation=find(app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value == app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData);
                SelectedValue=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value; %selected value

                if SelectedLocation ~= length(app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items)
                    SelectedItem=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation); %selected item name

                    %switch current selection with selection below
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation)=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation+1);
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items(SelectedLocation+1)=SelectedItem;
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation)=app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation+1);
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation+1)=SelectedValue;
                    app.ProcessOrderListBox.Value=app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData(SelectedLocation+1);

                end
            end
        end

        % Button pushed function: ResetOrderButton
        function ResetOrderButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Resets order of processes to default order
            %==============================================================

            CustomProcessTreeCheckedNodesChanged(app, app.CustomProcessTree);
            % %check which nodes are checked
            % CheckedNodes=app.ProcessTree.CheckedNodes;
            % 
            % %Reorder processes based on order in tree   
            % SelectedProcesses=zeros(1,9);
            % SelectedProcNames=cell(1,length(CheckedNodes));
            % for i=1:length(CheckedNodes)
            %     NodeData=CheckedNodes(i).NodeData;
            %     SelectedProcesses(NodeData)=NodeData;
            %     SelectedProcNames{i}=CheckedNodes(i).Text;
            % end
            % app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items=SelectedProcNames;
            % app.ProcessOrderListBox.ItemsData=SelectedProcesses(SelectedProcesses ~=0);
            
        end

        % Button pushed function: ProcessButton
        function ProcessButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Runs selected processes
            %==============================================================

            %Processing applied
            app.Processed=1;
            %Enable Reset data button
            app.ResetDataButton.Enable=1;
            %Disable Process button while processing
            app.ProcessButton.Enable=0;
            %Allow for saving the processed data
            app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.Enable=1; app.SavematEditField.Enable=1;

            app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll=cell(1,4);

            %check if being applied to average of data
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 1
                Data=app.POriginalAvgPlottedData;
                DataTime=app.Time;  
                app.AverageCheckBox.Enable=0;

            else %trials data
                %Data and time
                Data=app.Conditions_DataAll{1,1};
                DataTime=app.Conditions_DataAll{1,2}; %time in seconds

            end

            %Process Data
            [DataTime,Data]=ProcessingData(app,Data,DataTime);

            %Save order of processes
            app.ProcessOrder(:,1)=app.ProcessOrderListBox.Items';

            %update app time and allplotted data/avgPlotted data
            app.Time=DataTime;
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 1 %Process average of data
                app.AvgConditionData=Data;

                %Plot
                ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1); %plot current selected condition

            else
                app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{1,1}=Data;
                app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{1,2}=DataTime;

                %Plot
                SelectAllCondButtonPushed(app,1); %Select all
                ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app,1); %plot current selected condition

            end

            %Enable Process button after processing
            app.ProcessButton.Enable=1;
        end

        % Button pushed function: ResetDataButton
        function ResetDataButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Resets data and plot to before processing
            %==============================================================

            app.Processed=0;
            app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll=cell(1,4);
            app.Time=app.Conditions_DataAll{1,2}; %reset time
            app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.Enable=0; app.SavematEditField.Enable=0;
            %Reset data
            if app.AverageCheckBox.Value == 0
                app.AllPlotted_Data=app.POriginalAllPlottedData;
                ConditionTrialsListBoxValueChanged(app,1);
            else
                app.AvgConditionData=app.POriginalAvgPlottedData;
                ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1);
                app.AverageCheckBox.Enable=1;
            end

            app.ProcessOrder=[]; %clear process order
            app.DownSampledRate=[]; %clear downsampled rate
            app.SampleRateLabel.Text=['Sample Rate: ' num2str(app.AllSampleRate) 'Hz'];

        end

        % Button pushed function: SaveProcessedDatamatButton
        function SaveProcessedDatamatButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Save data that is currently in the main graph to a .mat file
            %The purpose of this button is to save processed data, so it
            %can be used outside of the app
            %==============================================================


            app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.BackgroundColor=[0.8,0.8,0.8];
            pause(1);

            %filename
            if isempty(app.SavematEditField.Value)
                app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];
                msgbox('Please Enter Filename');
                return;
            end

            SelectedTrials=cellfun(@str2double, app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Value');

            %Create variable
            AllPlottedData(:,1)=app.AllPlotted_Data(~cellfun(@isempty, app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1)),1); %remove empty cells
            AllPlottedData{1,2}=app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2};
            AllPlottedData(:,3)=mat2cell(SelectedTrials,ones(1,length(SelectedTrials)));

            %Add condition names
            for i=1:length(AllPlottedData(:,1)) %check for each trial
                STrial=AllPlottedData{i,3};
                for i2=1:length(app.ConditionsListBox.Items) %which condition it belongs to 
                    if STrial >= app.numTrialsCondition(i2,2) && STrial <= app.numTrialsCondition(i2,3) 
                        AllPlottedData{i,4}=app.ConditionsListBox.Items{i2}; %add condition name to variable
                    end
                end
            end
            
            AllPlottedData{end+1,1}=0;
            AllPlottedData(2:end,:)=AllPlottedData(1:end-1,:);
            AllPlottedData{1,1}='Data'; AllPlottedData{1,2}='Time'; AllPlottedData{1,3}='Trial'; AllPlottedData{1,4}='Condition';
            ProcessedOrder=app.ProcessOrder;

            %check if "Results" folder exists if not, create it
            ResultsFolderDir=fullfile(app.Directory.SaveFolder, 'Results');
            if exist(ResultsFolderDir,'dir') ~= 7 %7 folder exists
                mkdir(ResultsFolderDir);
            end

            %Processed Data folder
            ProcessedDataFolder=fullfile(ResultsFolderDir,'Processed Data');
            if exist(ProcessedDataFolder,'dir') ~= 7 %7 folder exists
                mkdir(ProcessedDataFolder);
            end


            %check for .mat extension
            matFileName=app.SavematEditField.Value;
            if length(matFileName) >= 4 && all(matFileName(end-3:end) == '.mat')
                matFileName(end-3:end)='';
            end
            save(fullfile(ProcessedDataFolder,[matFileName '.mat']),'AllPlottedData','ProcessedOrder');
            pause(1);
            app.SaveProcessedDatamatButton.BackgroundColor=[0.39,0.83,0.07];

        end

        % Value changed function: AverageCheckBox
        function AverageCheckBoxValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Plots average of currently plotted data
            %==============================================================

            %Determine if the plot needs to be averaged or separated
            switch app.AverageCheckBox.Value
                case 1 %take average

                    %check if working with raw data
                    if string(app.TabGroup.SelectedTab.Title) == "Data Organization" %raw data
                        SelectedTrialsData=cell2mat(app.AllPlotted_Data(:,1));
                        app.Plotted_Data=mean(SelectedTrialsData,1);
                        
                        %AllPlotted_Data updated 
                        app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,2);
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{1,1}=app.Plotted_Data;
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;

                        app.Plotted_Data=app.Plotted_Data*app.Convert2PlottedUnits;
                        %Plot desired data
                        cla(app.UIAxes);
                        plot(app.UIAxes, app.Time, app.Plotted_Data);
                        app.UIAxes.XLabel.String = 'Time (s)';
                        app.UIAxes.YLabel.String = app.PlottedUnits;
                        app.UIAxes.Title.String = app.CurrChTitle;
                        %update Scaling edit fields
                        app.MinEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(1);
                        app.MaxEditField.Value=app.UIAxes.XLim(2);
                        app.MinEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(1);
                        app.MaxEditField_2.Value=app.UIAxes.YLim(2);

                    else %work with condition data
                        %check if data was processed
                        if app.Processed == 1
                            Data=app.Processed_Conditions_DataAll{1,1};
                            app.ResetDataButton.Enable=0;
                        else
                            Data=app.Conditions_DataAll{1,1};
                        end

                        %Take average of each condition
                        Loc=app.numTrialsCondition; %columns: # trials, first trial, last trial
                        app.AvgConditionData=[];
                        for i=1:length(Loc(:,1)) %for each condition take the average
                            ConditionData=Data(Loc(i,2):Loc(i,3),:);
                            app.AvgConditionData(i,:)=mean(ConditionData);
                        end

                        %AllPlotted_Data updated 
                        app.AllPlotted_Data=cell(1,2);
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{1,1}=app.AvgConditionData; 
                        app.AllPlotted_Data{1,2}=app.Time;

                        app.POriginalAvgPlottedData=app.AvgConditionData; %for processing reset

                        %Disable individual trials for conditions and select all trials button
                        app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Enable=0;
                        app.SelectAllCondButton.Enable=0;

                        ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1);
                    end

                case 0 %plot all selected plots

                    if string(app.TabGroup.SelectedTab.Title) == "Data Organization" %raw data
                        TrialSelectListBoxValueChanged(app,1);

                    else %condition data

                        %Enable individual trials for conditions and select all trials button
                        app.ConditionTrialsListBox.Enable=1;
                        app.SelectAllCondButton.Enable=1;
                        app.ResetDataButton.Enable=1;

                        ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1);
                        app.POriginalAvgPlottedData=[]; %for processing reset


                    end




            end


        end

        % Selection change function: TabGroup
        function TabGroupSelectionChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %When the selected tab changes, change what data is plotted
            %==============================================================

            CurrSelectedTab=string(app.TabGroup.SelectedTab.Title);
            if app.EpochTypesButtonGroup.Enable == 1
    
                switch CurrSelectedTab
    
                    case "Data Organization" %if the newly selected tab is the "Data Organization" tab, run Find Events to load in raw data
                        FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
                        app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                        app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
    
                    case {"Process", "Analysis"} %if the newly selected tab is the "Process" or "Analyze" tab
                        if app.PrevSelectedTab == "Data Organization" %if the previously selected tab was the "Data Organization" tab
                            %Check if there is condition data
                            if (all(size(app.Conditions_DataAll) == [1 2])) && all(cellfun(@isempty,app.Conditions_DataAll))
                                %do nothing
                            else %switch to condition data
                                ConditionsListBoxValueChanged(app,1); 
                            end 
                        end
                        if CurrSelectedTab == "Analysis"
                            app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                            app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                        else
                            app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                            app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                        end
    
                end

                app.PrevSelectedTab = CurrSelectedTab;

            else
                if CurrSelectedTab == "Analysis"
                    app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                    app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=1;
                else
                    app.LeftArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                    app.RightArrowAnalyzeButton.Enable=0;
                end
            end

        end

        % Button pushed function: RefreshButton
        function RefreshButtonPushed(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Resets app
            %==============================================================

            app.refreshApp;
        end

        % Value changed function: AutoFillEditField
        function AutoFillEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Sets how many trials to add on automatically using the
            %Auto fill button
            %==============================================================
            AddOnValue = app.AutoFillEditField.Value;
            %Value should be greater than zero
            if AddOnValue <=0
                app.AutoFillEditField.Value=0;
            end
        end

        % Value changed function: MinEditField
        function MinEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set minimum value for x limit on graph
            %==============================================================
            NewXLim_Min = app.MinEditField.Value;
            app.UIAxes.XLim=[NewXLim_Min app.MaxEditField.Value];

        end

        % Value changed function: MaxEditField
        function MaxEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set maximum value for x limit on graph
            %==============================================================
            NewXLim_Max = app.MaxEditField.Value;
            app.UIAxes.XLim=[app.MinEditField.Value NewXLim_Max];

        end

        % Value changed function: MinEditField_2
        function MinEditField_2ValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set minimum value for y limit on graph
            %==============================================================
            NewYLim_Min = app.MinEditField_2.Value;
            app.UIAxes.YLim=[NewYLim_Min app.MaxEditField_2.Value];

        end

            %==============================================================
            %Set maximum value for y limit on graph
            %==============================================================
            NewYLim_Max = app.MaxEditField_2.Value;
            app.UIAxes.YLim=[app.MinEditField_2.Value NewYLim_Max];

        end

        % Value changed function: PreDurationsEditField
        function PreDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update the time before the event (comment or event) to plot
            %If value is invalid, reset to previous value
            %Update plot and data
            %==============================================================

            app.PreDur = app.PreDurationsEditField.Value;       %Time before comment/event to include in seconds
            %if value is less than or equal to zero, auto fill with previous value
            if app.PreDur<= 0
                app.PreDurationsEditField.Value=event.PreviousValue;
                app.PreDur=app.PreDurationsEditField.Value;                     %add check for if value is too large !!!
            end
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1; %Disable loading indicator for Find Events
            FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;


        end

        % Value changed function: PostDurationsEditField
        function PostDurationsEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update the time after the event (comment or event) to plot
            %If value is invalid, reset to previous value
            %Update plot and data
            %==============================================================
            app.PostDur = app.PostDurationsEditField.Value;     %Time after comment/event to include in seconds
            %if value is less than or equal to zero, auto fill with previous value
            if app.PostDur<= 0
                app.PostDurationsEditField.Value=event.PreviousValue;
                app.PostDur=app.PostDurationsEditField.Value;                     %add check for if value is too large !!!
            end
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1; %Disable loading indicator for Find Events
            FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;

        end

        % Value changed function: PreTriggermsEditField
        function PreTriggermsEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update the time to shift the data "back" by !!!need better wording for this
            %Update plot and data
            %==============================================================
            app.PreTriggerTime = app.PreTriggermsEditField.Value;               %add check for if value is too large !!!
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1; %Disable loading indicator for Find Events
            FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;

        end

        % Value changed function: PostTriggermsEditField
        function PostTriggermsEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Update the time after zero to plot
            %Update plot and data
            %==============================================================
            app.PostTriggerTime = app.PostTriggermsEditField.Value;             %add check for if value is too large !!!
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=1; %Disable loading indicator for Find Events
            FindEventsButtonPushed(app,1);
            app.DisableLI_FindEvents=0;

        end

        % Value changed function: DetectionSettingsDropDown
        function DetectionSettingsDropDownValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Type of detection for finding events
            %==============================================================

            app.DetectionSetting = app.DetectionSettingsDropDown.Value;

        end

        % Value changed function: EventChannelEditField
        function EventChannelEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set event channel for finding events
            %If value entered is invalid, set value to previous value
            %==============================================================

            app.EventChannel = app.EventChannelEditField.Value;
            %Check if value entered is less than zero or larger than the number of channels
            if app.EventChannel > app.numChannels
                app.EventChannelEditField.Value=event.PreviousValue;
                app.EventChannel = app.EventChannelEditField.Value;
            elseif app.EventChannel < 0
                app.EventChannelEditField.Value=event.PreviousValue;
                app.EventChannel = app.EventChannelEditField.Value;
            end


        end

        % Value changed function: ThresholdmVEditField
        function ThresholdmVEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set event threshold for finding events
            %==============================================================
            app.EventThresh = app.ThresholdmVEditField.Value;

        end

        % Value changed function: SearchISIsEditField
        function SearchISIsEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set search ISI for finding events
            %If value entered is invalid, set value to previous value
            %==============================================================

            app.SearchISI = app.SearchISIsEditField.Value;
            if app.SearchISI < 0
                app.SearchISIsEditField.Value=event.PreviousValue;
                app.SearchISI = app.SearchISIsEditField.Value;
            end

        end

        % Value changed function: PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField
        function PreStimPeriodmsStartEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set start time for pre-stim period
            %==============================================================
            app.PreStimStart= app.PreStimPeriodmsStartEditField.Value;

        end

        % Value changed function: PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField
        function PreStimPeriodmsEndEditFieldValueChanged(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Set end time for pre-stim period
            %==============================================================
            app.PreStimEnd= app.PreStimPeriodmsEndEditField.Value;

        end

        % Close request function: UIFigure
        function UIFigureCloseRequest(app, event)
            %==============================================================
            %Close Matlab App
            %Unload any libraries, close any other apps and figures
            %==============================================================

            %Confirm close
            ConfirmClose=questdlg('If not already done, save the condition file. Do you want to close the app?','Confirm Close','Close','Cancel','Cancel');
            if string(ConfirmClose) == "Cancel"
                return;
            end

            %unload CEDS64ML library if loaded
            if libisloaded('ceds64int')
                unloadlibrary ceds64int;
            end

            %close figures
            try
                delete(app.BashFig); %close bash app 5/19/26: not implemented
            end
            try
                close(app.PlotOnsetOffsetSwitchFig) %close Plot Onset/Offset figure
            end
            try
                close(app.CustomAnalysisOpts) %close Custom Analyze pop-up
            end
            %Close custom processing pop-ups
            if string(class(app.CustomProcessingOpts)) == "struct"
                Processes=fieldnames(app.CustomProcessingOpts);
                for i=1:length(Processes)
                    try
                        close(app.CustomProcessingOpts.(Processes{i}));
                    end
                end
            end

            delete(app);

        end

        % Button pushed function: ClearLoadingScreenButton
        function ClearLoadingScreenButtonPushed(app, event)
            %This is for bug testing purposes and will eventually be removed
            loadingScreen(app,0); %Enable GUI Screen
            app.DefineConditionsPanel.Enable=1; %Enable Conditions Panel
        end
    end

    % App creation and deletion
    methods (Access = public)

        % Construct app
        function app = TMSAnalysisToolBox_v2_1_0

            % Create UIFigure and components
            createComponents(app)

            % Register the app with App Designer
            registerApp(app, app.UIFigure)

            % Execute the startup function
            runStartupFcn(app, @startupFcn)

            if nargout == 0
                clear app
            end
        end

        % Code that executes before app deletion
        function delete(app)

            % Delete UIFigure when app is deleted
            delete(app.UIFigure)
        end
    end
end
